### [1] [Self-Organized Learning in Oscillatory Neural Networks with Memristive Signed Couplings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00286)
*Riley Acker,Aman Desai,Garrett Kenyon,Frank Barrows*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于忆阻边带抑制性耦合的振荡神经网络（ONN）神经形态原语，通过电路仿真验证其在自联想任务中的去噪能力，并理论证明符号化有效权重（负权重）对于反相吸引子自主持久存在的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值Hopfield/Ising模型通常假设符号化权重，但ONN的神经形态实现因器件和电路限制难以实现负权重。为扩大振荡器网络可访问的吸引子结构类型（超越纯同相耦合），并支持反相约束在训练后自主持久存在的相位编码记忆，亟需一种实用的抑制性（负）权重实现方案。

Method: 采用忆阻边（memristive edges）实现抑制性耦合，构建ONN神经形态原语；通过电路仿真验证系统在自联想任务中对噪声输入的去噪能力；结合理论分析证明符号化有效权重对反相吸引子自主持久存在的必要性。

Result: 电路仿真表明该忆阻抑制性耦合ONN系统能在自联想任务中有效去噪；理论分析确认符号化有效权重（即包含负权重）是反相吸引子在释放后仍能自主持久存在的必要条件。

Conclusion: 抑制性（负）权重的实用化实现路径对ONN架构至关重要，它不仅能扩展吸引子类型，还能支撑具备持久反相约束的相位编码记忆，使ONN更接近连续学习与推理的理想架构。

Abstract: Oscillatory neural networks (ONNs) have emerged as a promising neuromorphic architecture, leveraging coupled dynamical systems to perform computation and represent information through phase relationships. Their interactions can be designed to support intrinsic energy-minimizing dynamics, enabling tasks such as associative memory and optimization, and positioning them as a candidate architecture for continuous learning and inference. We present a neuromorphic primitive implemented using memristive edges with inhibitory couplings as a potential design for autonomous learning, and provide circuit simulation validation that the system is capable of denoising noisy inputs on an auto-associative task. While numerical Hopfield/Ising models routinely assume signed weights, neuromorphic implementations of ONNs often fail to realize negative weights due to device and circuit constraints. A practically implementable route to inhibitory (negative) weights is particularly valuable: it expands the class of attractor structures accessible to oscillator networks beyond purely synchronous couplings, and supports phase-coded memories where anti-phase constraints are not merely transiently enforced during training but can persist autonomously after release. We provide circuit simulations and theoretical analyses demonstrating that signed effective weights are necessary for anti-phase attractors to persist autonomously.

</details>


### [2] [From Consistency to Collaborative Discovery: MFEA-CoD for Multitask Novelty Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00761)
*Jiao Liu,Yanchi Li,Hua Yu,Abhishek Gupta,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出MFEA-CoD算法，将进化多任务优化从一致性迁移转向协同发现，通过多任务排斥算子和自适应跨任务迁移机制，有效提升多任务新颖性搜索的效率，并在欺骗性目标场景中展现优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有进化多任务（EMT）研究主要聚焦于目标驱动优化，利用任务间一致性加速收敛至预定义最优解，但缺乏对行为新颖性的协同探索机制。

Method: 提出MFEA-CoD算法，包含：(1) 多任务排斥算子，鼓励不同任务探索统一搜索空间的不同区域以减少冗余发现；(2) 自适应跨任务迁移机制，根据在线贡献调整迁移概率，利用新颖性提升区域的重叠共享机会；(3) 扩展至新颖性增强优化，联合考虑行为新颖性与目标信息以缓解欺骗性目标导致的早熟收敛。

Result: 在合成盆地型问题、欺骗性迷宫导航、MuJoCo策略优化和生成式新颖性搜索问题上，MFEA-CoD相比基线方法显著提升了多样化新颖解的发现效率，并在欺骗性目标景观中展现明显优势。

Conclusion: 将EMT从一致性迁移拓展到协同发现是有效的，MFEA-CoD通过任务间排斥与自适应迁移机制，在多任务新颖性搜索任务中比传统EMT方法更具优势。

Abstract: Evolutionary multitasking (EMT) has shown strong capability in solving multiple optimization problems simultaneously by exploiting latent inter-task consistency, such as similarities in promising solutions or search directions. However, most existing EMT studies remain focused on objective-driven optimization, where such consistency is mainly used to accelerate convergence toward predefined optima. In this paper, we move EMT from consistency to collaborative discovery and propose a multifactorial evolutionary algorithm with collaborative discovery (MFEA-CoD) for multitask novelty search. Unlike conventional EMT, MFEA-CoD coordinates multiple novelty search tasks to collaboratively discover behaviorally novel solutions rather than merely transferring consistent search information for faster convergence. Specifically, a multitask repulsion operator encourages different tasks to explore distinct regions of the unified search space, thereby reducing redundant behavioral discoveries. Meanwhile, an adaptive inter-task transfer mechanism exploits shared discovery opportunities in overlapping novelty-improving regions by adjusting the transfer probability according to the online contribution of transferred information. Furthermore, MFEA-CoD is extended to multitask novelty-augmented optimization, where behavioral novelty is jointly considered with objective information to alleviate premature convergence caused by deceptive objectives. Experiments on synthetic basin-type problems, deceptive maze navigation problems, MuJoCo policy optimization problems, and generative novelty search problems demonstrate that MFEA-CoD improves the efficiency of discovering diverse novel solutions and shows clear advantages in deceptive objective landscapes.

</details>


### [3] [MMAO-Dyn: A Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer for Dynamic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00846)
*Jinliang Xu,Liping Ma*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文提出MMAO-Dyn，在不替换代谢控制循环的前提下将代谢多智能体优化器扩展为动态优化方法，在216组动态基准测试中平均离线误差28.07，优于对比方法，但优势主要体现在恢复导向的资源再分配而非全局主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索代谢多智能体优化器（MMAO）的核心代谢控制循环是否能够自然地产生动态优化行为，而不需要引入外部自适应模块进行替代。

Method: 提出MMAO-Dyn，将私有能量、公共预算、角色漂移、成功反馈和生命周期更替等机制映射到非平稳动态优化设置中；在18种动态连续基准场景（移位Sphere、Ackley、Rastrigin，维度10D/20D/30D，两种变化严重程度，12个随机种子）上进行了216组实验，并与通用MMAO、动态随机搜索、动态PSO-lite、动态DE-lite及三种消融变体对比。

Result: MMAO-Dyn平均离线误差28.07，优于Generic-MMAO（29.36）、Dynamic-PSO-lite（34.65）、Dynamic-DE-lite（67.09）和Dynamic-RandomSearch（111.37）；在Sphere和Rastrigin族上的聚合鲁棒性以及10步后变化恢复方面优势明显，但与Dynamic-PSO-lite的胜率对比不利，且NoMemoryRefresh消融与完整方法非常接近。

Conclusion: MMAO-Dyn是MMAO家族扩展的可信结果：代谢循环能够产生有意义的动态行为，但其当前最强价值在于面向恢复的资源再分配，而非全局主导或完全优化的子机制设计。

Abstract: This paper studies whether the Metabolic Multi-Agent Optimizer (MMAO) can be credibly derived into a dynamic-optimization method without replacing its core metabolic control loop by external adaptation modules. The proposed MMAO-Dyn maps private energy, communal budget, role drift, success feedback, and lifecycle turnover to a nonstationary setting in which environmental changes repeatedly invalidate previously useful local structure. We evaluate MMAO-Dyn on an 18-scenario synthetic dynamic continuous benchmark matrix covering shifted sphere, shifted Ackley, and shifted Rastrigin landscapes at $10D$, $20D$, and $30D$, with two change severities and 12 seeds per scenario. The comparison layer includes a generic MMAO variant without dynamic derivation, dynamic random search, dynamic PSO-lite, dynamic DE-lite, and three endogenous ablations. Across the full 216-run matrix, MMAO-Dyn attains mean offline error $28.07$, improving over Generic-MMAO ($29.36$), Dynamic-PSO-lite ($34.65$), Dynamic-DE-lite ($67.09$), and Dynamic-RandomSearch ($111.37$). The gains are clearest in aggregate robustness on sphere and Rastrigin families and in 10-step post-change recovery relative to the generic backbone, whereas the seed-aligned comparison with Dynamic-PSO-lite remains unfavorable in win-loss count and the \texttt{NoMemoryRefresh} ablation stays very close to the full method. We therefore position MMAO-Dyn as a credible family-expansion result for MMAO: the metabolic loop can generate meaningful dynamic behavior, but the strongest current value lies in recovery-oriented resource redistribution rather than in universal dominance or in a fully optimized submechanism design.

</details>

### [4] [Certificate-Carrying Transformation of Event-Driven Block Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00563)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证书携带的源到源重写方法，用于保证块式端用户语言（如Scratch）的行为保持。该方法通过一个可信的、失败关闭的检查器重新计算每个重写的行为保持条件，而不是信任优化器。研究在Lean中形式化了一个协同帧精化定理，构建了六个重写族检查器，并在300个真实Scratch项目上验证，实现了94.3%的接受率、亚秒级认证成本和零假阳性接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具通过程序分析和测试建立行为保持，但缺乏经过检验的保证。端用户语言如Scratch运行数千万程序，需要一种可证明的行为保持优化方法，确保优化器错误不会导致不合理的接受。

Method: 将优化转化为证书携带的源到源重写：非可信优化器提出重写，可信的失败关闭检查器在显式观察透镜下重新计算每个行为保持依赖的侧条件。核心论点是协同帧精化定理：在无线程让步的窗口内，任何线程观察到之前已被覆盖的写操作可以被移除。该定理在Lean中形式化，并涵盖两个具体重写族（变量状态和渲染器状态）。

Result: 在300个真实Scratch项目上，检查器在94.3%的项目（283/300）上接受行为保持重写；认证成本低于每个项目0.1秒；跨族对抗性攻击4278个扰动重写产生零假阳性接受。审计发现8个假阳性接受（被测试套件遗漏），现已全部拒绝。剥离语义侧条件的消融实验显示，仅靠分析和测试的重写会被虚拟机确认改变行为，而完整检查器拒绝所有此类重写。

Conclusion: 为并发、事件驱动的端用户语言提供了行为保持保证。检查器重新计算每个所需条件而非信任优化器声明，保持了小的可信基。结果表明证书携带方法能够有效保证端用户语言程序优化的正确性。

Abstract: Block-based end-user languages such as Scratch run tens of millions of programs. Existing tools establish behavior preservation through program analysis and testing without a checked guarantee. We turn optimization into certificate-carrying source-to-source rewriting. An untrusted optimizer proposes a rewrite; a trusted, fail-closed checker accepts it only after recomputing every side condition that the rewrite's behavior preservation depends on under an explicit observation lens. The checker is the sole authority: given a correct checker and a small, explicitly stated set of model-to-VM assumptions, an optimizer bug cannot mint an unsound acceptance. The observation lens is a parameter, and the central soundness argument is a cooperative-frame refinement theorem: a write overwritten before any thread observes it, within a window in which no thread yields, can be removed. We mechanize this theorem in Lean and show that one parametric statement covers two concrete rewrite families instantiated to variable state and renderer state. We build a checker for six rewrite families and evaluate it on 300 real Scratch projects. The checker accepts a behavior-preserving rewrite on 94.3% of projects (283 of 300); certification costs under one tenth of a second per project; and a cross-family adversarial campaign of 4,278 perturbed rewrites produces zero false accepts. An audit found eight false accepts the per-family test suites missed; each is now rejected. An ablation that strips the semantic side conditions, leaving analysis and testing alone, ships rewrites the virtual machine confirms change behavior; the full checker rejects every one. The result shows how to provide behavior-preservation guarantees for a concurrent, event-driven, end-user language. The checker recomputes every required condition instead of trusting optimizer claims, keeping the trusted base small.

</details>


### [5] [Practical Range Refinement Types with Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00824)
*Valentin Aebi,Carlo A. Furia*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: Ranger是一个专注于整数区间类型的细化类型系统，通过双向类型推断和轻量级流敏感静态分析减少注解开销，在实验语言Licorne上实现，能简洁表达和验证超出Java/Scala标准类型系统的属性，并与Checker Framework和Liquid Java等系统相比具有优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有细化类型系统虽然增强了传统类型系统的表达能力，但其注解开销过大，限制了在实际主流语言中的采用。论文旨在降低这种注解负担，使细化类型更易于使用。

Method: 提出了Ranger细化类型系统，聚焦整数区间类型；采用双向类型系统运行类型推断算法，减少用户需要编写的辅助注解；集成了轻量级流敏感静态分析技术，无需显式注解即可精确捕获程序行为。

Result: 在Licorne实验性编程语言上实现了Ranger。实验表明Ranger能简洁表达和验证多种有用属性，这些属性超出了Java和Scala标准静态类型系统的能力；与Java Checker Framework和Liquid Java等其他扩展类型系统相比，Ranger在区间相关属性检查方面表现更好。

Conclusion: Ranger通过降低注解开销和与命令式结构无缝集成，使得细化类型系统更实用，在保证表达力的同时提高了可用性，为细化类型在实际编程语言中的推广提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Refinement types are a static verification technique that aims at increasing the expressivity of traditional type systems while remaining easy and natural to use. While systems based on refinement types have been developed for several mainstream languages, their practical adoption remains limited by their annotation overhead, which is often a more significant burden than when using the "plain" type annotations of languages like Java or Scala.
  To improve the state of the art, this paper introduces Ranger: a refinement type system designed to keep the annotation overhead small and to seamlessly integrate with imperative-style constructs like variables and loops. As the name suggests, Ranger focuses on integer range types: a particular kind of refinement types that express bounded integer ranges. Such types are widely useful to verify correct index manipulation and in-bounds data accesses, among others. To combine expressiveness and succinctness, Ranger is based on a bidirectional type system, which runs a type inference algorithm to provide the typechecking pass with information useful to reduce the need for user-written auxiliary annotations. Ranger also integrates other forms of lightweight flow-sensitive static analysis techniques that precisely capture the program's behavior without explicit annotations.
  We implemented Ranger on top of the Licorne experimental programming language. Our experiments show that Ranger's implementation can concisely express and verify a variety of useful properties that fall beyond the capabilities of standard static type systems like those of Java and Scala, and that Ranger compares favorably to other extended type systems, such as the Java Checker Framework and Liquid Java, that can also check properties about ranges.

</details>

### [6] [A Non-Line-of-Sight, Multi-Modality-based Side-Channel IP Theft Attack on Additive Manufacturing Using Dual Smartphones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00186)
*Amirhossein Jamarani,Diba Afroze,Yazhou Tu,Mark Yampolskiy,Xiali Hei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用两部智能手机内部传感器（麦克风和磁力计），在60厘米外非视距条件下，以98.89%的命令级重建精度成功重构3D打印机的G-code指令的侧信道攻击方法，验证了增材制造系统中知识产权泄露的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D打印机侧信道攻击面临三大挑战：G-code重建成功率和准确率低、攻击距离有限、依赖专业且显眼的采集工具。亟需一种更高效、隐蔽且远距离的攻击方法。

Method: 使用两部智能手机内置传感器（声学+磁力），在60厘米非视距条件下采集3D打印机的声学与磁辐射侧信道信号，通过信号处理与特征提取重构G-code命令。

Result: 命令级G-code重建准确率达98.89%；攻击策略可迁移至不同环境下的另一款3D打印机。

Conclusion: 该攻击成功证明了通过非专业设备可在较远距离下实现对3D打印机IP的未经授权访问，揭示了增材制造系统在侧信道安全方面的严重缺陷，亟需开发相应的防御措施。

Abstract: Additive Manufacturing (AM) has revolutionized major sectors, including aerospace, automotive, and healthcare, by enabling adjustable production. As the usage of AM increases, so does the risk of Intellectual Property (IP) leakage during the printing process due to unintended side-channel emissions. Current studies and attack scenarios on 3D printers face three challenges: low success and accuracy rates in final G-code reconstruction, limited distance range for attacking the 3D printer's IP, and reliance on specialized, overt data-collection tools. This paper presents a side-channel attack that addresses the noted limitations by using two smartphones' internal sensors. We position the smartphones 60 cm away in a non-line-of-sight setup to collect the 3D printer's acoustic and magnetic emissions. Our attack successfully reconstructs the G-code commands of the final objects at a rate of 98.89% on command-level reconstruction accuracy. Additionally, we evaluate the transferability of our attack strategy by applying it to another 3D printer in a different environment. Our proven unauthorized access to the reconstructed G-code and thus to the IP of the AM system indicates the security weaknesses in 3D printing, highlighting the need for mitigating side-channel attacks.

</details>


### [7] [Federated Sovereign Transport Protocol (FSTP): Verifiable Coordination Without Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00213)
*Ramón Soto C.,Liz Soto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出联邦主权传输协议（FSTP），通过Rust类型系统在编译时强制数据封闭、上下文身份去关联化以及基于Blocklace的事件日志，使数据隔离成为协议本身的属性，而非依赖运维策略，实现“可证明但不暴露”的联邦验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦协议仅定义消息格式和传递语义，将数据封闭交由运维策略处理，缺乏结构性约束，无法保证符合规范的服务器不会泄露内部数据。

Method: 1) 同步代理的输出类型集在形式上封闭，Rust类型系统在编译时强制原始内部数据不出现在联邦消息中；2) 上下文身份模型为每个联邦关系派生独立、不可关联的标识符；3) 基于Blocklace的事件基底提供防篡改、偏序日志，同步代价与节点状态对称差成正比，支持数据擦除而不破坏哈希链。

Result: 联邦参与者可在不访问产生这些人工制品的内部数据的情况下验证过程发生、凭证真实以及结果未被篡改，实现“可证明但不暴露”。

Conclusion: FSTP作为Velyzor治理平台的节点间传输层，以协议级约束解决了异构隐私需求联邦网络中的数据封闭问题，规范与参考实现已以Apache 2.0开源。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Federated Sovereign Transport Protocol (FSTP), a synchronization boundary and transport layer for federated networks in which nodes have heterogeneous privacy requirements. Existing federation protocols leave data confinement to operator policy: they define message formats and delivery semantics but impose no structural constraint on what a conforming server may emit. FSTP addresses this gap by making data confinement a property of the protocol itself.
  The central mechanism is a synchronization agent whose output type set is formally closed. Raw internal data cannot appear in any federation message because the constraint is enforced by the Rust type system at compile time, not by a runtime check. A contextual identity model derives a separate, unlinkable identifier for each federation relationship, preventing cross-context correlation structurally. A Blocklace-based event substrate provides tamper-evident, partially ordered logging with synchronization cost proportional to the symmetric difference between node states, and supports data erasure without breaking the hash chain.
  The result is proof without exposure: a federation participant can verify that a process occurred, that a credential is authentic, and that an outcome is uncorrupted without accessing the internal data that produced these artifacts. FSTP is developed as the inter-node transport layer of Velyzor, a governance platform for institutions with demanding confidentiality requirements. The specification and reference implementation are released as open-source infrastructure under Apache 2.0; source code and figures accompany this paper.

</details>


### [8] [(A)I Sees What You Don't: Exploiting New Attack Surfaces in Third-Party Mobile Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00333)
*Zidong Zhang,Zhentao Xie,Wenrui Diao,Jianliang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了第三方VLM驱动的移动代理应用存在两大独特攻击面（屏幕感知攻击面和信道滥用攻击面），通过七种具体攻击方法可在无需任何权限的情况下劫持代理行为并执行任意命令，对用户视觉上完全不可察觉，暴露了自主代理设计中的根本信任错配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着VLM驱动的第三方移动代理成为自动化智能手机交互的新范式，这些高权限代理通过截图感知设备状态并基于VLM推理执行操作，引入了新的攻击面或使原本无害的界面变得可被利用，亟需系统性地分析其安全态势。

Method: 总结了第三方移动代理应用与一般应用在环境交互上的关键差异，识别出两大独特攻击面：屏幕感知攻击面（利用人眼与机器视觉的差距）和信道滥用攻击面（拦截或操纵代理执行管道），并设计实现了七种具体攻击（包括潜台词文本注入、不可见像素区利用、截图篡改和主机PC命令注入等）。

Result: 在五个主流移动代理框架上的评估表明，恶意应用可以在无需任何权限的情况下劫持代理动作并实现任意命令执行，同时保持对用户视觉上的不可区分性。

Conclusion: 研究发现揭示了自主代理设计中的根本信任错配，强调了在多租户平台上亟需感知感知的安全模型（perception-aware security models）。

Abstract: Third-party mobile agents powered by Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for automating smartphone interactions. These agents act as high-privilege decision-makers, perceiving device states through screenshots and executing actions via VLM reasoning, transforming how an agent app interacts with the environment (i.e., other apps or the OS). Correspondingly, this transformation introduces new attack surfaces or transforms benign/harmless interfaces into exploitable ones for mobile devices. In this paper, we summarize key differences between third-party mobile agent apps and general apps when interacting with the environment, analyze the security posture of agents, and identify two unique attack surfaces compared to general mobile apps: the Screen Perception Attack Surface, which exploits the gap between human and machine vision, and the Misused Channel Attack Surface, which intercepts or manipulates the agent's execution pipeline. We design and implement seven concrete attacks, from subliminal text injection and invisible pixel zone exploitation to screenshot tampering and host PC command injection. Our evaluation of five popular mobile agent frameworks demonstrates that a malicious app can hijack agent actions and achieve arbitrary command execution even without any privilege permissions, while remaining visually indistinguishable to users. These findings reveal a fundamental trust mismatch in autonomous agent design and highlight the urgent need for perception-aware security models on multi-tenant platforms.

</details>


### [9] [ReShift: Aha-Moment-Driven Reasoning-Level Backdoor Attacks on Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00361)
*Zhihao Dou,Qinjian Zhao,Zhiqiang Gao,Sumon Biswas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ReShift框架，通过在推理层面（链式思维轨迹）植入后门，实现更隐蔽的对抗攻击，结合PRDC数据构造和SRJO联合优化策略，利用熵反弹信号表征推理重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要操纵最终输出，产生的推理痕迹不一致且易被检测，亟需更隐蔽的推理级后门攻击方法。

Method: 提出ReShift框架，包含：1) Poisoned Reasoning-Aware Data Construction (PRDC)流水线构造中毒推理数据；2) Supervised-Reinforcement Joint Optimization (SRJO)策略联合优化监督与强化学习；3) 形式化Entropy Rebound作为推理重定向的原理性信号，提供熵间隙与轨迹级散度的理论保证。

Result: 在保持干净任务性能的同时实现高攻击成功率，生成逼真的推理轨迹，显著提升对现有防御的隐蔽性。

Conclusion: ReShift通过显式重定向内部CoT轨迹而非仅操纵最终输出，实现了更隐蔽有效的后门攻击，为VLM安全性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Vision--Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, yet remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Existing methods primarily manipulate final outputs, often producing reasoning traces that are inconsistent or easily detectable. In this paper, we propose ReShift, the novel aha-moment-driven reasoning-level backdoor framework that explicitly redirects the internal chain-of-thought (CoT) trajectory while preserving surface-level coherence. ReShift introduces a Poisoned Reasoning-Aware Data Construction (PRDC) pipeline and a Supervised--Reinforcement Joint Optimization (SRJO) strategy to induce stable trigger-conditioned reasoning shifts. We further formalize Entropy Rebound as a principled signal for characterizing reasoning redirection and provide theoretical guaranties linking entropy gaps to trajectory-level divergence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReShift achieves high attack success rates while maintaining clean-task performance and realistic reasoning traces, substantially improving stealthiness against existing defenses.

</details>


### [10] [SoK: Attack and Defense Landscape of Mobile On-device AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00362)
*Yujin Huang,Xin Zheng,Xingliang Yuan,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统化了移动端侧AI（MoAI）系统的安全知识体系，覆盖安全支柱、攻击与防御全景，并指出了当前研究的空白与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoAI系统（本地部署AI模型+传统移动软件组件）虽能实现隐私保护、低延迟和离线AI功能，但因AI模型本地存储带来了新的安全风险，而此前缺乏系统化的安全研究框架。

Method: 通过对MoAI系统的安全支柱、攻击面与防御面进行全面的知识系统化（SoK），构建了首个系统性分析框架。

Result: 建立了MoAI系统攻击与防御全景的系统化框架，识别出当前攻击与防御研究的未解决空白，并指出了有前景的未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建安全的MoAI系统奠定了基础，并为该关键领域的研究提供了系统性框架支撑。配套资源已开源。

Abstract: Mobile on-device AI (MoAI) systems that integrate locally deployed AI models with conventional mobile software components are emerging as a key paradigm for delivering intelligent functionality directly on end-user devices. By moving inference from remote cloud services to the local mobile environment, such systems enable privacy-preserving, low-latency, and offline-capable AI functionality, yet introduce new security risks arising from the local storage of AI models. This paper presents the first comprehensive systematization of knowledge on MoAI security, covering security pillars, attack landscape, and defense landscape of MoAI systems. We further identify unresolved gaps in current attack and defense research and point to promising directions for future research in this emerging area. Our work establishes the first systematic framework for understanding the attack and defense landscapes of MoAI systems, serving as a foundation for building secure MoAI systems and advancing research in this critical domain. Companion resources are available at https://github.com/Jinxhy/Awesome-MoAI-Security.

</details>


### [11] [KidnapRAG: A Black-Box Attack for Hijacking Reasoning in Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00422)
*Chanwoo Choi,Euntae Kim,Kyuho Lee,Youngsam Chun,Jinhee Jeong,Eunmi Kim,Myunggyo Oh,Junseo Jang,Buru Chang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出KidnapRAG，一种针对Agentic RAG系统的黑盒投毒攻击方法，通过三阶段恶意文档（Bait, Chain-Link, Mal-Ins）劫持智能体的多步推理链，在无需白盒访问权限的条件下实现对模型输出的操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Agentic RAG系统的投毒攻击大多假设白盒访问权限（如系统提示、推理轨迹、检索器或模型参数），这在真实场景中难以实现。作者旨在探索更现实的黑盒设定下，仅通过发布可被外部检索的恶意文档来攻击Agentic RAG系统的可能性。

Method: 提出KidnapRAG，一种顺序投毒攻击方法，包含三种角色文档：(1) Bait——吸引初始检索；(2) Chain-Link——诱导查询重构；(3) Mal-Ins——提供攻击者控制的虚假证据。三者协同作用，逐步劫持智能体的多步推理链。

Result: 在多种Agentic RAG框架、LLM骨干网络和基准测试上的实验表明，KidnapRAG在黑盒条件下持续优于现有投毒攻击基线。进一步分析显示，该方法能逐步削弱原始检索意图、重定向检索行为、并增加对攻击者控制证据的依赖。

Conclusion: KidnapRAG在黑盒设定下成功攻击了Agentic RAG系统，揭示了此类系统在对抗性投毒攻击下的脆弱性，为未来构建更鲁棒的RAG系统提供了重要警示。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems are vulnerable to poisoning attacks that inject malicious documents into the retrieval process to manipulate model outputs. Recent Agentic RAG systems are more robust to such attacks because they iteratively perform retrieval and reasoning, allowing them to ignore weakly relevant poisoned documents and preserve the reasoning chain induced by the user query. However, existing attacks on Agentic RAG systems often assume white-box access to system prompts, reasoning traces, retrievers, or model parameters, limiting their applicability in realistic settings. In this paper, we study black-box poisoning attacks against Agentic RAG systems, where the attacker can only publish externally retrievable poisoned documents. We propose KidnapRAG, a sequential poisoning attack that hijacks the agent's multi-step reasoning chain using three role-specific documents: Bait, Chain-Link, and Mal-Ins, which attract initial retrieval, induce query reformulation, and provide attacker-controlled evidence, respectively. Experiments across multiple Agentic RAG frameworks, LLM backbones, and benchmarks show that KidnapRAG consistently outperforms existing poisoning baselines under black-box conditions. Further analyses show that KidnapRAG progressively weakens the original retrieval intent, redirects retrieval behavior, and increases reliance on attacker-controlled evidence. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/chanwoochoi316/KidnapRAG.

</details>


### [12] [Minos: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Provenance-Based Backward Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00440)
*Jiahui Wang,Zhenyuan Li,Zhengkai Wang,Xiangmin Shen,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Minos是一个多智能体框架，将基于溯源的后向追踪建模为大语言模型驱动的推理过程，采用双层架构（事件级分析+图探索），在五个公开数据集的14个攻击场景上实现了平均0.92召回率和0.64精确度，攻击子图压缩49%，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于溯源的后向追踪方法依赖低级统计特征和刚性遍历策略，难以捕获高级对抗意图且面临依赖爆炸问题，亟需能够理解攻击者意图并高效剪枝搜索空间的新型方法。

Method: 采用双层架构：(1)事件级分析——结合分层上下文管理、带引文验证的检索增强推理和对抗性思辨；(2)图探索——在有限状态机下协调四个专用智能体，用假设引导推理和先计数后查询协议替代穷举遍历。

Result: 在五个公开数据集14个攻击场景中，平均召回率0.92、精确率0.64，显著优于现有基线，攻击子图压缩49%，且生成可解释推理过程。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的推理在自动化溯源后向追踪中有效，既能提升检测精度又能压缩搜索空间，同时保持过程可解释性，有利于取证审计和系统改进。

Abstract: Sophisticated cyber attacks, particularly Advanced Persistent Threats (APTs), require effective post-intrusion forensic analysis. Provenance-based backward tracking reconstructs attack scenarios by tracing causality from security alerts, but existing methods rely on low-level statistical features and rigid traversal strategies, limiting their ability to capture high-level adversarial intent and suffering from dependency explosion. We present Minos, a multi-agent framework that formulates backward tracking as an LLM-driven reasoning process. Minos adopts a two-tiered architecture: for event-level analysis, it combines hierarchical context management, retrieval-augmented reasoning with citation verification, and adversarial deliberation to improve reasoning quality; for graph exploration, it coordinates four specialized agents under a finite state machine (FSM), replacing exhaustive traversal with hypothesis-guided reasoning and count-first query protocols to efficiently prune the search space. Experiments on 14 attack scenarios across five public datasets show that Minos achieves an average recall of 0.92 and precision of 0.64, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines while producing attack subgraphs that are 49% more compact. Moreover, Minos generates interpretable reasoning throughout the tracking process, facilitating forensic auditing and system refinement. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of LLM-driven reasoning for automated provenance-based backward tracking.

</details>


### [13] [Beyond the Prompt: Jailbreaking Function-Calling LLMs via Simulated Moderation Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00481)
*Junlong Liu,Haobo Wang,Weiqi Luo,Xiaojun Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SMT（模拟审核轨迹）攻击框架，利用状态化函数调用环境中结构化参数、工具输出与模型上下文混合的架构漏洞，通过多轮模拟审核工作流逐步削弱模型安全约束，成功绕过商业LLM的安全防线，证明仅靠提示级防护不足以防御工具增强型LLM系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提示级别的越狱攻击与防御，忽视了在具有状态和函数调用的LLM应用环境中，开发者定义的模式、结构化参数和不可信工具输出被混合到同一共享模型上下文中，导致信任边界模糊，攻击面扩大。

Method: 提出SMT（Simulated Moderation Traces）黑盒攻击框架，通过构建多轮交互轨迹模拟合法审核工作流：1）利用虚构的审核框架以红队测试为借口诱导有害输出；2）将安全拒绝视为执行失败，通过验证反馈逐步削弱模型安全约束，最终触发有害生成。

Result: 在5个不同提供商的主流商业LLM上，基于两个标准化安全基准的广泛实验表明，SMT在攻击成功率和HarmScore上持续达到最高水平，同时所需查询次数接近最少，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 仅靠提示级别的净化措施不足以防御工具增强型LLM系统，迫切需要跨模式、参数、工具输出和累积对话状态的上下文感知验证机制。

Abstract: Jailbreak attacks remain a critical threat to the safe deployment of large language models (LLMs). While prior work has primarily studied attacks and defenses at the prompt level, we show that this prompt-centric paradigm overlooks a structural vulnerability in stateful, function-calling environments. In such applications, developer-defined schemas, structured arguments, and untrusted tool outputs are interleaved into a single shared model context. This architecture expands the attack surface by blurring the boundary between trusted control logic and untrusted data, allowing adversarial intent to be distributed across a multi-turn execution path. We exploit this architectural flaw through SMT, a black-box attack framework based on Simulated Moderation Traces. Departing from purely prompt-based interactions, SMT constructs a multi-turn trajectory that simulates a legitimate moderation-auditing workflow. Within this trajectory, a fabricated moderation frame leverages red-team testing as a pretext to elicit harmful generations. The subsequent validation feedback treats safety refusals as execution failures, prompting refinements that gradually weaken the model's safety constraints and ultimately trigger harmful outputs. Extensive empirical evaluations on prominent commercial LLMs from five different providers across two standardized safety benchmarks show that SMT consistently achieves the highest average attack success rate and HarmScore while requiring a near-minimal number of queries, substantially outperforming existing baselines. These findings demonstrate that prompt-level sanitization alone is fundamentally insufficient for defending tool-enabled LLM systems and highlight the urgent need for context-aware validation across schemas, arguments, tool outputs, and accumulated conversation state. The code is available at https://github.com/liujlong27/SMT.

</details>


### [14] [Cross-Domain Generalization Failure in Lightweight Intrusion Detection Models for IIoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00553)
*MD Azizul Hakim,Md Shihab Uddin,Talha Ibne Anis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了轻量级机器学习模型在IIoT入侵检测中的跨网络泛化能力，发现模型严重依赖粗粒度端口类别特征（这是一种捷径），且评估协议和类别分布会影响结论。模型在未见网络上的表现与域内准确性差异显著，且对抗鲁棒性与跨网络泛化无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量级IIoT入侵检测模型仅在训练网络内评估，其在未见网络上的行为未经验证，部署可行性存疑。

Method: 在1个IIoT数据集上训练4种轻量级架构，使用跨数据集共享的特征表示，在2个结构不同的IIoT数据集上直接评估（不微调）；对最佳模型进行可解释性分析、类别不平衡影响分析、对抗鲁棒性及目标域微调恢复评估。

Result: 模型严重依赖粗粒度端口类别特征（捷径），该特征在源域攻击流量中出现频率是目标域的96-435倍；评估协议可逆转目标域泛化难度的排序；对抗鲁棒性与跨网络泛化无关；不同架构的微调恢复能力差异显著。

Conclusion: 应使用真实类别分布下的跨网络评估来评估IIoT入侵检测模型的部署就绪度，而非仅依赖域内准确率。

Abstract: Lightweight machine learning models are increasingly proposed for intrusion detection in Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) networks due to their suitability for resource-constrained edge deployment. Most reported results evaluate these models only within their training network, leaving behavior on unseen networks unverified. This study trains four lightweight architectures on one IIoT dataset and evaluates them, without retraining, on two structurally distinct IIoT datasets using a feature representation restricted to attributes available across all three sources. Explainability analysis across two top-performing models shows both rely overwhelmingly on coarse port-category features; the most influential category occurs in source-domain attack traffic at 96 to 435 times the rate in the two target domains, indicating that coarsening port resolution relocates rather than removes a documented shortcut. Evaluation under naturally imbalanced class distributions reveals a further effect: the evaluation protocol used can reverse which target network appears to pose the greater generalization challenge. Adversarial robustness and recovery through limited target-domain exposure are also assessed; robustness to adversarial perturbation is unrelated to cross-network generalization, and recovery through adaptation varies considerably by architecture. These findings suggest deployment readiness should be assessed using cross-network evaluation under realistic class distributions, rather than within-domain accuracy alone.

</details>


### [15] [The Rise and Fall of Google's Privacy Sandbox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00693)
*Rachid Youssef Grib,Alberto Verna,Nikhil Jha,Martino Trevisan,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次纵向测量了Google Privacy Sandbox在Web上的部署情况，发现其API采纳率极低且持续下降，仅CHIPS例外。2025年Google宣布终止该项目后，隐私保护型兴趣广告的挑战仍未解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Google于2025年10月宣布终止Privacy Sandbox，此前经历了近五年的实验。作者希望对其实际部署情况进行首次纵向、考虑用户同意的测量，以评估其采纳程度和影响。

Method: 使用自定义调用监听器，对前10,000个网站进行每周爬取，监测所有主要API在项目终止前几个月的使用情况。

Result: 大部分API仅被少数参与者使用，且活动持续下降；即使Google计划保留的API也未见增长；仅CHIPS为例外。

Conclusion: Privacy Sandbox的终结表明，实现隐私保护型兴趣广告的挑战仍未解决。

Abstract: On October 17th, 2025, Google announced the retirement of most Privacy Sandbox APIs, concluding nearly five years of experimentation with its alternative to privacy-invasive data collection on the Web. Designed to balance privacy with advertising functionality and cross-site tracking, the initiative faced repeated redesigns and limited ecosystem support.
  In this work, we present the first longitudinal, consent-aware measurement of the Privacy Sandbox's deployment across the Web. Using a custom call listener and weekly crawls of the top-10,000 websites, we monitor the usage of all major APIs in the months preceding their retirement. Adoption had already stagnated well before Google's announcement: most APIs were used by only a handful of actors, whose activity declined steadily throughout our study. Even the APIs that Google plans to maintain show no sign of growth. The sole exception is Cookies Having Independent Partitioned State (CHIPS). Overall, the demise of the Privacy Sandbox leaves unresolved the challenge of enabling privacy-preserving interest-based advertising.

</details>


### [16] [Know Thy Neighbor: Cross-TEE Mutual Attestation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00695)
*Daniel Andrade,João N. Silva,Miguel P. Correia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Hema协议，一个形式化验证的异构互认证协议，用于同一或不同TEE类型上可信应用实例之间的双向认证，解决了跨TEE认证需要双方支持对方软件栈的效率和安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨TEE类型的互认证方案需要每个TEE类型支持所有其他TEE类型的认证软件栈，效率低下且存在安全风险；需要一种专用且高效的跨TEE互认证解决方案。

Method: 提出Hema（Heterogeneous Mutual Attestation）协议，通过形式化验证的方法设计了一个支持同一TEE类型及不同TEE类型（如SGX、TrustZone）之间可信应用实例的双向认证协议。

Result: Hema协议在效率和安全性方面优于传统双向远程认证方案，且通过了形式化验证。

Conclusion: 专用跨TEE互认证方案相比传统双向远程认证具有显著优势，Hema协议为异构TEE环境下的可信通信提供了形式化验证的安全保障。

Abstract: Cloud services are composed of multiple heterogeneous distributed components and instances that communicate with one another. This occurs both in applications and services running in traditional execution environments and in trusted applications (TAs) running in trusted execution environments (TEEs). TA instances use attestation before exchanging information to ensure all parties meet the expected security conditions. The straightforward solution to mutually attesting two TA instances that are willing to communicate is employing remote attestation mechanisms in both directions. This is typically the case when the two TA instances are running on TEEs of the same type. In order to support cross-TEE attestation, such an approach, that is, using remote attestation in both directions, would require each TEE type (e.g., SGX, TrustZone) to support the attestation software stack of all other TEE types with which it needs to interact. A dedicated cross-TEE mutual attestation solution has multiple benefits in terms of efficiency and security. This paper presents the Heterogeneous Mutual Attestation (Hema) protocol, a formally-verified protocol for the mutual attestation of TA instances running on the same TEE type or on different TEE types.

</details>


### [17] [Forensic-Oriented Intrusion Detection Using Synthetic Network Traffic Data and Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00763)
*Jose Luis Vela Alonso,Carmen Pellicer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个面向数字取证的网络入侵检测框架，通过合成数据训练（SDV+CTGAN）与XGBoost分类器结合SHAP可解释性，在满足ISO/IEC 27037等取证标准的同时实现F1-macro=0.96的检测性能，解决了传统ML管线缺乏可追溯性、可复现性和法庭可辩护性的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习入侵检测管线将原始证据作为训练数据，产生不透明的分类结果且缺乏实例级解释，无法满足数字取证调查对可追溯、可复现、法庭可辩护的分析输出要求。

Method: 构建符合ISO/IEC 27037、27041、27042及NIST SP 800-86标准的取证导向框架：(1) 原始数据作为不可变、哈希验证的工件，通过SDV+CTGAN生成参数化合成衍生物进行训练；(2) XGBoost对表格化网络流数据进行二分类；(3) SHAP TreeExplainer提供实例级特征归因，将统计预测映射到可观测的网络行为。

Result: CICIDS2017上TSTR评估F1-macro=0.96（接近真实数据基线0.97）；KS检验平均|KS|=0.38证实合成数据隐私保护；跨数据集验证（UNSW-NB15、Kitsune）确定约30维数值流级特征是合成训练有效性的部署边界；SHAP归因在真实与合成实例上对暴力破解、端口扫描、DoS攻击表现一致。

Conclusion: 合成数据训练在保持取证相关攻击指纹的前提下可实现与真实数据训练相当的检测性能，为满足数字取证标准的可解释入侵检测提供了可行方案，但受限于特征空间维度（约30维数值特征以内有效）。

Abstract: Digital forensic investigations of network intrusions require analytical outputs that are traceable, reproducible, and court-defensible - requirements existing machine learning pipelines do not satisfy, since they treat original evidence as training data and produce opaque classifications without instance-level justification. This paper presents a forensic-oriented intrusion detection framework resolving both problems simultaneously, integrating synthetic data generation, binary classification, and explainability within a single pipeline governed by ISO/IEC 27037, 27041, 27042, and NIST SP 800-86.
  The framework operationalises the ISO/IEC 27037 requirement for strict separation between original digital evidence and derived analytical artefacts. Original datasets are treated as immutable, hash-verified artefacts; all training operates on parameterized synthetic derivatives via SDV + CTGAN. XGBoost binary classification provides high-performance detection on tabular network flow data, and SHAP TreeExplainer produces instance-level feature attributions mapping statistical predictions to observable network behaviour for forensic reporting.
  Train-on-Synthetic, Test-on-Real (TSTR) evaluation on CICIDS2017 achieves F1-macro = 0.96, within cross-validation variance of the real-data baseline (0.97). Kolmogorov-Smirnov testing confirms synthetic privacy preservation (mean |KS| = 0.38) alongside operational utility. Cross-dataset validation on UNSW-NB15 and Kitsune identifies feature space dimensionality as the primary determinant of synthetic training effectiveness, establishing a practical deployment boundary of approximately 30 numeric flow-level features. SHAP attributions for Brute Force, Port Scan, and DoS attacks are consistent across real and synthetic instances, confirming synthetic training preserves forensically relevant attack fingerprints required for expert witness testimony.

</details>


### [18] [No Country for Old Privacy: The Evolving Challenges of Anonymity in Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00772)
*Ben Hawkins,Joshua Levett,Siamak F. Shahandashti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 通过对比特币网络中五种第二代匿名协议（CoinJoin、CoinSwap、CoinShuffle、Stealth Addresses）的纵向测量研究，识别了数百万笔相关交易，发现这些协议采用率极低（不足1%），且监管事件后可见使用量急剧下降，未发现标准化Stealth Address采用，表明隐私用户已转向更隐蔽的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比特币网络中的隐私保护协议（如CoinJoin、CoinSwap等）虽然被提出，但其实际采用情况缺乏系统的实证研究。现有工作多聚焦于第一代匿名技术，对第二代协议的大规模、长期采用模式知之甚少，尤其是在监管环境变化下的演变趋势。

Method: 采用纵向测量研究方法，实施并优化了一套启发式过滤规则，对Bitcoin网络中的CoinJoin、CoinSwap、CoinShuffle和Stealth Addresses四种第二代匿名协议进行可检测实例的识别与追踪。

Result: 识别出超过594万笔CoinJoin交易和2330万笔CoinSwap交易；发现CoinShuffle的使用与Wasabi钱包运营时期高度吻合；所有协议采用率均低于网络交易的1%；关键监管事件后，可检测使用量出现急剧下降；未发现标准化Stealth Address的采用证据。

Conclusion: 第二代隐私协议在比特币网络中的采用率持续偏低且可检测性有限，未能形成统一的隐私标准，表明追求隐私的用户已向更透明性更低、更难以检测的方法迁移。

Abstract: We present a longitudinal measurement study on the adoption of detectable, second-generation anonymisation protocols in the Bitcoin network, including CoinJoin, CoinSwap, CoinShuffle and Stealth Addresses. By implementing and refining a suite of heuristic filters, we identify over 5.94 million CoinJoin and 23.3 million CoinSwap transactions. Besides, the use of CoinShuffle was unexpectedly found to be closely aligned with the Wasabi wallet operation period. Our analysis reveals consistently low adoption rates, with these protocols constituting less than 1% of network transactions, and a sharp decline in detectable usage following key regulatory events. Furthermore, we find no evidence of standardised Stealth Address adoption, indicating a failure to converge on a common privacy standard. This study provides a comprehensive picture of a niche ecosystem whose on-chain visibility has been largely suppressed, strongly suggesting the migration of privacy-seeking users to less transparent and less detectable methods.

</details>


### [19] [Toward a Unified Security and Privacy Framework for AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01019)
*Bidushi Barua,Ahsan Khan,Kangfeng Ye,Panagiotis Papanastasiou,Yifan Liu,Mohit Bidikar,Anthony Moulds,Julie McCann,Poonam Yadav*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从跨层视角系统调查了AI原生6G网络中的安全与隐私问题，提出了统一的安全与隐私框架、跨层威胁分类体系及相应的对抗措施，识别了关键研究空白和未来方向，为构建可信的AI原生6G生态系统提供基础性参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有6G安全与隐私方法在各类新兴技术、网络架构、AI系统和标准化工作之间高度碎片化，缺乏统一视角，无法应对AI原生6G网络高度异构的安全与隐私挑战。

Method: 采用跨层视角，首先梳理现有安全与隐私方法在多个维度的碎片化现状，提出统一的安全与隐私框架；在此基础上构建包含基础设施层、网络与架构层、AI层、隐私层及安全管理层的跨层威胁分类体系，分析代表性威胁并映射至对应的跨层对抗措施，包括将标准协调作为安全功能。

Result: 形成了一套完整的跨层威胁分类体系及对抗措施映射，识别了关键研究空白，提出了未来优先研究方向，涵盖安全、隐私保护、弹性和全球互操作性等方面。

Conclusion: 该调查为研究人员、从业者和标准化社区提供了设计、评估和部署可信AI原生6G系统的整体基础，推动实现安全、隐私保护、弹性且全球互操作的6G网络。

Abstract: Sixth Generation (6G) communication networks are expected to evolve into AI-native, highly autonomous ecosystems that integrate communication, computing, sensing, and artificial intelligence. While these capabilities enable unprecedented connectivity and intelligent services, they also create a highly heterogeneous security and privacy landscape that cannot be addressed through isolated, technology-specific solutions. This paper presents a comprehensive survey of security and privacy in AI-native 6G networks from a cross-layer perspective. We first examine the fragmentation of existing security and privacy approaches across emerging technologies, network architectures, AI systems, and standardization efforts, motivating the need for a unified security and privacy framework. Building upon this framework, we develop a cross-layer threat taxonomy encompassing infrastructure, network and architectural, AI, privacy, and security management domains, and analyze representative threats across key AI-native 6G technologies. Furthermore, we map these threats to corresponding cross-layer countermeasures, including standards harmonization as a security function, and identify critical research gaps and future priorities for secure, interoperable, and trustworthy AI-native 6G ecosystems. Finally, we discuss future research directions toward realizing secure, privacy-preserving, resilient, and globally interoperable 6G networks. This survey provides researchers, practitioners, and standardization communities with a holistic foundation for the design, evaluation, and deployment of trustworthy AI-native 6G systems.

</details>


### [20] [Antaeus: Hunting Repository-Level Logic Vulnerabilities via Context-Grounded LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01138)
*Michele Armillotta,Nicolò Romandini,Rebecca Montanari,Lorenzo Cavallaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Antaeus是一个利用仓库级代码上下文检测逻辑漏洞的框架，通过函数优先级排序、上下文推理、比较验证和结构化报告，在28个仓库中检测出15个逻辑漏洞，优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的漏洞检测器在处理内存安全等可表达安全属性的漏洞时表现良好，但逻辑漏洞需要推断应用特定的安全不变量和隐含假设，即使是最先进的智能体模型也难以应对，因为这些不变量通常隐含在无关代码中。

Method: Antaeus采用仓库级流水线，包含四个阶段：(1) 函数优先级排序——利用轻量级仓库范围安全信号筛选相关代码；(2) 上下文推理——结合局部代码上下文和仓库级视角分析应用功能、安全资源和信任边界；(3) 比较验证——通过对比验证剔除反映项目规范而非真正漏洞的发现；(4) 结构化报告——输出漏洞、违反的安全条件和证据。

Result: 在28个包含确认逻辑漏洞的仓库上，Antaeus检测并解释了15个漏洞，在相当token使用量和成本下优于函数级和智能体级基线模型。

Conclusion: 结合仓库级代码上下文的LLM推理能够有效检测逻辑漏洞，且在不增加成本的情况下超越现有方法。

Abstract: LLM-based vulnerability detectors have shown promising results in identifying memory-safety bugs and vulnerability classes whose violations can often be expressed through established security properties. Logic vulnerabilities, however, pose a different challenge, as their identification requires inferring application-specific security invariants and implicit assumptions about intended behavior. Even frontier agentic models struggle because these invariants are often implicit and buried among unrelated code. Motivated by this gap, we present Antaeus, a framework for detecting logic vulnerabilities that grounds LLM reasoning in repository-level code context. Antaeus follows a repository-scale pipeline combining function prioritization, context-grounded reasoning, comparative validation, and structured reporting. It ranks functions using lightweight repo-wide security signals, directing costly LLM analysis toward relevant code and reducing calls, cost, and triage effort. For each prioritized function, Antaeus combines local code context with a repository-level view of the application's functionality, security resources, and trust boundaries. This enables reasoning about how the function is executed within the broader application rather than as an isolated snippet. Antaeus identifies security-sensitive sinks, derives safety conditions for safe execution, and checks whether they are locally satisfied. Candidate findings undergo comparative validation, pruning concerns that reflect project-wide norms rather than distinctive violations. Finally, Antaeus reports sinks, violated safety conditions, and evidence, making findings actionable and traceable. We evaluate Antaeus on 28 repositories with confirmed logic vulnerabilities and compare it against function-level and agentic models. Antaeus detects and explains 15 vulnerabilities, outperforming baselines with comparable token usage and cost.

</details>


### [21] [Detecting Adversarial Evasion Attacks Against Autoencoder-Based Network Intrusion Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01194)
*Niklas Bunzel,Ashim Siwakoti*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对PANDA框架对网络入侵检测系统（NIDS）的逃逸攻击，提出了两种互补检测器：残差定位检测器（RLD）和特征空间扰动一致性检测器（FPC）。两种检测器在UQ-IoT数据集上对良性、恶意和对抗性流量均实现了接近完美的检测性能（TNR、TPR、精确率、召回率和F1分数均≥0.99），有效防御了PANDA式对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PANDA框架能够将视觉领域的对抗样本迁移至网络域，通过将包序列转换为可逆灰度图像，利用梯度攻击（如掩蔽FGSM）扰动基于自编码器的NIDS的异常评分，且不改变攻击语义，使得防御者难以区分良性流量和精心扰动的恶意流量。现有防御手段不足，亟需有效的检测机制。

Method: 提出两种互补检测器：1) 残差定位检测器（RLD），在图像空间中追踪到达时间间隔特征区域的重建误差空间集中度；2) 特征空间扰动一致性检测器（FPC），在包特征空间中直接基于包级到达时间间隔特征进行扰动一致性检查。两者结合重建评分与扰动一致性检查，在图像空间和包特征空间两个层面进行防御。

Result: 在UQ-IoT数据集上对多种IoT设备的良性、恶意和对抗性流量进行测试，两种检测器均达到TNR、TPR、精确率、召回率和F1分数≥0.99的近乎完美检测性能。

Conclusion: 将基于重建的评分与扰动一致性检查相结合，在图像空间和包特征空间中协同防御，为应对新兴的PANDA式NIDS对抗攻击提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Evasion attacks deliberately manipulate input to an ML-based system to produce an incorrect prediction while the manipulated input still appears benign. The PANDA framework has demonstrated that adversarial examples developed for the vision domain can be transferred to the network domain by converting packet sequences into invertible grayscale images, enabling gradient-based attacks such as masked FGSM against autoencoder-based network intrusion detection systems (NIDS). These attacks manipulate the NIDS anomaly score without altering the underlying attack semantics, leaving defenders without a straightforward way to distinguish between benign flows and carefully perturbed malicious traffic. In this paper, we propose two complementary detectors: the Residual Localisation Detector (RLD), which tracks the spatial concentration of reconstruction errors in the inter-arrival time feature region in image space; and the Feature-Space Perturbation Consistency (FPC) Detector, which operates directly on packet-level inter-arrival time features in packet-feature space. We evaluate both detectors on benign, malicious, and adversarial traffic from multiple IoT devices in the UQ-IoT dataset. Both detectors achieve near-perfect detection performance (TNR, TPR, precision, recall, and F1-score $\geq 0.99$) against adversarial examples across the evaluated IoT traffic. Our results indicate that integrating reconstruction-based scoring with perturbation consistency checks, in both image space and packet-feature space, offers a practical defence against emerging PANDA-style adversarial attacks on NIDS.

</details>


### [22] [All-out Attack: Optimal Block Withholding Under Pay-Per-Share Scheme](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01209)
*Mustafa Doger,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了在PPS和FPPS奖励机制下，区块扣留攻击（BWH）的最优策略是全盘攻击（AoA），攻击者将所有算力投入受害矿池、只提交部分工作量证明（pPoW）并扣留所有有效区块，并证明该机制并非激励相容的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的BWH攻击研究主要针对区块依赖奖励方案，但当代大多数矿池采用基于份额的支付方案（PPS/FPPS），已有文献声称这些机制是激励相容的。本文旨在验证PPS/FPPS下的BWH攻击特性，并证明其并非激励相容。

Method: 理论分析BWH在PPS和FPPS方案下的最优策略，推导攻击者的收益函数，分析难度调整前和难度调整后的收益变化，并与FAW等高级变种进行对比。

Result: 1) PPS/FPPS下BWH攻击者的最优策略是AoA：将所有算力投入受害池，只提交pPoW并扣留所有fPoW；2) 难度调整前攻击者损失可忽略，调整后因难度降低，攻击者单位时间产生的pPoW增加，相对收益为α/(1-α)；3) 诚实矿工与攻击者获得相同比例收益，但矿池运营者因向攻击者支付pPoW报酬却无fPoW补偿而遭受损失；4) FAW等高级变种不会带来额外利润。

Conclusion: PPS和FPPS方案在BWH攻击下并非激励相容，AoA是最优策略，且攻击者获益来源于矿池运营者的损失。FAW等高级变种无额外收益。

Abstract: Classical Block Withholding (BWH) attacks have been extensively studied in block-dependent reward schemes, where pool members are compensated upon a block discovery within the pool. However, most contemporary mining pools operate under share-based scheme wherein participants are paid immediately upon submission of valid shares. In this paper, we analyze BWH under Pay-Per-Share (PPS) and Full-PPS (FPPS) schemes for Nakamoto-style blockchains and prove that these mechanisms are not incentive compatible -- contrary to claims in prior literature. Under PPS/FPPS, the optimal strategy for a BWH attacker is the All-out Attack (AoA): the adversary allocates its entire hashpower toward the victim pool, submitting only partial Proof-of-Work shares (pPoW) while withholding all valid blocks, i.e., full Proof-of-Work (fPoW).
  Under AoA, prior to the first difficulty adjustment, the adversary incurs negligible loss due to the withheld fPoWs. After the first difficulty adjustment, which reduces block difficulty, the adversary generates more pPoWs per unit time, achieving a relative gain of $\fracα{1-α}$ compared to pre-adjustment rates, where $α$ is the fraction of adversarial hashpower. Moreover, per unit time and per unit hashpower, all honest miners benefit at the same rate as the adversary. In contrast, the victim pool operator incurs losses: it pays the attacker out-of-pocket for pPoW submissions but receives no fPoW compensation in return. Finally, advanced variants of BWH, such as Fork After Withholding (FAW), do not yield additional profit to the attacker.

</details>

### [23] [EVOTS: Evolutionary Transformer Search for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00154)
*AbdElRahman ElSaid,Damir Pulatov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EVOTS框架，通过进化神经架构搜索自动发现适用于多变量时间序列预测的自适应Transformer类架构，在ETT系列数据集的多预测设定下取得了有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测任务间差异大，现有方法大多依赖固定Transformer架构，缺乏针对特定任务的适应性设计，演化神经架构设计在该领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 采用模块化基因组表示架构，灵活组合注意力、前馈和投影组件；引入修复机制保证进化过程中架构的结构有效性，从而在无需手工设计规则的前提下有效探索多样化的架构空间。

Result: 在ETTh1、ETTh2、ETTm1、ETTm2四个数据集上，覆盖单变量到单变量、多变量到单变量、多变量到多变量三种预测设定，预测时段96/192/336/720；多变量到多变量设定下，进化架构的MSE与强Transformer基线相当或更优。

Conclusion: 进化搜索能够在实际运行时约束下有效发现灵活且高性能的Transformer类架构，适用于多变量时间序列预测任务。

Abstract: Evolutionary neural architecture design for multivariate time-series forecasting remains underexplored, with most approaches relying on fixed Transformer architectures despite substantial variation across tasks and forecasting settings. This paper introduces an evolutionary neural architecture search framework for discovering task-adaptive Transformer-like models for time-series forecasting (EVOTS). Architectures are encoded using a modular genome representation that enables flexible composition of attention, feed-forward, and projection components, while a repair mechanism enforces structural validity throughout the evolutionary process. This formulation allows effective exploration of a diverse architecture space without relying on hand-crafted design rules. The proposed approach is evaluated on four benchmark datasets from the ETT family (ETTh1, ETTh2, ETTm1, and ETTm2) under multiple forecasting settings, including univariate-to-univariate, multivariate-to-univariate, and multivariate-to-multivariate prediction, with horizons of 96, 192, 336, and 720. In the multivariate-to-multivariate setting, the evolved architectures achieve competitive and, in several cases, improved mean squared error relative to a strong Transformer-based baseline. Additional analyses examine performance differences across forecasting settings and report wall-clock training time to provide a coarse indication of computational cost. Overall, the results demonstrate that evolutionary search can effectively discover flexible and high-performing Transformer-like architectures for multivariate time-series forecasting within practical runtime constraints.

</details>


### [24] [Representation as a Bottleneck for Mechanistic Interpretability: The Manifestation Unit Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00089)
*Hussein Chouman,Wataru Sasaki,Tomokazu Matsui,Hirohiko Suwa,Keiichi Yasumoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifestation Units协议——一种类型化元组协议(E,S,R,D,G)+注意力头原语(T)，用于将神经网络的机制可解释性分析结果组织成结构化字段，支持自动填充和混合检索。在生成视觉、判别视觉和语言模型上的实验表明，类型化结构在检索上显著优于非结构化基线，CNN过滤器满足因果充分性和必要性，并发现了不可约的双字段核心(S+R)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机制可解释性分析产生的大量组件级分析结果（如选择性表、电路图、特征列表）无法被轻松复用、查询或用于下游审计/干预，被锁定在每项研究的专属笔记本中，缺乏可组合性和自然语言可查询性。

Method: 提出Manifestation Units协议——一种类型化元组协议(E,S,R,D,G)+注意力头原语(T)，将每个组件的统计信息组织成结构化字段。通过混合检索（Hybrid Retrieval）实现查询，并在beta-VAE（生成视觉）、CNN（判别视觉）和GPT-2（语言）三种架构上实例化验证。

Result: 类型化结构在检索任务上显著优于非结构化基线；CNN过滤器满足因果充分性和必要性（在匹配预算控制下）；协议兼容注意力头原语，无需修改即可恢复已知的IOI电路成员（在匹配检索预算下）；发现不可约的双字段核心(S+R)，其余字段要么冗余要么干扰。

Conclusion: 本文提出的是机制可解释性的模式基础设施（schema infrastructure），而非前沿规模的验证，旨在解决可解释性分析结果的可复用性和可查询性问题。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability has produced a rich inventory of component-level analyses that characterise what neural-network components encode and how they interact. Their outputs, however, are not easily reusable: selectivity tables, circuit diagrams, and feature lists remain locked in per-study notebooks - non-composable, not queryable in natural language, and not directly actionable for downstream audit or intervention. We study the representation layer that sits between these analyses and downstream use as a bottleneck that can be evaluated independently, and introduce Manifestation Units, a typed tuple protocol (E, S, R, D, G) extended with attention-head primitives (T) for transformer architectures, organising per-component statistics into structured fields populated automatically and queried through hybrid retrieval. Instantiated across generative vision (beta-VAE), discriminative vision (CNN), and language (GPT-2), the protocol supports two findings: typed structure substantially outperforms unstructured baselines on retrieval, and CNN filters retrieved by the schema satisfy causal sufficiency and necessity criteria under matched-budget controls. The schema absorbs attention-head primitives without modification, set-recovers known IOI circuit members under retrieval-budget-matched controls, and reveals an irreducible two-field core (S+R) with remaining fields either redundant or actively interfering. We present this as schema infrastructure for mechanistic interpretability rather than frontier-scale validation.

</details>


### [25] [SNAP-FM: Sparse Nonlinear Accelerated Projection for Physics-Constrained Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00095)
*Alaina Kolli,Theodoros Xenakis,Utkarsh Utkarsh,Pengfei Cai,Rafael Gomez-Bombarelli,Alan Edelman,Christopher Vincent Rackauckas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对生成模型在物理仿真中无法保证守恒定律、边界条件等物理约束的问题，提出利用稀疏GPU非线性优化加速约束采样的方法。通过ExaModels.jl和MadNLP.jl暴露并求解块稀疏雅可比和KKT系统，在Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM)基准测试中显著加速了非线性约束投影，同时保持约束满足性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型作为物理仿真的可扩展替代方案，无法保证输出遵守物理定律（守恒律、边界条件、非线性不变量）。现有约束采样方法在推理时强制执行这些约束，但投影/校正/轨迹优化步骤的计算成本高昂，尤其是对于非线性约束。标准ML框架的密集张量运算和稀疏求解器组合性有限，掩盖了物理约束的天然结构，使得高效批处理非线性优化难以实现。

Method: 利用样本批处理和局部PDE耦合在投影子问题中自然诱导的块稀疏结构（块稀疏雅可比和KKT系统），通过ExaModels.jl暴露该结构，并使用MadNLP.jl配合GPU稀疏分解求解所得稀疏非线性规划问题。

Result: 在Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM)框架上，针对线性、非线性、一维和二维PDE基准测试，该方法加速了非线性约束投影，同时维持了约束满足性。

Conclusion: 稀疏GPU非线性优化是实现科学机器学习中约束生成采样的实用基础。

Abstract: Generative models have emerged as scalable surrogates for physical simulation, yet they offer no guarantee that their outputs respect the conservation laws, boundary conditions, and nonlinear invariants that govern the underlying physics. Constrained sampling closes this gap, enforcing such constraints exactly at inference time without retraining, but at a computational cost: projection, correction, and trajectory-optimization steps are repeated during sampling, with these steps becoming expensive for nonlinear constraints. Standard ML frameworks exacerbate this: their dense tensor algebra and limited sparse solver composability obscure the structure that physical constraints naturally induce, making efficient batched nonlinear optimization difficult to realize in practice. We address this bottleneck by exploiting the structure that sample-wise batching and local PDE couplings induce in the projection subproblems -- namely, block-sparse Jacobian and KKT systems -- exposing this structure using ExaModels.jl and solving the resulting sparse nonlinear programs with MadNLP.jl and GPU sparse factorization. Applied to Physics-Constrained Flow Matching (PCFM), on PDE benchmarks with linear, nonlinear, one-dimensional, and two-dimensional constraints, this approach accelerates nonlinear constraint projection while maintaining constraint satisfaction. These results show that sparse GPU nonlinear optimization is a practical foundation for constrained generative sampling in scientific machine learning.

</details>

*Rui Shu,Tianpei Xia,Jingzhu He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过解耦实验（SemiScope vs Tuned-Clf）发现，在半监督学习（SSL）流水线中，使用Self-Training + 贝叶斯优化的分类器调参 + 验证集阈值调优即可达到甚至超越完整SSL流水线的性能。分类器超参数优化（HPO）可恢复SemiScope相较于Default ST+RF性能提升的86%。主要贡献是分解验证协议，而非推荐新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL在安全分类中的应用常作为黑盒使用（默认参数、固定分类器、不处理伪标签类别不平衡），且近期自动化工作虽取得增益，但难以归因——这些增益是来自SSL与分类器的有效交互，还是仅仅来自下游分类器的调优？需要解耦两方面的贡献。

Method: 构建SemiScope分析工具（非部署推荐），使用贝叶斯优化联合调优SSL设置、置信度过滤、过采样和分类器。关键控制组Tuned-Clf固定SSL为默认参数，但获得与SemiScope相同的100次分类器调优预算和验证集阈值调优。在5个二分类表格安全数据集上，使用10%标签率，通过TOST（最小效应量±1.0 g-measure）进行比较。

Result: SemiScope在所有数据集上击败默认SSL基线，超过最强基线0.7-12.7点。在等预算控制下，Tuned-Clf在4/5数据集上与完整流水线统计等价（Phishing无结论）。分类器HPO单独恢复SemiScope相对于Default ST+RF性能提升的中位数86%。

Conclusion: 更简单的方案即可达到相近性能：使用Self-Training，通过贝叶斯优化调优分类器，并在验证数据上调优决策阈值。该方案在4个数据集上达到20-30%标签率、Drebin上40%标签率时，g-measure与有监督RF相差1以内，且标签率低于或等于Default ST+RF。

Abstract: Background. Labeled data for security classification is scarce. Semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates labels from a small labeled pool to larger unlabeled pools. Yet security applications often use SSL as a black box: default parameters, a fixed classifier, and no handling of pseudo-label-induced class imbalance. Aims. Recent work reports sizeable gains from optimizing SSL pipelines via joint search, AutoML, or per-component tuning. These gains are hard to attribute: they may reflect useful SSL-classifier interactions, or mostly from simply tuning the downstream classifier. We disentangle these effects for binary tabular security data with classical SSL and tree-based classifiers. Method. We build SemiScope as an analysis instrument, not a deployment recommendation. It uses Bayesian Optimization to jointly tune SSL settings, confidence filtering, oversampling, and the classifier. The key control, Tuned-Clf, fixes SSL to defaults but gets the same 100-trial classifier budget and validation-set threshold tuning as SemiScope. At 10% labels, we compare them with paired TOST using a +/-1.0 g-measure smallest effect of interest. Results. SemiScope beats every default SSL baseline on all five datasets, improving over the strongest by 0.7-12.7 points. Under the equal-budget control, Tuned-Clf is statistically equivalent to the full pipeline on 4 of 5 datasets; Phishing is inconclusive. Classifier HPO alone recovers a median 86% of SemiScope's gain over Default Self-Training (ST) + Random Forest (RF). Conclusions. The reusable contribution is the decomposition protocol. A simpler recipe suffices: use Self-Training, tune the classifier with Bayesian Optimization, and tune the decision threshold on validation data. It reaches within 1 g-measure of Supervised RF at 20-30% labels on four datasets and 40% on Drebin, at the same or lower label rate than Default ST + RF on every dataset.

</details>


### [27] [A Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Fully Synthetic Survival Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00127)
*Niccolò Maria Rizzi,Eugenio Lomurno,Alberto Archetti,Matteo Matteucci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoGS通过从多种生成模型中筛选高质量合成样本而非直接生成，在少量生存分析数据上实现了优于真实数据训练的隐私保护效果，C-index平均提升2.17，IBS提升0.67。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生存分析在临床场景中面临数据稀缺与隐私限制，现有表格生成模型在小样本下性能不足，亟需一种能利用有限数据生成高质量合成样本的方法。

Method: 提出FoGS框架：从四种不同架构的生成模型中采样候选样本，用七种生存模型集成评分筛选；两层优化管道中外层优化选择策略（生成器配额、评分器权重、随机补充、事件时间与删失分层平衡），内层优化下游XGBoost-Cox模型。

Result: 在16个公共数据集上，FoGS在C-index上平均提升2.17，IBS提升0.67；9/16数据集两项指标均提升，13/16至少一项提升；多数场景匹配或超越真实数据训练效果，隐私边界无显著退化。

Conclusion: 基于异构生成器池的样本筛选可作为隐私受限临床场景中真实数据训练的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Survival analysis models time-to-event data, but in clinical settings training data are costly and scarce: events accrue over years of follow-up, cohorts are small, and privacy regulations restrict sharing across institutions. Tabular generative models promise augmentation and privacy-preserving cohort sharing, yet are themselves data-hungry -- on the small cohorts typical of survival analysis, a single generator rarely characterizes the population well enough for downstream models trained on its output to match real-data performance. FoGS (Filtered Mixture-of-Generators for Survival analysis) reframes synthetic-data construction as sample selection rather than generation. A candidate pool is drawn from four architecturally distinct tabular generators, and each sample is scored by an ensemble of seven survival models trained on real data, using proper scoring rules as a per-sample plausibility proxy. A two-level pipeline optimizes, in its outer loop, a selection policy -- generator quotas, scorer weights, a random complement, and stratified balancing on event time and censoring -- against held-out downstream performance, while an inner loop tunes the downstream model (XGBoost-Cox). On 16 public datasets under train-on-synthetic, test-on-real (C-index and IBS, $0$--$100$ scale), FoGS yields mean improvements of $+2.17$ in C-index and $+0.67$ in IBS, improving both metrics on 9 of 16 datasets and at least one on 13 (one-sided Wilcoxon $p=0.039$ and $p=0.035$). It matches or exceeds real-data training on most cohorts, with no significant change in nearest-neighbour privacy margin relative to unfiltered sampling. Sample filtering over a heterogeneous generator pool is thus a viable substitute for real-data training in privacy-restricted clinical settings.

</details>


### [28] [GRPO, Dr. GRPO, and DAPO Are Three Operations on One Number: The Group-Standard-Deviation Identity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00152)
*Yong Yi Bay,Kathleen A. Yearick*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示GRPO、Dr. GRPO和DAPO三种主流推理训练方法本质上是同一"分歧度旋钮"的不同设置——它们都在调整一个关键数值：标准偏差，即模型对同一问题多次采样回答的"对/错"标记间的标准差。该数值决定了训练更新的幅度：分歧越大更新越强，完全一致则无学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清三种看似不同的推理训练方法（GRPO、Dr. GRPO、DAPO）之间的本质联系，证明它们并非独立技巧，而是同一机制下的不同参数设置。

Method: 理论分析三个方法在数学上的等价性，证明它们均通过调整组内回答的标准偏差来调控训练更新幅度；在Big-Math难度数据集和受控训练实验中验证理论预测。

Result: 证明了"组标准差恒等式"：对于对/错奖励信号，标准差恰好等于训练更新的大小；GRPO除以标准差、Dr. GRPO去掉除法、DAPO丢弃零标准差组，三者是该旋钮的三种设置。

Conclusion: 标准偏差归一化并非无害的预处理步骤，而是决定学习何时发生以及学习强度的核心控制旋钮。

Abstract: Three of the most popular methods for training language models to reason look like three different tricks. They are not. All three adjust a single number: standard deviation, reflecting how much a prompt's sampled answers disagree. When such a model is trained, it answers each problem many times, and an automatic checker marks every answer right or wrong. The standard deviation of those marks measures the disagreement: largest when the answers split evenly between right and wrong, and zero when they all agree. Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) divides by this number, GRPO Done Right (Dr. GRPO) drops the division, and Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) discards the groups where it is zero. Each is presented as its own fix, yet this paper proves they are three settings of one dial. That dial is not cosmetic: for right-or-wrong rewards, the disagreement is exactly the size of the training update, the group-standard-deviation identity. A split group teaches the most, while a unanimous group teaches nothing and falls silent. The same result says which problems deserve the most weight and how many tries each one needs. This paper confirms the intuition on a large real difficulty dataset (Big-Math) and in a controlled training run. What looks like a harmless normalization step is the dial that decides where learning happens and how strongly.

</details>


### [29] [FRAME: Learning the Adaptation Domain with a Mixture of Fractional-Fourier Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00162)
*Tom Saliencro,Maya Lindqvist,Rohan Desai,Priya Nair,Daniel Whitmore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分数阶傅里叶混合专家（Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts），一种可学习变换域的PEFT方法，每个专家携带可学习的分数阶傅里叶阶数，在空间域与傅里叶域之间连续插值，通过路由令牌到不同阶数的专家实现低秩更新的最紧凑表示，且不同阶数算子天然去相关，减少干扰并提升多任务组合能力。在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上超越多种强基线，且学习到的阶数在任务和层级上具有可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（低秩适配器在空间域，谱方法在固定傅里叶域）的变换域是固定的，但最优域因任务、层、令牌而异，单一基无法在所有场景下最优。本文旨在将域的选择本身作为可学习的设计自由度。

Method: 提出Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts架构：每个专家带有一个可学习的分数阶傅里叶阶数（单标量），通过chirp-FFT代理以O(d log d)计算变换；MoE路由机制将令牌分配给不同阶数的专家，实现在空间-频谱连续谱上的自适应放置；不同阶数的分数阶傅里叶算子天然互不相关，降低专家间干扰。

Result: 在LLaMA-3.1-8B和Qwen2.5-7B上，覆盖常识、数学、代码、知识等基准，该方法超越MoE-LoRA、FlyLoRA、FourierMoE、HMoRA等强基线，同时保持小活性参数预算。学习到的阶数在任务和层级上表现出可解释的专门化模式。

Conclusion: 分数阶傅里叶混合专家方法通过可学习的变换域选择，有效提升了PEFT的多任务适应能力，且开销极小（仅额外优化一个标量），实验结果验证了其有效性及可解释性。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) reparameterizes weight updates in a fixed basis: low-rank adapters operate in the spatial domain, while a recent line of spectral methods operates in a fixed Fourier domain. We argue that the choice of domain is itself a design degree of freedom that should be learned, and that no single basis is optimal across tasks, layers, or tokens. We introduce Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts, a mixture-of-experts adapter in which every expert carries a learnable fractional-Fourier order that continuously interpolates between the spatial domain (recovering vanilla LoRA) and the Fourier domain (recovering a spectral adapter). Routing tokens through experts that occupy different points on this spatial-spectral continuum lets the model place each low-rank update in the domain where it is most compact, and -- because fractional-Fourier operators of different orders are mutually incoherent -- makes the experts naturally decorrelated, which reduces interference and improves multi-task composition. The order is a single scalar per expert, trained with a separate optimizer, and the transform is computed with an $\mathcal{O}(d\log d)$ chirp--FFT surrogate, so Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts adds negligible cost over standard MoE-LoRA. Across commonsense, mathematical, code, and knowledge benchmarks on LLaMA-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, Fractional-Fourier Mixture of Experts improves over strong MoE-LoRA and spectral baselines -- including FlyLoRA, FourierMoE, and HMoRA -- while keeping the active-parameter budget small, and analysis shows that the learned orders specialize by task and layer in interpretable ways.

</details>


### [30] [Verifiable Rewards for Calibrated Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00164)
*Sadanand Singh,Allam Reddy,Manan Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可验证奖励的强化学习方法，通过无标签的状态条件经验胜率作为奖励，并采用直接预测或梯度掩码保护推理链，成功训练7B模型达到与博彩市场相当的校准水平，解决了传统强化学习在随机性预测任务中校准退化的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习本可以利用可验证奖励（如Brier分数）训练校准的概率预测器，但在实际应用中会降低校准性能。现有方法只解决了认知不确定性（模型置信度伴随可验证正确/错误答案），而本文关注随机性预测（预测本身就是输出，标签是随机结果），以NFL实时胜率预测为测试平台，研究如何解决单标签噪声导致的校准退化问题。

Method: 提出一种可验证的无标签奖励函数——基于状态条件的历史结果经验胜率，消除单标签噪声；通过直接预测或梯度掩码将梯度隔离在推理链之外，防止策略梯度破坏推理过程；仅使用该奖励训练，无需人工标签或监督微调。

Result: 7B模型通过直接预测达到与博彩市场相当的校准水平，优于零样本前沿模型；该模型与前沿模型及表格估计器达到相同的Brier分数，表明博彩市场的剩余优势来源于模型共享输入之外的实时比赛信息；梯度掩码方法保留了推理能力，而标准思维链训练会破坏推理。

Conclusion: 可验证的无标签奖励配合梯度隔离策略可以有效训练校准的概率预测器，解决随机性预测中标签噪声导致的校准退化问题，同时保留模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards can in principle train calibrated probabilistic forecasters, since a proper scoring rule such as the Brier score is computed from outcomes alone and is minimized in expectation by the true probability. In practice it degrades calibration, and existing remedies address epistemic uncertainty, where a model's confidence accompanies a verifiably correct or incorrect answer. We study aleatoric forecasting, where the forecast itself is the output and the label is one stochastic outcome, taking NFL in-game win probability as a testbed with the betting market as a reference. Rewarding the realized per-play outcome fails, because the single outcome is a noisy target and the policy gradient corrupts the chain of thought. We introduce a verifiable, label-free reward, a state-conditioned empirical win rate estimated from past outcomes, that removes the label noise, and we keep the gradient off the reasoning, by direct prediction or a gradient mask, so it cannot be corrupted. Trained with this reward alone, without human labels or supervised fine-tuning, a 7B model reaches the calibration of the betting market by direct prediction and is better calibrated than a zero-shot frontier model. That frontier model and a tabular estimator reach the same Brier score as this model, identifying the market's small remaining edge as live in-game information beyond their shared inputs. Masking the gradient, rather than dropping the chain of thought, preserves reasoning from which the forecast follows, which ordinary chain-of-thought training corrupts.

</details>


### [31] [Scaling Up Thermodynamic AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00170)
*Andrew G. Moore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于高温吉布斯采样伊辛模型的热力学计算设备训练算法，实现了深度卷积网络的逆向推理，在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100上分别达到94.9%和76.0%的准确率，并理论证明了推理成本与性能之间存在可控的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于伊辛模型的热力学计算设备在低功耗AI推理和边缘计算方面潜力巨大，但缺乏可扩展的大模型训练方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，将已有的理论对应关系转化为实际可行的训练算法。

Method: 将高温吉布斯采样伊辛系统的时间平均行为与前馈神经网络推理的理论对应关系，转化为基于反向传播的可扩展训练算法，用于训练在伊辛机硬件上执行热力学推理的深度卷积网络。

Result: 图像分类模型在CIFAR-10上达到94.9%准确率，在CIFAR-100上达到76.0%准确率（使用二元吉布斯采样）。建立了推理成本与准确性之间关系的数学理论，并实验验证了自相关时间的控制方法。渐近分析表明推理成本受限于与性能的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 推理成本与性能之间存在可控的权衡关系，可计算最优推理调度算法，为高温热力学AI模型的硬件开发提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Thermodynamic computing devices based on the Ising model show great promise for low-power AI inference and edge computing, but scalable methods for training large models for such hardware remain limited. Prior theory shows that the time-averaged behavior of high-temperature Gibbs-sampled Ising systems can implement feed-forward neural inference. We turn this theoretical correspondence into a scalable and purely backpropagation-based algorithm for training deep convolutional networks for thermodynamic inference on Ising machine hardware. Our image classification models achieve accuracies of 94.9% on CIFAR-10 and 76.0% on CIFAR-100 under binary Gibbs sampling. We then develop and experimentally validate a mathematical theory relating inference cost to accuracy and controlling autocorrelation times. Subsequently, we calculate asymptotic results showing that inference cost is bounded by a well-controlled tradeoff with performance and exhibit algorithms for computing optimal inference schedules. Finally, we discuss implications for hardware development and the future of high-temperature thermodynamic AI models.

</details>


### [32] [TallyTrain: Communication-Efficient Federated Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00173)
*Radhakrishna Achanta,Will Reed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TallyTrain是一种联邦学习协议，通过仅传输每个peer的argmax类别索引（每探针仅需⌈log₂C⌉比特），同时压缩了模型规模和类别数量两个正交轴的通信开销。在非IID训练下，硬标签多数投票比软标签蒸馏更优，因为软标签会放大未训练好模型的错误自信。标准基准测试中，TallyTrain在通信量降低三个数量级的情况下匹配或超越软标签蒸馏，其带宽桥接变体在Pareto意义上支配FedAvg、FedProx和FedDF的所有测试点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在模型规模和类别数量两个正交维度上受限于带宽：模型大小限制了参数平均方法可以合并的频率，而类别数量使得在大词汇量下每探针的软标签蒸馏成本过高。随着现代系统规模扩大，这两个瓶颈愈发收紧。

Method: 提出TallyTrain协议，将类别数量轴压缩到每探针⌈log₂C⌉比特，仅传输每个peer的argmax类别索引（硬标签）。基于非IID场景下未训练好的peer"自信但错误"的特性，采用多数投票过滤噪声。还提出带宽桥接变体，将廉价的硬标签共识与稀疏参数合并结合。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，TallyTrain在通信量降低三个数量级的情况下匹配或超越软标签蒸馏。带宽桥接变体在Pareto意义上支配FedAvg、FedProx和FedDF的所有测试操作点。

Conclusion: 硬标签共识在联邦学习非IID场景下不仅压缩通信，还能比软标签蒸馏获得更好的效果，为联邦学习的带宽效率提供了新的设计思路。

Abstract: Federated learning is bandwidth-bound on two orthogonal axes: model size, which limits how often parameter-averaging methods can afford to merge, and class count, which makes per-probe soft-label distillation prohibitive at large vocabularies. Both ceilings tighten as modern systems scale. We collapse the class-count axis to $\lceil \log_2 C \rceil$ bits per probe by transmitting only each peer's $\arg\max$ class index, where $C$ is the number of output classes. The resulting protocol, TallyTrain, is not merely compressed: under non-IID training it can be preferable to soft-label distillation, because under-trained peers are confidently wrong and majority voting filters this noise where soft-label averaging amplifies it. Across standard benchmarks, TallyTrain matches or beats soft-label distillation at up to three orders of magnitude less communication. We also relax the model-size axis: we compose the cheap hard-label consensus with sparse parameter merges to obtain a bandwidth-bridge variant, which Pareto-dominates every tested operating point of the standard FedAvg, FedProx and FedDF baselines.

</details>
