Real-time path planning must simultaneously accommodate high robot densities and heterogeneous motion capabilities, while communication delays, execution uncertainties, and other disturbances may cause robots to deviate from the temporal assumptions underlying planned paths. Such deviations can lead to excessive waiting and congestion propagation across the fleet. This paper presents SCALE, a reactive online coordination framework that enables real-time planning while maintaining robust execution. Within this framework, we introduce a motion-induced conflict reduction mechanism to support the online generation of feasible paths for online conflict resolution. To mitigate the effects of disturbances, we further design a generalized Conjugate Action-Precedence Hypergraph (CAPH) that adaptively adjusts precedence relations among robots. Extensive validation experiments, together with a three-day deployment in a warehouse, demonstrate the

</details>


### [147] [Domain Arithmetic: One-Shot VLA Adaptation under Environmental Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00666)
*Taewook Kang,Taeheon Kim,Donghyun Shin,Jonghyun Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART是一种基于类比推理的权重向量算术方法，通过单次演示即可使VLA模型适应环境变化（如相机视角或机器人型号改变），优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在环境变化（相机位姿变化、机器人更换等）下性能显著下降，而传统适应方法需要收集大量演示数据，成本高昂。

Method: 提出Domain ARiThmetic (DART)，通过权重向量算术（添加领域特定信息）实现类比适应，利用子空间对齐过滤奇异分量中的噪声，仅需单次演示即可完成适应。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，DART在单次演示场景下，面对视觉和本体变化（不同机器人），均优于现有的VLA适应方法。

Conclusion: DART通过高效的权重算术和子空间对齐，显著降低了VLA模型跨环境适应的数据需求，为机器人策略迁移提供了低成本、高效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often fail to perform the same learned tasks under environmental shifts, such as changes in camera pose and shifts to a different but similar robot (e.g., from Panda to UR5e). Adapting these models to the shifted environment (i.e., target domain) often requires training on multiple demonstrations for each task, which are costly to collect. To reduce the burden of data curation and training, we propose an analogy-based method that adapts VLA models under environmental shifts through weight vector arithmetic with domain-specific information addition, named Domain ARiThmetic (DART). Unlike prior approaches, DART requires collecting only a single demonstration, enabling efficient adaptation. To accurately isolate domain-specific information for addition, DART performs subspace alignment between singular components in weight vectors to filter out noisy components. In both simulated and real-world experiments, DART outperforms existing VLA adaptation methods in one-shot scenarios across diverse visual and embodiment shifts. Code is available at https://github.com/snumprlab/dart.

</details>


### [148] [Path Planning in Physically Viable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00673)
*Su Ann Low,Cheng-Hsi Hsiao,Xingjian Li,Adam J. Thorpe,Ufuk Topcu,Krishna Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理可行的世界模型，通过将3D高斯溅射重建场景与物理仿真结合，使机器人能够在部署前评估未来地形变化（如洪水）对导航路线的可行性影响，从而避免因使用过时地图而导致的路径安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在非结构化户外环境中依赖部署前采集的场景重建进行路径规划，但地图可能因环境物理变化而过时，导致原本可行的路线变得不安全或不可达，且偏远环境难以重新建图。

Method: 1) 使用物理仿真增强重建的3D高斯溅射场景，生成物理修改后的环境版本，无需重新采集传感器数据或重建地图；2) 实现考虑物理事件、障碍物和变形的地形感知规划器，评估路线在变化后的可行性。

Result: 在德克萨斯州中部真实户外场地，模拟不同严重程度的洪水场景进行测试。结果表明，物理可行世界模型能够暴露长期路线失败和重新规划行为，而这些在仅基于原始重建环境规划时无法显现。

Conclusion: 物理可行世界模型使机器人能够在部署前评估未来地形变化如何影响路线可行性，避免在条件变化后陷入无法撤退或恢复的危险境地。

Abstract: Robots deployed in unstructured outdoor environments often plan from scene reconstructions collected before deployment because operators cannot remap large or remote sites before every mission. As a result, robots must make long-horizon planning decisions using stale maps that assume the terrain remains unchanged, even though physical changes to the environment may render previously feasible routes unsafe or unreachable at execution time. We present a physically viable world model for evaluating what-if queries for robot navigation under future terrain change. The system augments reconstructed 3D Gaussian splat scenes with physics-based simulation to generate physically modified versions of the same environment without recollecting sensor data or rebuilding the map. We then implement a terrain-aware planner that accounts for physical events, obstacles, and deformations that are simulated by the world model. This allows robots and human operators to evaluate whether planned routes remain feasible before committing to a planned route, particularly in constrained environments where retreat or recovery may become impossible once conditions change. We evaluate the system on a real outdoor field site in Central Texas using simulated flooding across multiple severity levels. We measure route and mission feasibility as terrain conditions deteriorate under physically simulated interventions. Our results show that physically viable world models expose long-horizon route failures and rerouting behavior that are not apparent when planning only on the original reconstructed environment, allowing robots to evaluate how future terrain changes may affect route feasibility before deployment.

</details>


### [149] [Beyond Line of Sight: Hybrid Validation of V2X Collective Perception in Complex Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00874)
*Markos Antonopoulos,Anastasia Bolovinou,Bill Roungas,Elena Daskalaki,Angelos Amditis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向V2X集体感知的概率框架与混合验证方法，通过贝叶斯融合算法将多智能体异构传感器观测集成到共享概率占用网格中，结合CARLA仿真与车辆在环实验，在环形交叉口场景下将视野覆盖率提升260%，占用单元召回率从0.82提升至0.94。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体感知方法缺乏对感知不确定性的显式建模，且仿真与真实世界评估之间存在鸿沟，难以支持可解释、可信任的协同感知系统验证与安全部署。

Method: 提出贝叶斯融合算法构建概率占用网格，每个网格单元编码占用似然与不确定性；开发基于CARLA虚拟环境与车辆在环实验的混合测试框架。

Result: 在环形交叉口场景下，六智能体协同感知将视野覆盖率提升260%，占用单元召回率从仅自车的0.82提升至0.94（标称定位条件下）。

Conclusion: 所提方法为集体感知系统提供了可复现、可解释的验证基础，支撑协作式自动驾驶的安全认证与部署。

Abstract: This paper introduces a probabilistic framework and hybrid validation methodology for V2X-enabled Collective Perception (CP) in complex traffic scenarios. The proposed Bayesian fusion algorithm extends the perceptual horizon of connected and autonomous vehicles by integrating heterogeneous sensor observations from multiple agents into a shared probabilistic occupancy grid. Each cell of this grid encapsulates both occupancy likelihood and uncertainty, enabling explainable and trustworthy situational awareness beyond the ego vehicle's field of view. To bridge the gap between simulation and real-world evaluation, a hybrid testing framework is developed, combining CARLA-based virtual environments with vehicle-in-the-loop experimentation. Experimental results in a roundabout scenario demonstrate a 260 percent increase in field-of-view coverage and a rise in occupied-cell recall from 0.82 (ego-only) to 0.94 (six-agent CP) under nominal localization conditions. Overall, the proposed approach provides a reproducible and interpretable foundation for validating CP systems, supporting the safe and certifiable deployment of cooperative autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [150] [AMBUSH: Collaborative Capture in Complex Environments with Neural Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01029)
*Junfeng Chen,YinHang Luo,Xinyi Wang,Junrui Li,Meng Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于"伏击"策略的混合蒙特卡洛树搜索（H-MCTS）算法，使多个较慢追击者能在复杂环境中捕获速度更快的逃逸者，并通过神经网络加速提升规划效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多局限于无障碍环境或稀疏规则障碍物场景，缺乏在复杂环境中多慢速追捕者协作捕获快速逃逸者的高效解决方案。

Method: 首先设计参数化伏击策略（含离散与连续参数），考虑工作空间拓扑特性、截断视线可见性、相对速度比和有限捕获范围；其次提出H-MCTS算法优化参数实现长期规划；最后训练神经网络加速器离线学习参数排名，在线替代H-MCTS的rollout过程以加速规划。

Result: 在仿真和硬件实验中，该方法成功捕获速度两倍于追捕者的人类控制逃逸者，验证了效率和有效性。

Conclusion: 仅凭"伏击"策略就足以使多个慢速追捕者在复杂环境中高效捕获不同智能水平的快速逃逸者，神经网络加速可兼顾规划质量与效率。

Abstract: Collaborative capture of dynamic targets is common in nature as an essential strategy for weaker species against the strong. Similar concepts have shown to be useful for numerous robotic applications, such as security and surveillance, search and rescue. However, most existing works focus on analytical and geometric solutions or end-to-end reinforcement learning methods, which are largely constrained to obstacle-free environments or scenarios with sparse, regularly distributed obstacles. This work tackles the problem from a unique perspective: the renowned strategy of``ambush'' alone would suffice for multiple slower pursuers to capture one faster evader with different levels of intelligence efficiently in complex environments. A parameterized strategy of ambush (including discrete and continuous parameters) is designed first, which takes into account the topological properties of the workspace, the truncated line-of-sight visibility, the relative speed ratio and the limited capture range. Then, a Hybrid Monte Carlo Tree Search (H-MCTS) algorithm is proposed to optimize the associated parameters through long-term planning, enabling the identification of highly promising parameters for future capture. Lastly, the neural acceleration is trained offline to learn the ranking of different choices of parameters across various environments, and to directly predict scores, replacing the rollout process in H-MCTS. The neural acceleration is adopted during online H-MCTS to accelerate the planning procedure while guaranteeing the planning quality. Its efficiency and effectiveness are validated in extensive simulations and hardware experiments, against evaders with different capabilities and intelligence levels, including two-times higher velocity and human-controlled behavior.

</details>


### [151] [DART-VLN: Test-Time Memory Decay and Anti-Loop Regularization for Discrete Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01043)
*Shaoheng Zhang,Zhichen Li,Jie Mei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DART-VLN提出了一种无需训练、仅需测试时控制的框架，通过记忆衰减和防循环正则化，在不改变预训练模型的情况下，显著提升了离散视觉语言导航的记忆可靠性与路径效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的离散VLN智能体在部分可观测环境下存在两个关键缺陷：一是记忆读取时陈旧历史证据的干扰，二是动作选择时低效的局部回溯。这些问题即使在强冻结骨干网络下仍然存在。

Method: 提出DART-VLN框架，包含两个轻量级测试时控制模块：(1) Test-Time Memory Decay：一种读取侧记忆重加权规则，通过抑制陈旧和冗余证据来提升记忆质量，无需修改存储内容；(2) Anti-Loop Regularization：一种轻量级的下一跳惩罚机制，在动作选择时直接抑制立即反向移动。整个框架无需额外可学习参数，不改变主干网络。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE基准上：(1) 单独使用decay可稳定提升读取侧性能；(2) decay+anti-loop实现了最佳的质量-效率权衡，生成更短轨迹、更低运行时，并在关键场景下提升导航性能。行为分析证实anti-loop减少了局部回溯并改善了路径效率。

Conclusion: 适度的测试时控制（记忆衰减与防循环正则化）可以在无需重新训练的前提下，使基于记忆的离散VLN更可靠、更高效，为冻结骨干网络的测试时优化提供了有效思路。

Abstract: Memory-based discrete vision-language navigation (VLN) agents must act under partial observability, yet even strong frozen backbones remain vulnerable at test time. Two common failure modes are stale historical evidence at memory readout and inefficient local backtracking during action selection. We present DART-VLN, a training-free test-time control framework for discrete VLN. DART-VLN combines Test-Time Memory Decay, a read-side memory reweighting rule that suppresses stale and redundant evidence without rewriting stored content, with Anti-Loop Regularization, a lightweight next-hop penalty that discourages immediate reversals during action selection. The framework introduces no new learnable parameters and leaves the learned backbone unchanged. Experiments on R2R and REVERIE show a consistent pattern: decay-only provides stable read-side gains, while decay+anti-loop achieves the best overall quality-efficiency trade-off, yielding shorter trajectories, lower runtime, and improved navigation performance in key settings. Behavioral analysis further confirms that anti-loop regularization reduces local backtracking and improves path efficiency under frozen backbones. Overall, the results show that modest test-time control can make memory-based discrete VLN more reliable and efficient without retraining.

</details>


### [152] [Robots Ask the Way: Communication-Enabled Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01044)
*Valentino Sacco,Luca Scofano,Indro Spinelli,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通信增强型社交导航(CommNav)，让机器人在多人类环境中主动向居民询问目标人物信息。在Habitat 3.0c仿真环境中，通信模块使导航成功率提升10个百分点，且对自然语言指令具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交导航方法仅关注反应式避障和轨迹适应，缺乏通过人机通信主动收集信息的机制，难以在多智能体环境中高效定位特定目标个体。

Method: 提出CommNav任务，机器人主动向居民请求关于目标人物最近 sightings、位置和移动的信息；扩展Habitat 3.0为Habitat 3.0c支持多人类环境和信息交换协议；设计通信模块(COMM)并探索从结构化数据到自然语言的过渡（LLM生成指令和人类口语指令）。

Result: 在SOTA社交导航模型上添加COMM使Episode Success提升10个百分点；预训练COMM在通信预文本任务上有效应对偶发交互信号；导航策略对自然口语语言高度鲁棒，成功率与使用完美结构化数据的模型统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 显式人机通信显著增强多人导航性能；通信模块的预训练策略有效；策略对自然语言鲁棒。CommNav为主动信息获取式社交导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Assistive autonomous robots operating in multi-agent environments require efficient strategies to locate specific individuals among multiple residents. Current social navigation methods focus on reactive collision avoidance and trajectory adaptation, but lack mechanisms to proactively gather information through human-robot communication.
  We introduce Communication-enabled Social Navigation (CommNav). In this novel task, robotic agents actively seek assistance from residents to locate target individuals by requesting information about recent sightings, locations, and movements.
  To evaluate CommNav, we extend Habitat 3.0 to create Habitat 3.0c, a communication-enabled variant supporting multi-human environments with information exchange protocols. Adding our communication module (COMM) to a state-of-the-art social navigation model yields a 10 percentage-point improvement in Episode Success. We further investigate the transition from structured data to natural language by evaluating models trained on LLM-generated instructions and on colloquial instructions collected from a human study.
  Our experiments reveal that: (i) explicit human-robot communication substantially enhances multi-person navigation performance; (ii) pre-training COMM on a communication pretext task effectively addresses the challenge of occasional interaction signals; and (iii) the navigation policy is highly robust to natural, colloquial human language, achieving an episode success statistically similar to the model using perfect structured data.

</details>


### [153] [AutoSpeed: Annotation-Free Stage-Adaptive Motion Speed Learning for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01051)
*Qingda Hu,Ziheng Qiu,Jieru Zhao,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AutoSpeed是一个模型无关的学习框架，使基于imitation learning的视觉运动策略能够根据任务阶段自适应调整运动速度和时序预测范围，从而减少执行时间并提高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于IL的视觉运动策略以固定速度模仿专家演示，无法根据任务阶段难度动态调整执行速度和时序预测范围，限制了灵活性和整体任务吞吐量。

Method: 将不同速度下的未来轨迹作为候选优化目标，通过复合代价函数（权衡预测误差与预测范围）评估候选，并优化策略朝向最小代价候选。在频域中利用离散余弦变换（DCT）实现平滑的非整数速度缩放，无需速度或阶段标注。

Result: 显著减少任务执行时间，同时提高任务成功率。推断的运动速度与任务阶段之间存在强对应关系。

Conclusion: AutoSpeed是一种有效且模型无关的方法，能够为现有视觉运动策略赋予阶段自适应速度调制能力，兼顾效率与成功率。

Abstract: Different stages of manipulation tasks exhibit varying levels of difficulty, suggesting stage-dependent motion speeds and temporal prediction horizons. However, existing IL-based visuomotor policies typically imitate the execution speed of expert demonstrations and operate with a fixed temporal prediction horizon, limiting flexibility and overall task throughput. In this paper, we introduce AutoSpeed, a model-agnostic learning framework that enables existing visuomotor policies to predict trajectories with stage-adaptive motion speeds, without requiring speed or stage annotations. We treat future trajectories at different speeds as candidate optimization targets, evaluate each candidate using a composite cost that trades off prediction error against prediction horizon, and optimize the policy toward the minimum-cost candidate. With a fixed-length action sequence, speed modulation adjusts the effective temporal prediction horizon: simple stages are executed faster with a longer prediction horizon, whereas complex stages are executed more slowly with a shorter prediction horizon. Specifically, we implement speed modulation in the frequency domain via the discrete cosine transform (DCT), which enables smooth, non-integer speed scaling and thus preserves motion continuity. Extensive evaluations show that AutoSpeed substantially reduces task execution time while also improving success rates. Under the AutoSpeed framework, the inferred motion speeds exhibit a strong correspondence with task stages.

</details>


### [154] [RoboWorld: Fast and Reliable Neural Simulators for Generalist Robot Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01060)
*Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,JaeHyeok Doo,Sungdong Kim,Minjoon Seo,Hyungmok Son,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboWorld是一个自动化评估管道，结合了快速自回归视频世界模型与任务进度感知的视觉语言模型评分，通过Step Forcing技术减少训练-测试不匹配，实现与真实世界机器人评估的高度对齐（Pearson's r=0.989, Spearman's ρ=0.970）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频世界模型作为评估通用机器人策略的可扩展替代方案面临两大挑战：世界模型误差导致生成 rollout 不可靠，以及推理速度慢限制大规模吞吐量。

Method: 提出RoboWorld自动化评估管道，包含：(1) 快速自回归视频世界模型；(2) 任务进度感知的视觉语言模型评分器；(3) Step Forcing技术——结合锚定上下文和单步自回归上下文以减少训练-测试不匹配，同时保留动作-观测动态。

Result: RoboWorld与真实世界机器人评估高度对齐，Pearson's r = 0.989，Spearman's ρ = 0.970。

Conclusion: RoboWorld通过Step Forcing和任务进度感知评分有效解决了视频世界模型在机器人策略评估中的可靠性和效率问题，实现了与真实环境高度一致的自动化评估。

Abstract: Video world models are emerging as a scalable alternative for evaluating generalist robot policies, bypassing the physical constraints and engineering burdens of real-world deployment. However, evaluating policies with video world models remains challenging, as world-model errors can make generated rollouts unreliable and slow inference limits large-scale throughput. We introduce RoboWorld, an automated evaluation pipeline that pairs a fast autoregressive video world model with a task-progress-aware vision-language model scoring. To enable reliable long-horizon autoregressive world-model rollouts, we propose Step Forcing, which combines anchored and one-step self-forwarded contexts to reduce train--test mismatch while preserving action--observation dynamics. Together, these components enable RoboWorld to align strongly with real-world robot evaluation across tasks and environments, achieving Pearson's r = 0.989 and Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.970.

</details>


### [155] [Human-Centric Transferable Tactile Pre-Training for Dexterous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01067)
*Chi Zhang,Penglin Cai,Ziheng Xi,Haoqi Yuan,Hao Luo,Wanpeng Zhang,Sipeng Zheng,Chaoyi Xu,Zongqing Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出大规模触觉-动作数据集H-Tac（160小时第一人称人类视频，涵盖300+任务和13.5万条片段），并基于此构建可迁移触觉预训练系统TTP，通过统一触觉与动作空间弥合人机差距，利用触觉专家预测未来触觉信息以显式建模接触动力学，在仿真和实物机器人上实现了优越的泛化与精细操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉数据集规模小、接触覆盖窄，且视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在触觉模态上仅能进行与动力学无关的后训练，限制了在下游任务上的性能上限。

Method: 构建大规模触觉-动作数据集H-Tac；提出可迁移触觉预训练（TTP）框架，在预训练和后训练阶段使用统一的触觉与动作空间，以保留人机迁移过程中的先验知识；利用触觉专家进行未来触觉预测，显式建模接触动力学与精确物理交互。

Result: 在仿真和实物机器人上的大量实验表明，该模型实现了优越的性能，展现出鲁棒的泛化能力和精细操作能力。

Conclusion: TTP通过人机迁移为可扩展的触觉预训练铺平了道路。

Abstract: As an essential modality for dexterous and contact-rich tasks, tactile sensing provides precise force feedback that cannot be reliably inferred from vision. However, limited by hardware and data collection systems, existing datasets with tactility remain small in scale and narrow in contact coverage. Meanwhile, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models with tactile modality are constrained on dynamics-agnostic post-training, which limits the performance ceiling on downstream tasks. In this paper, we present H-Tac, a large-scale tactile-action dataset with 160-hour egocentric human videos containing more than 300 tasks and 135k episodes. Building upon this, we propose Transferable Tactile Pre-Training (TTP), a system of tactile-based pre-training on human data for fine-grained robotic tasks. To bridge the gap between humans and robots, we use unified tactile and action spaces throughout the pre-training and post-training phases, preserving prior knowledge during human-to-robot transfer. By leveraging a tactile expert for future tactile prediction, our framework explicitly models the contact dynamics and precise physical interactions. Extensive experiments in simulation and on real robots demonstrate that our model achieves superior performance, exhibiting robust generalization and fine-grained manipulation capabilities. TTP paves the way for scalable tactile pre-training via human-to-robot transfer.

</details>


### [156] [Where Am I? Semantic Map Grounding via Vision-Language Models for Multi-Modal Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01079)
*Suraj Borate,Aarav Shah,Madhu Vadali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将机器人定位重构为语义推理任务，使用微调的Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型（结合LoRA和轻量回归头），融合相机图像、激光雷达扫描和语义栅格地图直接预测连续位姿。在自定义数据集上达到96.75%完整位姿精度（位置误差0.11m，朝向误差5.7度），并展现出跨模态鲁棒性和对未知类别、不完整地图的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统几何定位方法在GPS缺失的室内环境中存在局限性，受人类利用物体级线索和标注地图进行定位的启发，探索将视觉-语言模型用于语义定位而非几何估计。

Method: 使用Qwen2.5-VL-7B作为骨干网络，通过LoRA微调，附加轻量回归头从最终隐藏状态直接预测连续位姿(x,y,theta)；采用组合位置-方向损失和课程学习策略；输入包含前视相机图像、极坐标激光雷达扫描和俯视语义栅格地图。

Result: 在18,017样本测试集上：位置精度98.23%，方向精度98.00%，完整位姿精度96.75%，平均位置误差0.11m，平均朝向误差5.7度，推理速度0.62s/样本。在7个未见物体类别上位置精度90.99%（仅降7.2个百分点）；不完整地图下微调恢复至93.72%。消融实验显示无激光雷达时精度95.06%，而仅相机无可见物体时精度降至70.74%，激光雷达可支撑92.33%。

Conclusion: 语义推理方法可有效替代几何方法实现室内定位，跨模态融合（尤其是激光雷达作为相机语义缺失时的备选）对鲁棒性至关重要；模型具备语义空间推理能力而非外观记忆，且对地图变化有适应性。

Abstract: We address robot localization in GPS-denied indoor environments by reframing it as a semantic reasoning task rather than a geometric estimation problem. Motivated by how humans localize using object-level cues and labeled maps, we ask whether a vision-language model, given a front camera image, a polar LiDAR scan, and a top-down semantic grid map, can infer the robot pose. We fine-tune Qwen2.5-VL-7B with LoRA and attach a lightweight regression head that predicts continuous pose coordinates (x, y, theta) directly from the final hidden state, bypassing text generation. Training uses a composite position-and-direction loss with curriculum learning on a custom Gazebo dataset of 120,112 samples and 527 scenes. On the in-distribution test set of 18,017 samples, the model achieves 98.23 percent position accuracy, 98.00 percent direction accuracy, 96.75 percent full pose accuracy, a mean position error of 0.11 m, and a mean orientation error of 5.7 degrees at 0.62 s per sample. Position accuracy drops by only 7.2 percentage points on seven unseen object categories, reaching 90.99 percent, supporting semantic spatial reasoning rather than appearance memorization. With incomplete maps, fine-tuning recovers performance to 93.72 percent position accuracy, showing adaptability to stale or partial map information. Two ablations highlight cross-modal complementarity. Without LiDAR, using only camera and map inputs, position accuracy remains 95.06 percent, only 3.2 percentage points below the full system. However, when the camera sees no visible objects in a wall-facing view, LiDAR sustains 92.33 percent position accuracy, compared with 70.74 percent when neither LiDAR nor visible objects are available. This shows that LiDAR becomes the primary localization signal when camera semantics are unavailable and provides a reliable fallback under occlusion or sparse layouts.

</details>


### [157] [ROSA: A Robotics Foundation Model Serving System for Robot Factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01088)
*Wenqi Jiang,Jason Clemons,Rowland O'Flaherty,Hugo Hadfield,Alperen Degirmenci,Shuran Song,Yashraj Narang,Christos Kozyrakis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROSA是一个面向机器人工厂的RFM服务系统，采用共享GPU池服务、机器人感知编程抽象和工厂目标驱动调度三大核心设计，相比传统专用服务系统可提升工厂生产力达12.06倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RFM服务系统基于单机器人-单模型假设，将推理视为边缘计算问题，由机器人本地或专用GPU处理，导致推理性能受限、电池续航差、GPU利用率低，无法满足工厂多机器人多模型场景需求。

Method: 提出ROSA系统，基于三大核心原则：(1)共享GPU池服务，机器人通过网络访问服务器级GPU提升推理性能和利用率；(2)提供机器人感知的编程抽象和系统设计，支持多模型流水线、任务级性能需求和故障处理；(3)采用工厂目标驱动调度，以最大化SLO合规的工厂生产力为目标，而非最小化单个请求延迟。实现基于Ray Serve、vLLM、PyTorch和JAX。

Result: 在真实机器人和合成大规模工作负载上评估，ROSA相比传统专用服务系统将工厂生产力提升高达12.06倍。

Conclusion: 打破单机器人-单模型假设，通过共享GPU池、机器人感知抽象和工厂目标驱动调度，显著提升了机器人工厂的整体生产力和资源利用效率。

Abstract: Robotics foundation models (RFMs) are making general-purpose robots increasingly practical for factory deployments. While RFM serving systems are central to this vision, existing systems are largely shaped by a single-robot, single-model assumption: inference is treated as an edge-computing problem handled by an on-robot or dedicated nearby GPU, and the serving objective is to minimize the latency of a single action model. In this paper, we propose ROSA, an RFM serving system for robot factories designed around three key principles. First, ROSA adopts shared GPU-pool serving, allowing a fleet of robots to access powerful server-class GPUs over the network in order to improve inference performance, battery duration, and GPU utilization. Second, ROSA provides a robotics-aware programming abstraction and system design that supports multi-model pipelines, per-task performance requirements, and failure handling. Third, ROSA uses factory-objective-driven scheduling to maximize SLO-qualified factory productivity rather than minimizing individual request latency. We implement ROSA on top of Ray Serve for distributed orchestration, with vLLM, PyTorch, and JAX as model-serving backends, and evaluate it on both real robots and synthetic large-scale workloads. The results show that ROSA improves factory productivity by up to 12.06x over conventional dedicated serving systems.

</details>


### [158] [Technical Report: Asynchronous Distributed Trajectory Estimation of Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01106)
*Adam Pooley,Matthew Hale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种异步块坐标下降算法用于分布式轨迹估计，通过近似最大后验估计问题，在引入可忽略误差的同时消除最多96.9%的智能体间通信，并证明迭代以指数速度收敛到最优估计。仿真显示误差比同类先进算法低64%，实验验证了对跨三个数量级延迟的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式轨迹估计实现通常未考虑智能体间通信和计算的异步性，导致实际应用受限。

Method: 提出异步块坐标下降算法，推导了最大后验估计问题的近似形式，在滑动窗口上估计机器人状态。

Result: 近似引入可忽略误差；消除最多96.9%的智能体间通信；迭代指数收敛到最优估计；仿真误差比SOTA算法低64%；对跨三个数量级的延迟具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的异步块坐标下降算法在显著降低通信开销的同时，保持了高精度估计和快速收敛，并能在实际机器人平台上应对大范围通信延迟。

Abstract: Distributed trajectory estimation arises in many applications across robotics, but existing implementations typically do not consider asynchrony in agents' communications and computations. Therefore, we propose an asynchronous block coordinate descent algorithm for distributed trajectory estimation. We consider a team of agents that observes a team of robots and estimates their states over a sliding window. The agents solve an approximation of the maximum a posteriori estimation problem, which we derive. We show this approximation introduces negligible errors and eliminates up to 96.9% of communications among agents. Next, we prove that agents' iterates converge exponentially fast to the optimal estimate of the robots' states. Simulations show that this approach has up to 64% less error than a comparable state-of-the-art algorithm. Experiments on mobile robots show the robustness of this approach to delays whose lengths span three orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [159] [FAR: Failure-Aware Retry for Test-Time Recovery and Continual Policy Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01111)
*Haoran Hao,Shahram Najam Syed,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Jeff Schneider*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Failure-Aware Retry (FAR) 框架，使机器人能够在测试时从过往失败中学习，自适应调整行为并自主完成任务。FAR 结合了失败对比偏好适应（从失败中构建偏好学习数据以引导策略远离无效行为）和轻量级动作扰动（鼓励局部探索），并将成功恢复轨迹纳入训练循环以实现持续策略改进。实验表明，FAR 在模拟和真实操作任务中显著提升了成功率和鲁棒性，分别比标准扩散策略平均提高17.6%和11.7%，同时在持续策略改进中显著提高了数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略在真实环境中部署时不可避免地会遇到失败，简单的重试往往会重复相同的错误，而现有的许多恢复方法依赖于人类干预。因此需要一种能使机器人自主从失败中学习并恢复的框架。

Method: FAR框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 失败对比偏好适应——从失败案例中构建偏好学习数据，引导策略远离先前失败的行为；(2) 轻量级动作扰动——在重试时引入动作噪声以鼓励局部探索；(3) 将成功恢复轨迹纳入训练循环，实现持续策略改进。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界的操作任务中，FAR 相对于标准扩散策略分别实现了平均 17.6% 和 11.7% 的成功率提升；同时在持续策略改进中，通过利用有信息的失败案例显著提升了数据效率（在重置预算和时间步预算下均表现优异）。

Conclusion: FAR 是一种有效的无需人类干预的失败恢复框架，能够使机器人在测试时从失败中学习，自适应地调整行为并最终自主完成任务，显著提升了策略的成功率和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robot policies inevitably encounter failures when deployed in real environments. Naive retries often repeat the same mistakes, while many existing recovery methods rely on human intervention. In this paper, we propose Failure-Aware Retry (FAR), a framework that enables robots to learn from previous failures at test time, adapt their behavior accordingly, and eventually complete the task autonomously. FAR combines Failure-Contrastive Preference Adaptation, which constructs preference learning data from failures to steer the policy away from previously unsuccessful behaviors, with lightweight action perturbations during retries to encourage local exploration. We further incorporate successful recovery trajectories into a training loop for continual policy improvement. Experiments in both simulation and real-world manipulation tasks show that FAR substantially improves success rates and robustness, with average gains of 17.6% over the standard diffusion policy in simulation and 11.7% in the real world. In addition, FAR significantly improves data efficiency under both reset and timestep budgets during continual policy improvement by exploiting informative failure cases.

</details>


### [160] [Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model for Robot Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01166)
*Zhiyi Li,Peilin Wu,Xiaoshen Han,Ruojin Cai,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化4D潜在预测模型，通过预测场景3D结构在潜在空间中的演化，实现了比传统2D视频预测方法更强的3D一致性和多视角连贯性，在复杂操作任务和真实机器人平台上取得了优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频的预测模型仅在2D序列上操作，缺乏3D几何理解能力，导致空间推理不精确且物理一致性差。

Method: 提出结构化4D潜在预测模型：在结构化潜在空间中预测场景3D结构的演化，条件为观测和文本指令；该表示可解码为多种3D格式，并通过目标条件逆动力学模块将生成的未来场景转化为可执行动作。

Result: 生成的未来场景具有强视觉质量、显著优于SOTA视频规划器的3D一致性和多视角连贯性；在复杂操作任务上表现优越，对新颖视觉条件具有鲁棒泛化能力，并在真实机器人平台上验证有效。

Conclusion: 结构化4D潜在预测模型作为规划器，通过3D几何感知的预测范式，有效解决了2D视频方法在空间推理中的局限性，是机器人任务泛化和长程规划的有前景方向。

Abstract: Video predictive models are emerging as a powerful paradigm in robotics, offering a promising path toward task generalization, long-horizon planning, and flexible decision-making. However, prevailing approaches often operate on 2D video sequences, inherently lacking the 3D geometric understanding necessary for precise spatial reasoning and physical consistency. We introduce a Structured 4D Latent Predictive Model, which predicts the evolution of a scene's 3D structure in a structured latent space conditioned on observations and textual instructions. Our representation encodes the scene holistically and can be decoded into diverse 3D formats, enabling a more complete and 3D consistent scene understanding. This structured 4D latent predictive model serves as a planner, generating future scenes that are translated into executable actions by a goal-conditioned inverse dynamics module. Experiments demonstrate that our model generates futures with strong visual quality, substantially better 3D consistency and multi-view coherence compared to state-of-the-art video-based planners. Consequently, our full planning pipeline achieves superior performance on complex manipulation tasks, exhibits robust generalization to novel visual conditions, and proves effective on real-world robotic platforms. Our website is available at https://structured-4d-model.github.io/.

</details>


### [161] [FastBridge: Closing the Model-Based Realization Gap in Safety Filters on 3D Gaussian Splatting for Fast Quadrotor Flight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01200)
*Tscholl Dario,Nakka Yashwanth Kumar,Gunter Brian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于3D高斯溅射(3DGS)的非线性、执行器感知的安全滤波器，通过全四旋翼动力学实现快速避障。与现有3DGS安全滤波器相比，该方法将轨迹加加速度降低47%，运行速度快2.25倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS安全滤波器使用简化模型(单/双积分器)，忽略执行器限制并假设加速度可瞬时实现，无法满足快速四旋翼飞行的实际需求。

Method: 基于3DGS的碰撞锥屏障，推导了高相对度的碰撞锥指数控制屏障函数(ECBF)和备份CBF，通过前向模拟备份策略在输入约束下保持QP可行性。

Result: 轨迹加加速度降低47%，运行速度提升2.25倍，在仿真和硬件上均验证了实时导航能力。

Conclusion: 引入执行器感知的全四旋翼动力学安全滤波器，相比简化模型方法在平滑性和计算效率上均有显著提升。

Abstract: Fast quadrotor flight requires safe obstacle avoidance under tight onboard compute limits. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) provides a continuous, geometry-aware scene representation for perception-driven navigation, existing 3DGS safety filters use reduced-order models such as single- and double-integrators that ignore actuator limits and assume commanded accelerations are realized instantaneously. Building on an analytic collision cone barrier for 3DGS, we introduce a nonlinear, actuator-aware safety filter enforced through the full quadrotor dynamics. We derive a high-relative-degree collision cone exponential CBF and a backup CBF that preserves QP feasibility under input constraints using a forward-simulated backup policy. Compared with a state-of-the-art 3DGS safety filter, our approach reduces trajectory jerk by 47% and runs 2.25 times faster. We validate the method in simulation and on hardware for real-time navigation in cluttered, perception-derived environments.

</details>


### [162] [Sensorless Four-Channel Control Architecture Using Inverse Dynamics Modeling for Human-Scale Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01201)
*Amir Noohian,Dylan Miller,Justin Valentine,Alan Lynch,Martin Jagersand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道遥操作架构，在WAM平台上验证了其在人尺度遥操作中优于传统二/四通道方案，改善了位置和力跟踪，降低了操作者负荷，并提高了最大可传递阻抗，通过开门案例证明了其在全身接触任务中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统四通道遥操作架构在人尺度遥操作中受高惯性、建模困难以及对噪声大且昂贵的力/扭矩传感器的依赖所限，性能不佳。本文旨在解决这些局限，提出一种无需外部传感器的四通道方案。

Method: 基于逆动力学建模的无传感器四通道架构，在定制的WAM双边遥操作平台上实现并验证。

Result: 该方法在位置和力跟踪、操作者负荷、最大可传递阻抗方面均优于传统二通道和四通道方案及透明度增强方法；开门案例证明了其在人尺度全身接触操作中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的无传感器四通道架构有效克服了传统方案在人尺度遥操作中的局限性，显著提升了系统透明度和实用性，且无需依赖外部力/扭矩传感器。

Abstract: The four-channel teleoperation architecture is a well-established framework for achieving transparency in bilateral systems. However, its performance in human-scale teleoperation is limited by high inertia, modeling challenges, and reliance on noisy and costly force/torque sensors. This paper introduces a sensorless four-channel architecture based on inverse dynamics modeling. The controller is implemented and validated on a customized WAM bilateral teleoperation setup. Experiments demonstrate that the proposed approach outperforms conventional two- and four-channel schemes as well as transparency-enhancement methods, improving position and force tracking, reducing operator effort, and increasing maximum transmittable impedance without external sensors. A door-opening case study involving sustained whole-body contact along the manipulator further demonstrates the effectiveness of the method in realistic human-scale manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [163] [FurnitureVLA: Learning Long-Horizon Bimanual Furniture Assembly with Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01212)
*Chenyang Ma,Yue Yang,Radu Corcodel,Siddarth Jain,Andrew Wu,Chiori Hori,Diego Romeres*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FurnitureVLA，首个在真实尺度下使用视觉-语言-动作模型进行双臂家具装配的系统研究。通过构建可扩展的仿真流程、VR遥操作系统和进度增强的VLA模型，在三个家具类型上将平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，并在真实机器人平台上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人家具装配工作主要局限于玩具级场景或单臂操作，缺乏对真实尺度下双臂家具装配的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 形式化定义了真实尺度双臂家具装配任务；2) 开发了可扩展的仿真流程用于专家数据生成和评估；3) 构建了单操作员双臂控制的VR遥操作系统；4) 提出了进度增强的VLA模型，联合预测动作和连续进度信号，实现自动子任务转换并减少推理阶段的累积误差；5) 系统研究了影响真实尺度装配精度的感知和控制设计因素。

Result: 在三个家具类型上，平均仿真成功率从48%提升至80%，设计因素研究额外带来21%的提升。在真实Kinova Gen3平台上，最难任务仅下降16%的性能。

Conclusion: FurnitureVLA有效解决了真实尺度下长时序双臂家具装配的挑战，进度增强机制和设计因素优化显著提升了装配成功率，且仿真到真实迁移性能良好。

Abstract: Current work on robot furniture assembly mostly focuses on toy-scale settings or single-arm manipulation. We introduce FurnitureVLA, the first systematic study of real-scale bimanual furniture assembly using Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs). We formalize the task, develop a scalable simulation pipeline for expert data generation and evaluation, and build a VR teleoperation system for single-operator bimanual control to collect high-quality real-world demonstrations. To address extreme long-horizon assembly with up to 7 subtasks and 1550 control steps, we propose a progress-enhanced VLA, finetuned on semantically grounded subtasks, that jointly predicts actions and a continuous progress signal, enabling automatic subtask transitions and reducing compounding errors during inference. We further study perception and control design factors that critically affect precision in real-scale assembly. FurnitureVLA improves average simulation success from 48% to 80% compared to baselines across three furniture types, with an additional 21% gain from our design factor study. We validate on a real Kinova Gen3 platform with only 16% drop on the hardest task.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Evaluating Hardware Abstraction Layer Concepts for Software Defined Vehicles: Insights into Applicability and Effectiveness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00039)
*Akshay Narla,Johannes Stümpfle,Souvik Saha,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前汽车硬件抽象层（HAL）在软件定义汽车（SDV）中的适用性，并从智能手机、网络和工业自动化等非汽车领域提取跨领域见解，发现基于中间件的HAL提供可移植性和模块化，而基于虚拟化的HAL更支持安全性和OTA更新，最终提出融合两种方法的混合HAL设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车需要软硬件解耦，但现有汽车系统软硬件高度耦合，复杂性和异构性难以满足SDV需求。现有HAL在SDV环境中的适用性和有效性尚不明确。

Method: 系统评估当前汽车HAL，从非汽车领域（智能手机、网络、工业自动化）提取HAL机制，构建基于标准的评估框架，对比分析中间件和虚拟化两类HAL方案。

Result: 中间件HAL在可移植性和模块化方面表现良好；虚拟化HAL在安全性、OTA就绪性和硬件效率方面更优；两种方法均存在局限性。

Conclusion: 提出混合HAL设计建议，融合虚拟化隔离与中间件标准化，为设计灵活、可扩展且面向未来的汽车HAL提供行动指南。

Abstract: The emergence of Software-Defined Vehicles represents a fundamental shift in automotive design, prioritizing software-centric architectures over traditional hardware-driven models. SDVs require modularity, interoperability, real-time processing, and over-the-air update capabilities throughout the vehicle lifecycle. However, current vehicle systems, characterized by tightly coupled software and hardware, struggle to meet these demands due to their complexity and heterogeneity. A critical first step toward enabling SDVs is the decoupling of software from hardware, which can be facilitated through a robust Hardware Abstraction Layer. While existing HALs offer hardware independence and standardized interfaces, their applicability and effectiveness in SDV contexts remain uncertain. This paper systematically evaluates current automotive HALs and explores HAL mechanisms from non-automotive domains, including smartphones, networking, and industrial automation, to extract cross-domain insights relevant to SDV development. A criteria-driven evaluation framework is developed to assess HALs against SDV-specific needs. Findings reveal that while middleware-based HALs offer portability and modularity, hypervisor-based approaches better support safety, OTA readiness, and hardware efficiency. Limitations in both approaches are identified, prompting recommendations for a hybrid HAL design that integrates hypervisor isolation with middleware standardization. This paper contributes to the ongoing developments in automotive software architecture by offering insights into the applicability and effectiveness of current HAL strategies. It provides actionable guidance for designing flexible, scalable, and future-ready HALs to support SDVs across their lifecycle.

</details>


### [165] [AlgoBench: Benchmarking Algorithmic Adaptation in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00062)
*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ALGOBENCH框架，通过对已知竞赛编程问题进行结构化约束变换自动构建新算法问题，评估大语言模型超越功能正确性的算法适应能力。实验表明模型在新变体上性能显著下降，且多数正确解无法满足复杂度要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编程基准（如HumanEval、LiveCodeBench）的高通过率并不反映模型真正的算法推理能力，因为许多固定基准已泄露到训练数据中，模型可能通过记忆而非算法能力提升表现。

Method: 设计ALGOBENCH框架，通过结构化约束移位变换（constraint-shifting transformations）从已知竞赛问题自动生成新的算法变体，每个变体可追溯但使原参考算法失效；引入复杂度感知指标（OPTT、OPTS、TRAPRATE、GAPT、CONSENS）评估解决方案在渐近复杂度上的适配性。

Result: 多LLM和提示策略实验显示：模型在ALGOBENCH变体上性能急剧下降；检索增强会提高旧算法的复用率；许多看似正确的解无法满足所需复杂度要求；错误主要来自算法层面而非实现层面。

Conclusion: ALGOBENCH有效评估了模型超越功能正确性的算法适应能力，揭示了当前LLM在算法推理和复杂度匹配方面的根本性局限，为更可靠的代码生成评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: High pass rates on established programming benchmarks such as HumanEval and LiveCodeBench do not always show whether a model can reason about algorithms. Many fixed benchmarks eventually become part of the public training ecosystem through released problem statements, editorials, and generated solutions, allowing later models to improve partly by exposure rather than by stronger algorithmic ability. We introduce ALGOBENCH, a framework that automatically builds novel algorithmic problems from known competitive-programming problems through structured constraint-shifting transformations. Each accepted ALGOBENCH variant is traceable to a source problem, but must make the original reference algorithm fail. Beyond pass@$k$, we introduce complexity-aware metrics -- including OPTT, OPTS, TRAPRATE, GAPT, and CONSENS -- to test whether a solution is not only functionally correct but also asymptotically suitable for the generated problem. Experiments across multiple LLMs and prompting strategies show that performance drops sharply on ALGOBENCH variants, retrieval can increase reuse of the old algorithm, and many correct-looking solutions fail to meet the required complexity. Error analysis shows that failures are mainly algorithmic rather than implementation-level, suggesting that ALGOBENCH evaluates adaptation beyond functional correctness.

</details>


### [166] [An SOA-Based Big Data Management Framework for Primary Healthcare Centers in Bahrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00021)
*Hasan Abdulla,Fatema AlJazeeri,Shaikha Hasan,Zainab Almahafdha,Ehab Adwan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文采用设计科学研究方法论，为巴林一家初级保健中心设计并应用了基于面向服务架构（SOA）的大数据管理框架（SOA-MHC）。该架构采用分层设计（表示层、服务层、数据集成层），利用SOA的松耦合、可重用性和互操作性原则，实现了医疗大数据的高效集成、实时分析和运营优化。实证结果表明，该方案显著提升了数据集成与互操作性、实时分析能力、运营效率，并降低了成本，提高了患者参与度和满意度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗行业数据快速增长，有效管理大数据面临巨大挑战，特别是数据孤岛和系统互操作性不足的问题亟待解决。

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法论（DSRM）的六个系统阶段（问题识别、目标定义、设计/开发、演示、评估、沟通），构建SOA-MHC框架。框架采用表示层、服务层和数据集成层的分层架构，核心应用SOA的松耦合、可重用性和互操作性原则。数据收集与验证涉及德尔菲技术访谈、文献审查、观察和基准研究。

Result: 实证结果表明：改善了数据集成和跨系统互操作性；增强了实时数据分析和临床洞察；提升了运营效率；降低了成本；提高了患者参与度和满意度。该架构有效缓解了数据孤岛问题。

Conclusion: SOA-MHC证明了基于SOA的企业框架在医疗大数据管理中的实际效能，为医疗信息学中应用SOA原则提供了知识贡献，并为实践者和研究者提供了经过验证的部署路线图。

Abstract: The rapid data growth in the healthcare industry has presented significant challenges in managing big data effectively. This research designs and applies a service-oriented architecture-based approach for managing big data for a Primary Healthcare Center in Bahrain (SOA-MHC) to investigate the application of SOA principles in enterprise architecture. The research employs the Design Science Research Methodology (DSRM) to guide the development of the SOA-MHC artifact, following its six systematic phases: problem identification, objective definition, design/development, demonstration, evaluation, and communication. The SOA-MHC utilizes a layered architecture comprising a Presentation Layer, Service Layer, and Data Integration Layer. The framework leverages core SOA principles - specifically loose coupling, reusability, and interoperability - to create a modular system capable of handling the complexities of healthcare big data. Diverse data collection and validation methods were utilized, including stakeholder interviews via the Delphi technique, document reviews, observations, and benchmarking research. The study's empirical findings highlight significant operational benefits from the SOA-MHC implementation. These include improved data integration and interoperability across disparate systems, enhanced real-time data analytics and clinical insights, clear operational efficiency gains, cost reductions, and elevated patient engagement and satisfaction. The architecture demonstrates the practical efficacy of an SOA-based enterprise framework in mitigating fragmented data silos. This research contributes to the knowledge of applying SOA principles in medical informatics and provides a validated deployment roadmap for practitioners and researchers transforming data management methods in healthcare institutions.

</details>


### [167] [LLVM-Bench: Benchmarking and Advancing Large Language Models for LLVM Compiler Issue Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00700)
*Zhao Tian,Yingquan Zhao,Chenyao Suo,Meng Wang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLVM-Bench（首个LLVM问题解决基准，含423个真实任务）和LLVM-Gym（可扩展评估平台），系统评估了多种LLM、检索配置和智能体在LLVM编译器问题解决上的表现，并提出了轻量级集成方法LLVM-Ens，将解决率提升至21.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLVM作为广泛使用的编译器基础设施，其规模和复杂性使问题解决变得劳动密集且具有挑战性。虽然LLM在问题解决上取得了显著成功，但它们在复杂系统级LLVM编译器上的有效性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 构建了LLVM-Bench基准（423个真实任务）和LLVM-Gym评估平台（自动化复现、补丁应用、编译构建和测试执行）；对4种代表性LLM、6种检索配置和3种智能体进行系统研究；提出LLVM-Ens集成方法，通过整合多种技术的补丁、过滤错误和冗余候选来扩展补丁空间。

Result: 当前基于LLM的问题解决技术在LLVM-Bench上表现有限，主要失败模式是补丁无效和构建失败。不同LLM和智能体之间表现出强互补性。LLVM-Ens实现了高达21.99%的解决率。

Conclusion: 现有LLM技术在处理复杂系统级编译器问题时存在局限，但通过集成不同方法的互补优势，可以有效提升LLVM问题解决的性能。

Abstract: LLVM is a widely used compiler infrastructure whose scale and complexity make issue resolution labor-intensive and challenging. Although large language models (LLMs) have recently achieved remarkable success in issue resolution, their effectiveness on complex system-level LLVM compiler remains largely unexplored. To address this gap, we introduce LLVM-Bench, the first large-scale benchmark for LLVM issue resolution, containing 423 real-world, validated tasks collected from the LLVM project. We further develop LLVM-Gym, a scalable evaluation platform that automates issue reproduction, patch application, compiler building, and test execution. Using LLVM-Bench and LLVM-Gym, we conduct a comprehensive study of four representative LLMs, six retrieval configurations, and three agents. Our results show that current LLM-based issue resolution techniques remain limited on LLVM-Bench, with patch invalidity and build failures as the dominant failure modes. We further reveal a strong complementarity among different LLMs and agents, motivating LLVM-Ens, a lightweight ensemble approach that expands the patch space through integrating the patches generated by diverse techniques, filters incorrect and redundant candidates, and identifies the most promising solution. Our results show that LLVM-Ens achieves a resolution rate of up to 21.99%, further improving LLVM issue resolution.

</details>


### [168] [Stop Hand-Holding Your Coding Agent: Engineering the Loops that Replace Step-by-Step Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00038)
*Sandeco Macedo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了"循环规范"(loop specification)的概念，将其定义为一种由触发条件、目标、验证步骤、停止规则和记忆组成的可复用工件，用于替代逐步提示来驱动编码代理。论文区分了外部循环规范与普通编程循环和内部感知-行动-观察周期的区别，通过对50个真实循环库的编码分析，发现70%的循环在自主验证区运行，74%命名了终止状态，同时指出了自动化触发和持久记忆仍不成熟的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年中期出现了一句口号"停止提示你的代理，开始设计提示代理的循环"，本文旨在认真对待这一主张，给予系统的学术化处理，为这一新兴实践建立学科基础。

Method: 通过定义和界定学科范围、构建循环规范的解剖学和分类学（围绕触发条件、目标类型、五级验证阶梯、架构和命名终止状态）、对50个真实循环库进行手工编码分析，以及基于自我纠正、奖励黑客和模型即法官脆弱性等科学文献提出设计原则和反模式。

Result: 实践在关键领域已趋于成熟：70%的循环在自主验证区运行，74%命名了终止状态；但自动化触发和持久记忆仍相对不发达。

Conclusion: 循环工程是从提示到上下文到工具框架再到循环这一进展中的新层次，但它并未淘汰提示工程；循环和提示是各有不同用途的不同工具。实践需要尊重其局限性，包括验证负担、理解债务和认知投降的风险。

Abstract: In mid-2026 a slogan reorganized how practitioners talk about coding agents: stop prompting your agent, start designing the loop that prompts it. We take this claim seriously and give it a careful treatment. We call the object of the new practice the loop specification: a bounded, reusable artifact, made of a trigger, a goal, a verification step, a stopping rule and a memory, that a human hands to an agent harness (such as Claude Code or Codex) so the agent pursues a goal on its own, in place of step-by-step prompting. We distinguish this external loop specification from two things it is often confused with: an ordinary programming loop, and the internal perceive-act-observe cycle that the harness already provides as plumbing. We position loop engineering as a new layer in the progression from prompt to context to harness to loop, and we argue, against the stronger headlines, that it does not retire prompt engineering; loop and prompt are distinct tools with distinct uses. We offer four contributions: a definition and scope for the discipline; an anatomy and taxonomy of loop specifications organized around trigger, goal type, a five-level verification ladder, architecture, and named terminal states; a descriptive analysis of the Loop Library, a public corpus of fifty real loops that we code by hand; and a set of design principles and anti-patterns grounded in the scientific literature on self-correction, reward hacking and model-as-judge fragility. The corpus shows that practice has matured most where the discipline says it matters: seventy percent of loops verify in the autonomous zone of the ladder and seventy-four percent name their terminal states, while automated triggering and durable memory remain comparatively underdeveloped. We close with the limits the practice must respect, including the verification burden, comprehension debt and the risk of cognitive surrender.

</details>


### [169] [ATM: CID-Brokered Pre-Write Admission for Multi-Agent Code Co-Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00041)
*Eagl Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATM框架通过内容标识符代理、适配器引导的原子化、中立管家等机制，解决了多智能体LLM系统中并发写意图的治理问题，在单一治理域内实现可审计、可恢复的共享变异控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统可将软件工程工作分解为规划、生成、验证和修复，但在应用受治理的共享变异之前，系统必须决定哪些并发形成的写意图可以并行执行，哪些需要确定性组合或序列化，哪些必须走故障关闭路径。现有系统缺乏规范化的治理机制来处理这一并发写意图裁决问题。

Method: 提出AI-Atomic-Framework (ATM)框架，核心组件包括：(1) 内容标识符(CID)代理作为共享变异准入子系统；(2) 适配器引导的原子化将写意图映射到语义原子和有界区域；(3) 虚拟原子在不完全原子映射覆盖时提供临时可审计治理单元；(4) 中立管家而非提议代理直接执行受治理的共享写操作。整个框架将任务意图、仓库范围、写准入、验证和证据义务绑定为一条治理链。

Result: 通过12场景确定性设计矩阵、3个存档运行案例、ATM-AdmissionBench、3个存档同文件边界案例、3周外部采用者研究和操作恢复路由基准等综合评估，证明了该框架在单一域设置中的可行性、可审计性和有界可恢复性。

Conclusion: ATM方法在单域治理中可行，但不声称广泛的比较优势或跨克隆治理能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can decompose software-engineering work into planning, generation, validation, and repair, but a narrower systems problem remains: before any governed shared mutation is applied, a system must decide which concurrently formed write intents may proceed in parallel, which require deterministic composition or serialization, and which must take a fail-closed path. We address this problem with the AI-Atomic-Framework (ATM), a specification-grounded governance substrate for software agents operating within a single governance domain.
  ATM binds task intent, repository scope, write admission, validation, and evidence obligations into one governance chain. A Content Identifier (CID) broker serves as the shared-mutation admission subsystem. Adapter-guided atomization maps write intents to semantic atoms and bounded regions; when persistent atom-map coverage is incomplete, virtual atoms provide temporary auditable governance units for conservative comparison and routing. Governed shared writes are ultimately applied by a neutral steward rather than directly by proposing agents.
  Evaluation combines controlled, field, adoption, and extension evidence, including a 12-scenario deterministic design matrix, three archived runner cases, ATM-AdmissionBench, three archived same-file boundary cases, a three-week external-adopter study, and an operational recovery-routing benchmark. The results support feasibility, auditability, and bounded recoverability within the observed single-domain settings, but do not claim broad comparative superiority or cross-clone governance.

</details>


### [170] [A Taxonomy of Single-Turn Textual Prompt Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00043)
*Vennila Sooben,Eugene Syriani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单轮文本交互的提示模式分类体系，通过可重复方法识别出30个独特且规范的提示模式，并沿两个维度进行组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中"提示模式"术语定义不一致，且已有研究对提示模式的分类方式各异，缺乏统一的分类体系。

Method: 采用可重复的研究方法，对单轮、基于文本的交互场景下的提示模式进行分类学构建，识别出30个独特且规范的提示模式，并沿两个维度组织。

Result: 识别出30个独特且规范的提示模式，建立了沿两个维度组织的分类体系。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为提示工程提供了系统化的框架，有助于统一对提示模式的理解与应用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now widely employed in software development and everyday use. Interacting with LLMs requires crafting prompts, which range from simple ad hoc sentences to extensive, detailed, and structured instructions. Knowledge about prompt engineering has been documented in several surveys and catalogs in the literature. However, the term ``prompt pattern'' is defined differently across sources, and existing works have classified prompt patterns in different ways. In this report, we present a taxonomy of prompt patterns for single-turn, text-based interactions. Following a reproducible method, we identified 30 unique and canonical prompt patterns, organized along two dimensions.

</details>


### [171] [Comparing Large Language Models on Scrum Certification-Style Questions: Accuracy, Stability, and Error Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00048)
*Robson Alves Vilar,Emanuel Dantas Filho,Ademar França de Sousa Neto,Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Wagner Albuquerque,João Paiva,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了GPT-5 mini、Gemini 3 Flash和DeepSeek Chat 3.2三个LLM在993道Scrum认证风格题目上的表现，采用三种提示策略和多次执行，发现Gemini 3 Flash准确率最高，模型在单答案选择题上表现最好，在多选题和判断题上易出错，错误呈现系统性模式而非随机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs increasingly used in exam-style QA tasks, but rigorous evaluation of their performance in strict normative domains like Scrum certification is lacking.

Method: Evaluated three LLMs on 993 PSM I-style questions using zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and source-grounded prompting strategies with repeated executions; conducted quantitative topic/format analysis and qualitative error pattern analysis.

Result: Gemini 3 Flash achieved highest accuracy; single-answer MC best, multi-select and T/F worst; better on Artifacts/Empiricism/Product Value, worse on Scrum Values/Self-Managing Teams/Stakeholders; errors systematic: overgeneralization, restrictive wording, compound distractors, market-vs-strict definition conflicts.

Conclusion: LLMs show overall capability in domain-specific QA but exhibit systematic weaknesses in nuanced normative areas; highlights need for improved handling of strict definitions over market interpretations.

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in exam- and certification-style question answering tasks, where their ability to retrieve, interpret, and apply domain-specific knowledge can be systematically assessed. In Software Engineering, such settings are particularly relevant when questions depend on strict adherence to normative definitions, roles, artifacts, and rules. This paper evaluates the performance of three contemporary LLMs, \textit{GPT-5 mini}, \textit{Gemini 3 Flash}, and \textit{DeepSeek Chat 3.2}, in answering 993 Scrum certification-style questions aligned with the Professional Scrum Master I (PSM I) assessment format. We evaluated the models under three prompting strategies (\textit{zero-shot}, \textit{chain-of-thought}, and \textit{source-grounded}), with repeated executions to assess intra-model stability. We also analyzed performance across Scrum topics and question formats, complemented by a qualitative analysis of recurring error patterns in incorrect answers. Results revealed clear differences among models, with Gemini 3 Flash achieving the highest accuracy, followed by GPT-5 mini and DeepSeek Chat 3.2, while intra-model variability remained low across all conditions. By question format, the models achieved the highest accuracy on single-answer multiple-choice items, whereas multi-select and True/False questions were more error-prone. By topic, performance was more consistent in normatively explicit areas such as Artifacts, Empiricism, and Product Value, but more fragile in Scrum Values, Self-Managing Teams, and Stakeholders \& Customers. The qualitative analysis showed that errors were systematic rather than random, involving overgeneralization, restrictive wording, compound distractors, and conflicts between common market interpretations and strict Scrum definitions.

</details>


### [172] [Prompting GPT-5 on Scrum Certification Questions: An Empirical Accuracy Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00049)
*Mirko Perkusich,Danyllo Albuquerque,João Paiva,Robson Vilar,Emanuel Dantas,Ademar França de Sousa Neto,Rohit Gheyi,Kyller Gorgônio,Angelo Perkusich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过993个PSM认证问题测试了GPT-5在三种提示技术（零样本、思维链、引用来源）下对Scrum框架的推理准确性，发现所有方法均达到85%以上准确率，其中基于引用的变体表现最佳（89.1%），错误主要集中在多选问题及Scrum团队等解释性领域，错误类型包括与Scrum指南不一致（28%）、超出指南范围（34%）和过时/偏见解释（38%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM在敏捷软件开发中被用于文档、辅导和培训，特别是在PSM等认证备考场景中，需要验证LLM能否可靠地推理Scrum这一具有规范性、明确定义规则的框架，以确保其事实准确性。

Method: 构建包含993个经过验证的PSM对齐问题的数据集，使用GPT-5在零样本、思维链和引用来源三种提示技术下进行回答，比较不同提示方法对事实准确性的影响，并分析错误类型和分布。

Result: 所有提示方法均达到85%以上的认证级准确率，引用来源变体最佳（89.1%）。正确回答集中在定义完成、事件和产品待办列表管理等明确定义的领域以及单选题；多选问题和Scrum团队、产品价值等解释性领域表现不稳定。16.2%的问题至少一种提示失败，错误分为与Scrum指南不一致（28%）、超出指南范围（34%）和过时/偏见解释（38%）。

Conclusion: 提示技术能够带来适度但一致的改进，特别是在减少误解和版本漂移方面，支持LLM在敏捷学习和认证备考中更可靠地使用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in Agile Software Development for documentation, coaching, and training. As practitioners adopt these tools to prepare for certifications such as Professional Scrum Master (PSM), a key question is whether LLMs can reliably reason about Scrum, a framework with normative, well-defined rules described in the Scrum Guide (2020). This paper examines how different prompt techniques affect the factual accuracy of LLM responses to Scrum certification-style questions. A dataset of 993 validated PSM-aligned questions was answered by GPT-5 using three techniques: zero-shot, chain-of-thought, and with-source citation. All prompts achieved certification-level accuracy above 85\%, with the citation-based variant performing best (89.1\%) and yielding the lowest error rate. Correct answers concentrated in well-defined topics, such as \emph{Definition of Done}, Events, and Product Backlog Management, and in single-answer multiple-choice items, while multi-select questions and more interpretive areas, such as Scrum Team and Product Value, were less stable. Among questions where at least one prompt failed (16.2\%), errors clustered into misalignment with the Scrum Guide (28\%), content outside its scope (34\%), and outdated or biased interpretations (38\%). Overall, prompt techniques produced modest but consistent improvements, particularly in reducing misinterpretation and version drift, supporting more reliable use of LLMs in Agile learning and certification preparation.

</details>


### [173] [SWE-Router: Routing in Multi-turn Agentic Software Engineering Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00053)
*Seongho Son,Sangwoong Yoon,Jiahua Tang,Shuhan Wang,Lorenz Wolf,Ilija Bogunovic*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Router是一种基于部分轨迹的价值时序路由方法，通过在廉价模型执行若干探索轮次后根据中间轨迹决定是否升级到昂贵模型，在软件工程任务中大幅降低计算成本的同时保持强模型的大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由器仅基于任务描述进行路由决策，在智能体设置中存在信息理论贝叶斯误差下限：相似的issue可能隐藏简单拼写错误或多模块重构，而prompt无法区分二者，导致资源浪费。

Method: 提出SWE-Router，一种价值时序路由方法：先让廉价模型执行若干探索轮次，读取部分轨迹后，再决策是继续使用廉价模型还是升级到昂贵模型。提供了贝叶斯最优性定理证明该策略不劣于直接路由且严格更优。

Result: 在覆盖当代成本-能力前沿的弱-强LLM配对实验中，SWE-Router显著提升了SWE任务的成本效率，同时保持了强模型的大部分性能。

Conclusion: 基于部分轨迹的时序路由方法能有效突破基于任务描述路由的信息理论下限，实现成本与性能的更优权衡。同时发布了多LLM轨迹数据集以支持复现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) embedded in multi-turn agentic harnesses are reshaping software engineering (SWE), but routing every task to a frontier model is wasteful when many issues admit cheap fixes. Existing LLM routers operate on the task description alone, which inherits an information-theoretic Bayes-error floor in agentic settings: a similar issue can hide either a localized typo or a multi-module refactor, and the prompt does not separate the two. We introduce SWE-Router, a value-based temporal approach that lets a cheap model run for a few exploratory turns and reads the resulting partial trajectory before deciding whether to continue cheaply or to escalate to an expensive model. We provide a Bayes-optimality theorem showing that conditioning on the partial trajectory never harms routing and is strictly better whenever exploration is informative. Across the LLM pairs of weak and strong models spanning the contemporary cost--capability frontier, we show that SWE-Router greatly improves the cost efficiency of SWE tasks, while maintaining the majority of the performances of the stronger model. We additionally release a multi-LLM trajectory dataset which allows reproduction of our trajectory-level routing.

</details>


### [174] [A Quantitative Framework for Estimating System Complexity and Cost via Component Interface Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00054)
*Ken Y. Chan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于耦合有向图的正式建模框架，通过组件接口估算外部需求引发的系统变更复杂度与成本，支持图形、代数、表格多视图表示，并通过大型零售银行平台案例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在组件内部逻辑不透明的情况下，估算外部需求变化对系统整体的影响复杂度和成本，缺乏从架构建模到项目成本估算的桥梁。

Method: 将系统建模为耦合有向图，通过组件接口估算有界变更复杂度；关联外部驱动因素与成本因子到系统元素，实现变更成本有界估算；提供图形、代数、表格三种视图表示以适应不同抽象与计算需求。

Result: 通过大型零售银行平台集成案例验证了框架的有效性，能够为架构设计、软件工程和生命周期运维提供可操作的洞察。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥合了基于组件的建模与项目成本估算之间的差距，为系统变更管理提供了理论与实践结合的方法。

Abstract: This paper introduces a formal modeling framework designed to estimate the complexity and cost associated with system changes induced by external requirements. We model a system as a directed graph of couplings, capturing the intricate dependencies and information flows between components and elements within a specific context. The proposed method enables the estimation of bounded change complexity through component interfaces, even when internal logic remains opaque. Additionally, the framework provides a mechanism for bounding the cost of system-wide modifications by associating external drivers and cost factors with individual system elements. We propose a multi-view approach to the model, providing graphical, algebraic, and tabular representations to suit different levels of abstraction and computational needs. By bridging the gap between component-based modeling and project cost estimation, our method provides actionable insights for architecture design, software engineering, and lifecycle operations. The model is validated through a case study involving the integration of a large-scale retail banking platform.

</details>


### [175] [The Illusion of Safety: Multi-Tier Verification of AI vs. Human C++ Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00107)
*Saif Mahmud,Fadul Sikder,Yuede Ji,Zhang Haotian,Jeff,Lei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了VULBENCH-CPP基准，包含8,918个C++程序（来自三个开源LLM和人类作者），通过四层验证（功能测试、静态分析、动态分析、有界模型检查）发现：AI生成的C++代码触发运行时违例的概率约为人类代码的两倍，而静态分析会因代码长度混淆而错误地认为两者安全性相当，且各验证层检测到的违例类型差异很大，单一验证层不足以保证安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对AI生成代码的安全性评估多依赖静态分析，但静态分析仅标记警告而无法确认运行时违例或推理未测试路径。作者希望回答：AI生成的C++是否比人类编写的代码安全性更差？以及常见的验证工具对风险的评估是否一致？

Method: 构建VULBENCH-CPP基准，收集来自三个开源LLM（Gemma 3 27B IT、LLaMA 3.3 70B Instruct、Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B Instruct）和人类作者的8,918个C++程序，涵盖851道竞赛编程任务。每个程序通过四层验证进行标注：功能测试、静态分析（cppcheck, clang-tidy）、动态分析（ASan/UBSan）和有界模型检查（ESBMC）。考虑同一任务不同解之间的相关性，控制代码长度和测试通过率等协变量。

Result: AI生成代码触发经确认的运行时违例的概率约是人类代码的两倍，且在控制代码长度和测试通过率后仍然成立。静态分析下两者看起来安全性相当，但这种相似性具有误导性——表面相似反映的是代码长度因素而非真实安全性。各验证层检测到的违例类别差异很大，单一验证层不足以全面评估安全性。该差距在独立生成中表现一致。

Conclusion: AI生成C++代码确实比人类代码更不安全（运行时违例率约高出一倍），静态分析会因代码长度混淆而低估这种风险，需要结合多种验证手段才能可靠评估AI生成代码的安全性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate C++, a memory-unsafe language where a single overlooked violation can become an exploitable bug. Yet most security evaluations of AI-generated code rely on static analysis alone, which flags warnings without confirming runtime violations or reasoning about untested paths. We ask whether AI-generated C++ is measurably less safe than human-written code, and whether common verification tools agree on the risk. We introduce VULBENCH-CPP, a benchmark of 8,918 C++ programs from three open-weight LLMs (Gemma 3 27B IT, LLaMA 3.3 70B Instruct, Qwen 2.5 Coder 32B Instruct) and human authors across 851 competitive-programming tasks. Each program is annotated by four verification tiers: functional testing, static analysis (cppcheck, clang-tidy), dynamic analysis (ASan/UBSan), and bounded model checking (ESBMC). Accounting for the correlation among solutions to a shared task, we find that AI-generated code is roughly twice as likely as human code to trigger a confirmed runtime violation, even after controlling for code length and test pass-rate. Under static analysis the two look equally safe, but this is misleading: the apparent similarity reflects code length rather than real safety, and the tiers detect largely different classes of violation, so no single tier is sufficient. The gap is consistent across independent generations.

</details>


### [176] [Registry-Governed Agent Lifecycle:Completing EDDOps with Evaluation-DrivenRegistration, Promotion, and Retirement on AWS AgentCore](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00345)
*Richard Kang,Vincent Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EDDOps框架，将评估作为持续治理功能贯穿LLM Agent全生命周期，并在AWS Bedrock AgentCore上实现。通过成本-性能决策框架，将模型选择从基准排名转化为受治理的经济决策，实证验证了三种基础模型在两条部署路径上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业采用LLM Agent需要模型选择方法平衡质量、可靠性、安全性、延迟和成本，而传统TDD/BDD方法不适用于非确定性LLM Agent，亟需将评估作为持续治理功能的实践框架。

Method: 提出EDDOps概念框架并映射到AgentCore Runtime、Evaluations、Agent Registry和CloudWatch可观测性架构；通过30次单轮调用（6个Agent）、9次多轮评估及注册表集成治理的实证验证，构建成本-性能决策框架。

Result: 管理型Agent平台若提供追踪原生可观测性、可插拔评估框架和受治理的注册表发现机制，即可支持EDDOps；评估证据可将模型选择从基准排名转化为受治理的经济决策。

Conclusion: EDDOps为企业Agent架构中的模型选择提供了可操作的实践路径，将评估嵌入持续治理流程，使性能与成本可权衡决策。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of LLM agents requires model selection methods that balance quality, reliability, safety, latency, and cost. Evaluation-Driven Development and Operations (EDDOps) positions evaluation as a continuous governing function across the agent lifecycle rather than a terminal checkpoint. This paper presents a practitioner-oriented instantiation of EDDOps on AWS Bedrock AgentCore and proposes a cost-to-performance framework for selecting foundation models in enterprise agent architectures. We make three contributions: a conceptual synthesis explaining why traditional TDD/BDD methods are insufficient for non-deterministic LLM agents; an architectural mapping of the EDDOps reference architecture onto AgentCore Runtime, Evaluations, Agent Registry, and CloudWatch observability; and an empirical cost-to-performance decision framework validated through a proof-of-concept comparing three foundation models across two deployment paths. Using trace data from 30 single-turn invocations across six agents, 9 multi-turn evaluations, and registry-integrated governance, we show how evaluation evidence can convert model selection from a benchmark-ranking exercise into a governed economic decision. The results suggest that managed agent platforms can support EDDOps when they provide trace-native observability, pluggable evaluator frameworks, and governed registry-based discovery.

</details>


### [177] [BT-APE: A Computationally Light Backtracking Approach to Automatic Prompt Engineering for Requirements Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00427)
*Mohammad Amin Zadenoori,Waad Alhoshan,Jacek Dąbrowski,Liping Zhao,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级自动提示工程方法BT-APE，通过回溯搜索和动态示例选择迭代优化提示，在需求分类任务中以显著更低的计算开销（减少72%输入令牌和66%时间）达到与资源密集型APE方法相当的性能，大幅优于传统提示策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需求工程任务中广泛应用，但提示设计通常依赖手动试错，导致结果不一致且次优；自动提示构建在RE领域尚未被系统探索，其有效性未知。

Method: 将提示设计形式化为优化问题，通过LLM生成候选提示、回溯搜索和动态示例选择进行迭代优化；在三个基准数据集上使用五种指令微调LLM进行评估，与四种经典提示基线及一种SOTA APE基线对比。

Result: BT-APE与资源密集型APE基线PE2准确率几乎相同，均显著优于经典提示基线（效应量大）；BT-APE消耗约72%更少的输入令牌和66%更少的运行时间。

Conclusion: BT-APE是一种轻量级、计算高效的自动提示工程方法，适用于资源受限的需求分类部署；研究贡献包括开源工具包、首次系统对比APE与经典提示在需求分类上的表现，以及关于类别定义和提示演化如何影响性能的见解。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to requirements engineering (RE) tasks, yet the prompts guiding them are typically designed manually through trial and error, yielding inconsistent and suboptimal results. Automated prompt construction remains largely unexplored in RE, leaving its effectiveness unclear. To address this, we propose a lightweight Automatic Prompt Engineering approach, Backtracking APE (BT-APE), and apply it to requirements classification. We frame prompt design as an optimization problem, iteratively refining prompts via LLM-generated candidates, backtracking search, and dynamic example selection. Evaluating BT-APE on three benchmark datasets with five instruction-tuned LLMs, we compare it against four classical prompting baselines (zero-shot, few-shot, chain-of-thought, CoT+few-shot) and a state-of-the-art but resource-intensive APE baseline (PE2). BT-APE and PE2 achieve nearly identical accuracy, both substantially outperforming the classical baselines with large effect sizes; however, BT-APE imposes a far lighter computational footprint, consuming roughly 72% fewer input tokens and 66% less wall-clock time at equivalent accuracy, making it better suited to resource-constrained deployment. Our contributions are threefold: (i) a lightweight APE framework with an open interactive tool and replication package; (ii) the first systematic comparison of APE against classical prompting for requirements classification; and (iii) insights into how class definitions and prompt evolution affect performance.

</details>


### [178] [Social Popularity of GitHub Projects: A Lifeline or a Liability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00435)
*Mohit Kaushik,Kuljit Kaur Chahal*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对73,000+ GitHub仓库的加速失效时间（AFT）生存分析，发现人力资本是项目存活的最关键因素；过度社会关注反而成为负担，且与代码可读性、文档等接入性特征结合时，会加速项目停止活跃开发；贡献者数量与社交热度的交互作用揭示了劳动力保护的可见效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交编码平台（如GitHub）上大量仓库生存率低，但影响项目存活的若干因素（尤其是人力资本、社会关注、结构特征之间的交互效应）尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用加速失效时间（Accelerated Failure Time, AFT）生存分析框架，分析超过73,000个GitHub仓库数据，模型中考虑了预测变量的时变特性。

Result: 人力资本是项目存活的最关键决定因素；过度社会关注反而成为负担；当社会关注与代码可读性、文档等接入性特征共同作用时，会放大项目停止活跃开发的风险；贡献者数量与社会热度的交互作用能显现劳动力的保护效应。

Conclusion: 需要制定治理策略，平衡项目可见性与劳动力容量，以确保开源项目的长期韧性。

Abstract: Social coding platforms such as GitHub host millions of repositories, yet many suffer from high mortality rates. Despite this, several survival factors remain poorly understood. Human capital is widely recognized as essential. Social attention, while often assumed to be a lifeline, can become a liability. Structural features that improve onboarding, such as code readability and documentation, may also accelerate the cessation of active development when combined with massive visibility. To examine these dynamics, we analyzed more than 73,000 GitHub repositories using an Accelerated Failure Time (AFT) survival framework, which accounts for the time-varying nature of predictors. Our study identifies human capital as the most critical determinant of project survival. In contrast, excessive social attention emerges as a liability, and when coupled with accessibility features, it amplifies the risk of project inactivity. Importantly, when the number of contributors interacts with social popularity, the protective effect of labor becomes visible, highlighting the need for governance strategies that balance visibility with labor capacity to ensure the long-term resilience of open-source projects.

</details>


### [179] [Large Language Models for Multi-Lingual Equivalent Mutant Detection: An Extended Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00511)
*Honglin Shu,Zhao Tian,Dong Wang,Junji Yu,Jiazhe Zhang,Xuejie Cao,Junjie Chen,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了大型语言模型（LLMs）在等价变异体检测（EMD）中的应用，使用3,302个Java和1,088个C变异体对进行基准测试。实验表明，基于LLM的方法在F1分数上优于传统方法，微调后的代码嵌入策略检测精度最高，且推理时间与现有机器学习模型相当。微调后的LLMs还展现出跨语言泛化能力，为变异测试中的等价变异体检测问题提供了可行的新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变异测试中等价变异体带来额外成本和偏差，现有纯代码分析方法受限于编译器基础设施，机器学习方法受限于训练数据稀缺和泛化能力不足。LLMs在代码任务中展现出强大语义捕获能力，但其在等价变异体检测中的潜力尚未被充分探索，特别是在多语言场景下。

Method: 使用3,302个Java和1,088个C变异体对进行综合实证研究，对比LLM-based方法与传统方法、探索策略变体、评估效率并测试跨语言泛化能力。

Result: LLM-based方法在F1分数上优于传统方法；微调后的代码嵌入策略检测精度最高；推理时间与现有机器学习模型相当；微调后的LLMs展现出可测量的跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: LLMs是解决等价变异体检测长期挑战的可行且高效的方法，为推进变异测试的实践应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Mutation testing is a powerful technique for ensuring software quality. However, the presence of equivalent mutants introduces unnecessary costs and biases, limiting its practical effectiveness. Although numerous equivalent mutant detection (EMD) methods have been proposed, they often face distinct challenges: pure-code analysis methods can be limited by their reliance on specific compiler infrastructures, while existing machine-learning approaches remain constrained by scarce training data and limited generalization to unseen mutants. Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable performance across diverse code-related tasks by better capturing program semantics. Yet their potential for EMD remains largely unexplored, particularly in the multi-lingual context. This paper presents the first comprehensive empirical study on LLMs for EMD, using 3,302 Java and 1,088 C mutant pairs to benchmark against state-of-the-art methods, explore strategy variations, assess efficiency, and evaluate cross-lingual generalization. Experimental results show that LLM-based approaches achieve higher F1-scores than the evaluated traditional methods, with fine-tuned code embedding yielding the highest detection accuracy among the tested strategies. Moreover, LLM-based approaches strike a practical balance between effectiveness and efficiency with inference times comparable to existing machine-learning models. Importantly, fine-tuned LLMs demonstrate measurable generalization across programming languages. These findings establish LLMs as a viable and efficient approach for tackling the longstanding challenge of equivalent mutant detection, offering new directions for advancing mutation testing in practice.

</details>


### [180] [Auditing Empirical Comparisons in Quantum Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00516)
*Boshuai Ye,Peng Liang,Maryam Tavassoli Sabzevari,Arif Ali Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CLAIMSTAB-QC框架，用于审计量子软件中的经验性比较声明。对119篇论文的455个比较声明进行审计，发现仅8个声明有足够证据完成审计，其中2个维持原结论、4个未解决、2个被推翻，揭示了“实质性差距”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件论文中常见的工具性能比较声明，其结论往往依赖于基准范围、电路构造、编译、采样、后端噪声、优化器选择及资源预算等多个因素。现有测试、基准测试和可重复性方法无法直接审计这些比较声明是否得到原始材料中的证据支持。

Method: 提出CLAIMSTAB-QC框架，通过记录基线、指标、关系和可接受的证据，在计算结果前锁定比较设计，输出有范围的关系结果或明确的证据边界。对于严格的标量方向比较，将方向分类为Sustained（维持）、Unresolved（未解决）或Reversed（反转）。

Result: 对119篇论文的455个比较声明进行审计：175个可表示为审计计划，79个成为标量方向规划记录，53个可锁定审计或诊断设计，仅8个有足够匹配证据完成原始比较审计。8个审计结果：2个Sustained、4个Unresolved、2个Reversed。对24个基准相关比较的诊断显示，简单检查可能保留表面方向，但锁定审计设计下证据支持会减弱。

Conclusion: 量子软件论文中的经验性比较声明存在严重的“实质性差距”（materialization gap），即大多数声明缺乏足够的证据支持进行严格审计。CLAIMSTAB-QC框架能够系统性地揭示这一差距，并建议采用更严格的审计设计来评估比较声明的可靠性。

Abstract: Empirical quantum-software papers often report that one compiler, optimizer, backend, or ansatz outperforms another. Such comparisons are not properties of a tool alone: they can change with benchmark scope, circuit construction, compilation, sampling, backend or noise assumptions, optimizer choices, and resource budgets. Existing testing, benchmarking, and reproducibility methods help assess programs, tools, executions, and platforms, but they do not directly audit whether the reported comparison itself is supported by the evidence exposed in the source paper or accompanying materials.
  We present CLAIMSTAB-QC, a source-bounded framework for auditing empirical comparisons in quantum software. Given a reported comparison, the framework records the baselines, metric, relation, and admissible evidence; locks the comparison design before outcomes are computed; and reports either a scoped relation outcome or an explicit evidence boundary. For strict scalar-directional comparisons, the reported direction is classified as Sustained, Unresolved, or Reversed within the locked audit scope.
  We evaluate CLAIMSTAB-QC on 455 comparative claims from 119 quantum-software papers. The central finding is a materialization gap: 175 claims can be represented for audit planning, 79 become scalar-directional planning records, 53 yield lockable audit or diagnostic designs, and only 8 expose enough matched evidence to audit the original comparison without proxy reconstruction. These 8 records yield 2 Sustained, 4 Unresolved, and 2 Reversed outcomes. Controlled diagnostics over 24 benchmark-relevant comparisons further show that simpler checks can preserve apparent directions whose support weakens under locked audit designs.

</details>


### [181] [Rise From The Ashes: LLM-based Static Analysis for Deep Learning Framework Bugs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00555)
*Shaoyu Yang,Haifeng Lin,Chunrong Fang,Xiang Chen,Wei Cheng,Jiawei Liu,Yiyu Zhang,Hongyu Liu,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了Phoenix，首个基于LLM的深度学习框架静态分析技术。通过构建结构化语义桥接中间表示(SBIR)和多智能体工作流，成功在PyTorch中发现了31个真实新bug，其中20个已被合并修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习框架是关键的AI基础设施，其中隐藏的bug可能带来严重安全隐患。现有的动态测试方法（如模糊测试）虽然有效，但需要实际执行测试，计算成本高昂。静态分析可以无运行时执行检测bug，但现有工作因框架的多语言架构和张量相关程序状态而受限。

Method: 提出基于LLM的静态分析技术Phoenix，核心是构建结构化语义桥接中间表示(SBIR)来建模跨语言张量流和具体代码上下文。采用多智能体工作流：1) 摘要智能体从历史补丁和CWE规则中提炼bug摘要；2) 提取智能体识别bug相关的仓库符号进行代码检索；3) 生成智能体合成SBIR；4) 分析智能体检查SBIR并报告潜在bug。

Result: 在PyTorch中针对不同异构硬件后端（Intel CPU、NVIDIA CUDA、Apple MPS）发现了31个真实新bug，其中20个已提交并合并到上游。

Conclusion: Phoenix是动态DL框架测试的有效补充，展示了LLM在静态分析DL框架bug方面的潜力。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) frameworks are critical AI infrastructures that often hide bugs with serious security implications. While dynamic approaches such as fuzzing are effective in uncovering these bugs, they require real test execution and incur high computational costs. Static analysis is a natural complement because it can detect bugs without runtime execution, offering fast and scalable testing. Unfortunately, there is still limited work targeting static analysis for DL frameworks due to their multilingual architectures and tensor-related program state.
  We present Phoenix, the first LLM-based static analysis technique for DL frameworks. Our key insight is that cross-language tensor flows in DL frameworks can be modeled, together with concrete code context, as a structured semantic bridge intermediate representation (SBIR) that LLMs can analyze for potential bugs in tensor semantic propagation. We implement this insight through a multi-agent workflow. A summarization agent first distills bug summaries from historical bug-fix patches and CWE rules. Guided by each summary, an extraction agent identifies bug-relevant repository symbols for code retrieval, and a generation agent synthesizes grounded SBIRs from the retrieved context. Finally, an analysis agent is leveraged to check SBIRs and report potential bugs. Our evaluation shows that Phoenix is a practical complement to dynamic DL framework testing for bug finding. To date, Phoenix has found 31 real new bugs in PyTorch for different heterogeneous hardware backends (Intel CPU, NVIDIA CUDA, and Apple MPS). Among them, 20 submitted bug-fixing patches have been merged into upstream.

</details>


### [182] [A Methodology for Investigating AI Patterns Prevalence in Software Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00558)
*Srinath Perera,Hasinthaka Piyumal,Frank Leymann,Rania Khalaf*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合文献挖掘与主动学习的方法，从44篇AI模式相关文献中识别出14类AI模式，并在100个GitHub开源AI仓库中验证了最常见模式类的普遍性，8-way分类任务达到56%准确率和55%召回率，显著优于11%随机基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管文献中已提出大量AI模式，但它们在真实代码中的实际使用情况尚未得到实证验证。理解这些模式在实践中的实际应用，有助于澄清其重要性和实用性。

Method: 首先通过挖掘44篇已发表的AI模式相关文献，识别出14个AI模式类；然后采用主动学习方法，在100个GitHub开源AI仓库中评估最常见模式类的普遍性，并通过普遍性估计给出发生率的可信边界。

Result: 8-way分类任务中模型达到56%准确率和55%召回率，显著超越11%的随机基线；普遍性估计提供了可用于分析模式应用的有效边界。

Conclusion: 该 methodology 为理解AI模式在实际中的使用情况提供了坚实基础，填补了该领域缺乏经验数据的空白。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence(AI)-based applications take off, a clear understanding of AI patterns can uplift the quality of AI applications.
