Many AI patterns have been proposed in the literature; however, their prevalence in real-life code has not yet been validated. Understanding the actual use of those patterns in practice can clarify our understanding both of the significance of these patterns and their utility. In this paper, we present a methodology to a) identify relevant patterns by mining the literature and then to b) validate their presence and prevalence in actual code repositories using active learning. To that end, we identify 14 AI pattern classes by mining 44 published AI pattern-related sources. Then we use an active learning approach to determine the prevalence of the most common pattern class across 100 GitHub open AI repositories. Using prevalence estimation, we propose bounds on the accuracy of the occurrences. The model achieves 56\% accuracy and 55\% recall in an 8-way classification task, significantly outperforming the 11\% random-chance baseline. Furthermore, the prevalence estimation offers usable bounds for analyzing pattern applications. This methodology provides a robust foundation to start understanding how AI patterns are used in practice, a field that currently lacks empirical data.

</details>


### [183] [Towards Better Linux Kernel Fault Localization: Leveraging Contrastive Reasoning and Hierarchical Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00562)
*Haichi Wang,Ruiguo Yu,Yesong Pang,Yingquan Zhao,Junjie Chen,Jiajun Jiang,Zan Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CoHiKer是一种针对Linux内核的新型LLM-based故障定位技术，通过对比推理和层次化上下文分析，在文件级和方法级分别提升Top-1定位准确率高达26.07%和56.85%，同时降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有故障定位技术（包括传统方法和LLM-based方法）在处理Linux内核时效果不佳：传统方法缺乏对内核复杂架构的适配，而LLM-based方法仅将bug报告和源码视为纯文本，缺乏内核特定知识的深度整合，导致根因识别能力有限且无法实现细粒度定位。

Method: CoHiKer提出两个核心创新：(1) 对比推理（Contrastive Reasoning）——通过分析精心构造的通过/失败测试用例之间的行为差异来识别根因；(2) 层次化上下文分析（Hierarchical Context Analysis）——整合崩溃报告、系统调用语义、跨文件依赖及内核特定特征，系统性地将定位范围从文件层级缩小到方法层级。该方法将定位任务分解，通过结构化LLM提示实现语义推理。

Result: 在扩展的Linux内核bug数据集上，CoHiKer在文件级Top-1准确率提升高达26.07%，方法级提升56.85%，同时分别减少8.84%和28.9%的token消耗。在非内核数据集上也表现出良好的泛化能力（文件级Top-1提升15.5%，方法级提升5.3%）。

Conclusion: CoHiKer通过对比推理和层次化上下文分析，有效将内核特定知识融入LLM-based故障定位流程，显著提升了Linux内核中故障定位的准确性和效率，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Debugging the Linux kernel remains a formidable challenge due to its vast codebase, complex architecture, and low-level programming intricacies. Effective fault localization (FL) is thus essential for efficient kernel debugging and maintenance. While existing FL techniques (both traditional and LLM-based) have shown promise in general-purpose software, they are ill-suited for the kernel context. In particular, recent LLM-based techniques often treat bug reports and source code as plain text, lacking deep integration of kernel-specific knowledge, which limits their ability to identify root causes and achieve fine-grained localization. We present CoHiKer, a novel LLM-based FL technique tailored to the Linux kernel. CoHiKer introduces two key innovations: (1) contrastive reasoning, which identifies root causes by analyzing the behavioral divergence between carefully mutated passing and failing test cases, and (2) hierarchical context analysis, which systematically narrows the localization scope from files to methods by integrating crash reports, syscall semantics, inter-file dependencies, and kernel-specific features. Unlike prior techniques that rely on static understanding and full-code input, CoHiKer decomposes the localization task and enables structured LLM prompting to reason semantically over meaningful contexts. We evaluate CoHiKer on an extended Linux kernel bug dataset against five state-of-the-art baselines. CoHiKer consistently outperforms all competitors, improving Top-1 localization accuracy by up to 26.07% at the file level and 56.85% at the method level over state-of-the-art LLM-based baselines, while achieving up to 8.84% and 28.9% reductions in token consumption, respectively. Furthermore, CoHiKer demonstrates strong generalizability on the non-kernel dataset, with comparable gains (15.5% and 5.3% in Top-1 at file and method levels).

</details>


### [184] [SchedCheck: Schedule-Robustness Analysis for Event-Driven Block Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00623)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对Scratch这类基于块的并发编程语言，形式化了其调度空间，提出了调度鲁棒性定义，并实现了SchedCheck工具。该工具通过偏序探索和依赖模型，检测真实学生项目中至少21%的并发项目存在调度敏感问题，并在公开项目中复现了17.6%的比率。方法在两种协作式事件循环上均可实例化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Scratch等块语言中，初学者编写的程序本质上是并发协作调度的（green-flag脚本、广播、克隆体共享状态），但语言未规定执行顺序。编辑、保存或混改项目可能改变图层顺序，导致相同块但不同行为。需要形式化分析这类语言的调度敏感性问题。

Method: 形式化了Scratch虚拟机可实现调度空间为初始可执行目标顺序的排列；定义了基于固定视界下观察透镜格点上的调度鲁棒性；采用偏序探索，每个依赖等价类运行一个调度；实现了SchedCheck工具，集成到生产级Scratch VM上。

Result: 在224个真实学生项目中，至少21%的并发项目在评分透镜下存在调度敏感；公开项目随机抽样复现率为17.6%；两个真实混改项目导致字母排列不同；在手工构造的故障对和32个规范定义故障上，工具检测并定位所有调度故障，逻辑故障对照报告干净；Oracle暴露了依赖模型的4个不完备问题并已修复。

Conclusion: Scratch等块语言中的并发程序普遍存在调度敏感问题，SchedCheck可有效检测和定位此类故障。方法具有参数化特性，可迁移到其他协作式事件循环上。

Abstract: Block-based languages such as Scratch let beginners assemble interactive programs from sprites and scripts. These programs are concurrent in practice: green-flag scripts, broadcasts, and clones run as cooperatively scheduled threads over shared sprite and stage state, and their authors never write a thread. We show that such programs contain schedule-sensitive behaviors whose observable result depends on an execution order the language leaves open. Editing, saving, or remixing a project can produce a copy with the same blocks but a different layer order, changing the order the virtual machine starts scripts. We formalize the schedule space a Scratch virtual machine can realize as the permutations of the initial executable-target order, and define schedule-robustness against a lattice of observation lenses over a fixed horizon. A partial-order exploration runs one schedule per dependence-equivalence class, and on projects small enough to enumerate, an independent oracle confirms it recovers every realizable outcome. On larger projects, representatives stand in for the factorial under the validated dependence model. SchedCheck implements this on the production Scratch VM. Across 224 real student projects, at least 21% of the concurrent ones are schedule-sensitive at the grading lens, and a uniform random sample of public projects replicates the rate at 17.6%, with two real remixes of a deployed animation arranging its letters differently. On hand-built fault pairs and a generated benchmark of 32 spec-defined faults across four classes, the tool detects and localizes every schedule fault, with a logic-fault control reporting clean. The oracle exposed four unsoundness gaps in the dependence model, all repaired. The method is parametric in the execution model, instantiating unchanged on a second cooperative event loop.

</details>


### [185] [The Perception and Impact of Non-inclusive Language in Software Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00626)
*Ahmad J. Tayeb,Mohammad D. Alahmadi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对1,212份有效问卷的分析，本文发现软件开发者对"白名单/黑名单"等非包容性术语的整体感知负面程度较低，但女性和非二元性别开发者以及美国受访者更倾向于认为这些术语具有非包容性，且对其归属感和工作体验的影响存在显著的群体差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管软件行业正在推动用更包容的术语替代"白名单/黑名单"等传统词汇，但缺乏对这些术语如何影响开发者感知、团队动态、生产力、归属感和幸福感的实证研究。

Method: 对开源贡献者进行大规模问卷调查，收集1,517份响应，其中1,212份完整数据用于分析，比较不同人口群体对非包容性术语的感知及其对工作体验的影响。

Result: 整体上受访者报告的非包容性术语负面影响较低；但女性和非二元性别参与者以及美国受访者更倾向于认为这些术语非包容；在认为术语非包容的群体中，非二元性别者报告的总负面影响高于男性，女性在归属感方面受影响更大。

Conclusion: 非包容性术语的影响因人口群体而异，在推动术语替换的包容性倡议时应考虑这些差异，以创造更包容的软件开发生态。

Abstract: Terminology such as "whitelist/blacklist," "master/slave," "man-hours," or "dummy value" has long been part of the technical vocabulary used in software artifacts, including source code, version histories, and documentation. In recent years, however, many of these expressions have been recognized as potentially non-inclusive and unwelcoming to groups historically underrepresented in software development, such as people of color, women, and individuals with disabilities. Consequently, a growing movement within the software industry has sought to replace these terms with more inclusive alternatives. Despite these initiatives, little is empirically known about how software developers perceive such terminology or how its continued use may influence their professional experiences and sense of belonging.
  This paper addresses the knowledge gap by examining how software developers perceive non-inclusive terminology in software and its perceived impact on team dynamics, productivity, belonging, and well-being. We surveyed open-source contributors and received 1,517 responses, of which 1,212 were complete and analyzed. On average, respondents reported low negative workplace impact overall; however, perceptions and impacts varied by demographic group. Women and non-binary participants, as well as respondents residing in the United States, were more likely to view the terms as non-inclusive. Among those who considered the terminology non-inclusive, non-binary participants reported higher overall negative impacts than male respondents, and female participants reported higher impact specifically on their sense of belonging.

</details>


### [186] [Checked Program Recovery from Execution Video: A Sound Oracle for Untrusted Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00635)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Vid2Prog的系统，能够从视频记录中恢复可运行的Scratch程序，并利用双层验证预言机（静态检查器+渲染器）确保恢复结果的正确性，即使生成器（如神经网络）本身不可信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前从程序行为观察中恢复代码的工具依赖不可信的生成器（如神经模型或搜索），缺乏正确性保证。作者希望在这种不可信生成器场景下，实现可信任的程序恢复。

Method: Vid2Prog：从视频中提取每个精灵的运动、可见性和时序信息，结合已知资产清单综合生成无源代码的候选程序。核心是双层验证预言机：静态检查器证明程序与参考代码的透镜等价性并签发证书（在偏序独立性商充分条件下从不接受错误程序）；渲染器只能反驳或见证有限一致性，不能认证。闭环系统渲染结果并重新运行恢复以获取真实标签。

Result: 在精确透镜下，预言机在246个标记不同的配对中零误接受（包括对抗性并发陷阱）；对词汇外输入和真实项目选择拒绝或弃权。词汇内恢复达到逐帧复现，80%获得静态证书；真实项目恢复率14%（受限于词汇），系统从未用错误答案夸大该比率。前沿视觉语言模型单次恢复零成功，预言机辅助修复后仅少数成功，而结构化管线恢复全部。

Conclusion: 结构化的验证管线（静态检查器+渲染器）能够在不可信生成器下实现可信的程序恢复，验证预言机带来的正确性保障是弥补生成器与结构化方法之间差距的关键。

Abstract: A growing class of tools recovers a program from observations of its behavior using an untrusted generator, a neural model or a search, that proposes candidates with no correctness guarantee. We study how to make such recovery trustworthy, in the concrete setting of recovering a runnable Scratch program from a recording of its execution. The recording shows what the program does but never its code; many programs produce the same video, so the source cannot be recovered, and the right target is a program that behaves the same as far as the camera can tell, made precise with a lens. The core is a two-tier validation oracle with a deliberate verdict asymmetry. A static checker proves lens-equivalence to a reference and issues a certificate that, granting the partial-order independence quotient adequate, never accepts a wrong program; a renderer can only refute or witness finite agreement, never certify. Around it, Vid2Prog reads each sprite's motion, visibility, and timing from the video and a known-asset manifest and synthesizes a candidate source-free; a closed loop renders and runs recovery again for ground truth. Under the exact lens the oracle makes no false accept on 246 labeled differing pairs, including an adversarial battery built to trap its concurrency quotient; on inputs outside the vocabulary and on real projects it abstains or refutes, accepting none we test. In-vocabulary recoveries reproduce their source frame for frame and 80% earn a static certificate, while whole real projects, mostly outside the vocabulary, recover at 14%, a vocabulary-bound rate the system never inflates with a wrong answer. A frontier vision-language model recovers none of the matched programs single-shot, which oracle-in-the-loop repair lifts only to a few while the structured pipeline recovers all, the gap a sound checker makes for an untrusted generator.

</details>


### [187] [ClarifyCodeBench: Evaluating LLMs on Clarifying Ambiguous Requirements for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00711)
*Zheng Fang,Dongming Jin,Yihong dong,Yongmin Li,Kechi Zhang,Zhi Jin,Ge Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了ClarifyCodeBench，一个用于评估大语言模型在代码生成中主动澄清需求歧义能力的新型交互式基准测试。通过对6个最新LLM的系统评估，揭示了三个关键发现：强代码生成能力不等同于有效需求澄清、推理能力提升对识别歧义的增益有限、以及多歧义场景下性能急剧下降。研究强调了AI4SE领域需从静态代码合成转向交互式需求引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成基准测试大多假设输入需求是完美指定的，忽略了现实软件开发中需求经常模糊、不完整或欠指定的问题。LLM虽然擅长一次性代码合成，但主动澄清用户意图的能力尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一评估空白，量化LLM在需求歧义消解方面的交互能力。

Method: 构建了ClarifyCodeBench基准测试，包含从真实编程任务中收集的高质量人工标注数据（包括多种歧义类型、澄清问题及标准答案）。设计了两个量化评估指标：Turn-discounted Key Question Rate（轮次折扣关键问题率）和Optimal Round Adherence（最优轮次遵循度）。对6个最新LLM进行了系统评估。

Result: 三个关键发现：1) 能力解耦——强代码生成性能并不等同于有效的需求澄清能力；2) 推理悖论——增加计算推理虽提升代码正确性，但对识别歧义的改善微乎其微；3) 多歧义天花板——随着歧义密度增加，LLM的澄清性能急剧下降，在处理复杂真实世界规范时表现出显著瓶颈。

Conclusion: 未来AI4SE研究需要从静态代码合成转向交互式需求引导，LLM应具备更强的主动澄清能力以应对现实软件工程中的需求不确定性。

Abstract: Large Language Models have emerged as programming assistants. However, the efficacy of code generation is constrained by the quality of input requirements, which are frequently ambiguous, incomplete, or underspecified. While LLMs excel at one-shot code synthesis, their ability to proactively clarify intent remains underexplored, as a critical trait for robust software engineering. Existing benchmarks largely overlook this interactive bottleneck, assuming perfectly specified prompts that do not reflect the iterative nature of requirement elicitation. To bridge this gap, we introduce ClarifyCodeBench, a novel interactive benchmark for evaluating LLMs' capability in resolving requirement ambiguity. Constructed from real-world programming tasks, ClarifyCodeBench features high-quality manual annotations, including N unique ambiguity types, associated clarification questions, and corresponding ground-truth answers. Furthermore, we formalize two rigorous metrics to assess the interaction quality: Turn-discounted Key Question Rate, which penalizes inefficient questioning, and Optimal Round Adherence, which measures the precision of the elicitation process. We conduct a systematic evaluation of six state-of-the-art LLMs using ClarifyCodeBench. Our empirical results yield three critical insights: 1) Capability Decoupling: Strong code generation performance does not inherently translate to effective requirement clarification; 2) The Reasoning Paradox: While increased computational thinking enhances code correctness, it yields marginal gains in identifying ambiguities; 3) The Multi-ambiguity Ceiling: LLMs' clarification performance degrades sharply as the density of ambiguities increases, revealing a significant bottleneck in handling complex, real-world specifications. Our work underscores the necessity for future AI4SE research to transition from static synthesis to interactive elicitation.

</details>


### [188] [Stochastic Connectivity as the Foundation of a Runtime Model for Microservice Availability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00740)
*Anatoly A. Krasnovsky,Anna Maslovskaya*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机连通性的微服务端点运行时可用性形式化模型，通过服务依赖图、副本映射、状态概率测度和请求成功谓词，将端点可用性量化为微服务韧性的可测量指标。该模型可从分布式追踪和部署元数据重建，支持架构假设场景分析，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行评估。合成实验表明模型在封闭形式基准案例中匹配良好，并揭示了边瓶颈、关联故障等边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的故障注入和混沌实验成本高、操作风险大且难以对每次架构变更重复执行；而分布式追踪和部署元数据虽易于获取，但通常仅提供描述性信息（展示哪些服务交互过），无法揭示端点级别的可用性属性。因此需要一种低成本的、可重复的形式化方法来评估微服务端点可用性。

Method: 提出一种基于随机连通性的形式化运行时可用性模型，核心组件包括：类型化服务依赖图、副本映射、节点和边状态的概率测度、请求特定成功谓词。模型语义将服务副本的计算故障与逻辑依赖的通信故障分离，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟进行分析。模型可从追踪数据和部署制品重建，支持架构假设场景的参数化分析。

Result: 合成充分性研究表明：该模型在封闭形式基准案例中匹配良好（误差在采样范围内）；同时揭示了模型的边界条件，包括边瓶颈、关联故障、缺失追踪数据以及时间相关故障对可用性评估的影响。

Conclusion: 复制不能补偿瓶颈依赖，该模型可作为故障注入的补充或前置分析手段，为微服务韧性评估提供低成本、可重复的形式化方法。

Abstract: Microservice availability is commonly assessed by fault injection and chaos experiments, but such experiments are costly, operationally risky, and difficult to repeat for every architectural change. Distributed tracing and deployment metadata provide cheaper evidence, yet they usually remain descriptive: they show which services interacted, not what endpoint-level availability property follows. This paper proposes a formal runtime availability model based on stochastic connectivity for resilience-oriented analysis of microservice endpoints. It treats endpoint availability under explicit fault scenarios as a measurable facet of microservice resilience, combining a typed service-dependency graph, a replication map, a probability measure over node and edge states, and request-specific success predicates. Its semantics separates computational failures of service replicas from communication failures of logical dependencies, showing that replication cannot compensate for bottleneck dependencies. The model can be reconstructed from traces and deployment artifacts, parameterized for architectural what-if analysis, and analyzed by Monte Carlo simulation before or alongside fault injection. We define the model, its trace-to-model construction, elementary semantic properties, and a synthetic adequacy study. The study matches closed-form oracle cases within sampling error and exposes boundaries caused by edge bottlenecks, correlated failures, missing traces, and time-dependent failures.

</details>


### [189] [Knowledge-Enhanced Agentic Vulnerability Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00820)
*Sicong Cao,Hao Ma,Le Yu,Kangyi Ding,Xiaolei Liu,Terry Yue Zhuo,Bo Wang,Xingwei Lin,Xiaobing Sun,Linzhang Wang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出KeaRepair，一种基于代理的自动化漏洞修复方法，通过多维度漏洞知识库构建、工具增强的推理代理和知识级检索增强补丁生成，在55个C/C++漏洞上达到83.64%的修复率，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化漏洞修复方法难以可靠识别漏洞根因，且未能充分利用历史修复知识指导补丁生成，导致实际效果不佳。

Method: 首先从历史漏洞-补丁对中提取多维度漏洞知识并构建检索知识库；然后采用工具增强的代理进行ReAct风格推理，收集已验证的程序事实用于漏洞诊断；最后基于诊断结果进行知识级检索增强补丁生成，并通过编译、PoC重放和测试套件执行的闭环验证迭代优化补丁。

Result: 在55个可复现的C/C++漏洞上，KeaRepair配合Gemini-3.1-Pro成功修复46个漏洞（修复率83.64%），且修复了6个所有基线方法均无法处理的独特漏洞，并展现出强跨语言泛化能力。

Conclusion: KeaRepair通过将补丁生成建立在已验证的程序事实和高层漏洞知识之上，显著提升了自动化漏洞修复的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Frontier foundation models have changed the math on vulnerability discovery, but the bigger challenge is how the remediation side keeps up. Despite recent progresses in Automated Vulnerability Repair (AVR), current solutions struggle to reliably identify the root causes of vulnerabilities, and insufficiently utilize the prior fix knowledge to guide the patch generation process, thus undermining their effectiveness in practice.
  To address this gap, we propose KeaRepair, a novel agentic AVR approach that grounds patch generation in verified program facts and high-level vulnerability knowledge. Specifically, KeaRepair first extracts multi-dimensional vulnerability knowledge from historical vulnerability-patch pairs from dual complementary views, and constructs dedicated retrieval knowledge bases. It then employs a tool-augmented agent that performs ReAct-style reasoning to collect verified program facts for vulnerability diagnosis. Finally, based on the diagnostic results, KeaRepair performs knowledge-level retrieval-augmented patch generation and iteratively refines patches through a closed-loop validation process involving compilation, PoC replay, and test-suite execution. Experimental results show that KeaRepair significantly outperforms existing AVR approaches on 55 reproducible C/C++ vulnerabilities. When paired with Gemini-3.1-Pro, KeaRepair successfully repairs 46 vulnerabilities, achieving a repair rate of 83.64%. Moreover, KeaRepair fixes six unique vulnerabilities that none of the baselines can address, and further demonstrates strong cross-language generalizability.

</details>


### [190] [Petrify: Petri-net Based Analysis of Concurrency Properties in Java Bytecode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00830)
*Akshatha Shenoy,Carlo A. Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Petrify是一种新颖的自动验证技术，通过将Java字节码程序编码为Petri网，结合模型检查工具LoLA进行并发性质分析，在表达性和实用性之间取得了独特的平衡：Petri网编码精确且简洁，对参数大小不敏感；其原型工具jPetrify支持任何版本的Java及部分Kotlin程序，优于仅支持旧版Java的同类工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动形式化验证技术在表达力/精度与可扩展性/实用性之间存在显著权衡：有的专注于复杂性质的精确验证，有的侧重大规模程序的实用分析，缺乏能够兼顾二者的技术。

Method: 将Java字节码程序的语义编码为Petri网（PN），再利用先进的模型检查工具（如LoLA）对Petri网进行分析，从而验证并发性质。

Result: Petrify的Petri网编码是一种相当精确的程序并发行为编码方式，同时编码简洁，分析对参数大小保持不敏感；原型工具jPetrify可应用于任何版本Java及部分Kotlin程序，适用范围超过同类工具。

Conclusion: Petrify在表达性和实用性之间取得了独特的权衡，当前实验侧重于死锁等基本性质，但其方法可扩展至其他类型的并发分析，是未来工作方向。

Abstract: The landscape of automated formal verification is populated by techniques that make prominently different trade-offs: some focus on expressiveness and precision, supporting the verification of complex properties; others favor scalability and practicality, so that they are applicable to larger programs using different features. This paper presents Petrify, a novel automated verification technique for concurrency properties that achieves a distinctive trade-off. Petrify encodes the semantics of Java bytecode programs into Petri nets (PNs), which can be analyzed by state-of-the-art model checking tools such as LoLA. As our experiments demonstrate, Petrify's approach offers an interesting combination of expressiveness and practicality: PNs are a fairly precise encoding of the concurrent behavior of programs; at the same time, Petrify's PN encoding is succinct, so that its analysis remains quite insensitive to parameter size. Another practical benefit of targeting bytecode is that jPetrify, the prototype tool that implements the Petrify technique, is applicable to programs written in any version of Java and even a subset of Kotlin (another language that compiles to Java bytecode) while other similar tools are limited to older versions of Java. While this paper's experiments focus on analyzing fundamental properties like deadlock, Petrify's approach lends itself to be extended to other kinds of concurrency analysis, which we plan to tackle in future work.

</details>


### [191] [From Registry to Repository: How AI Agent Skills Are Written, Adapted, and Maintained](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00911)
*Haoyu Gao,Jai Lal Lulla,Hong Yi Lin,Sebastian Baltes,Christoph Treude,Mansooreh Zahedi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对AI编码智能体技能（skill）作为软件制品进行大规模实证研究，分析了18,463个公开技能和23,199个个人技能，发现技能复用以一次性复制为主（53%从未修改），定制主要适配本地环境，而行为契约几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体广泛使用技能（skill）作为结构化上下文（SKILL.md），公共注册表已有数万技能，但学界主要将其视为智能体能力而非软件制品，缺乏对技能作为可作者、复用、定制和维护的工程制品的系统理解。

Method: 从skills.sh采集18,463个公开技能，从5,876个GitHub仓库采集23,199个个人技能，识别出3,709个复用链接。使用基于LLM的SWEBOK知识领域分类、180个技能的专题分析和444次修改的定性编码。

Result: 软件构建（Software Construction）是最主导的知识领域；识别出六类内容类别和六类修改主题；复用以近逐字复制为主，53%被采纳后从未修改，本地维护以添加为主；定制主要适配本地环境，演化为积累内联领域知识；行为契约（用户交互、运行时监控、故障恢复）几乎不变。

Conclusion: 维护工作应聚焦于项目特定绑定；注册表和工具支持应使领域知识技能得以整合，减少孤立的再创作。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on skills: structured context bundles, typically a SKILL.md file with a YAML header and Markdown body, loaded on demand for domain knowledge, workflows, and scripts. Public registries such as skills.sh now host tens of thousands of skills, making them an emerging unit of reuse in agent-based software engineering. Yet skills have largely been viewed as agent capabilities rather than software artefacts whose content and evolution shape agent behaviour. We present the first empirical study of AI agent skills as engineered artefacts that are authored, reused, customised and maintained, across public registries and personal-use repositories. We mined 18,463 skills from skills.sh and 23,199 personal-use skills from 5,876 GitHub repositories, identifying 3,709 reuse links. LLM-based classification into SWEBOK knowledge areas (KAs) shows Software Construction dominates alongside a long tail of specialised areas. A thematic analysis of 180 skills identifies six content categories. Qualitative coding of 444 modifications reveals six themes, of which reworking operational specifications and adapting knowledge and resources are the primary target of change. Our findings show that reuse is largely a one-time copy operation: most reused skills remain near-verbatim, 53\% are never modified after adoption, and subsequent local maintenance is overwhelmingly additive. Customisation primarily adapts skills to local environments, whereas evolution accretes new inline domain knowledge. Across both, a stable behavioural contract -- how a skill interacts with users, monitors runtime state, and recovers from failures -- remains almost untouched. These results suggest maintenance effort should focus on project-specific bindings, and that registries and tool support should enable consolidating the domain knowledge skills re-author in isolation.

</details>


### [192] [Delta Debugging in the Absence of Test Oracles Through Metamorphic Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00929)
*Mingyue Jiang,Yongqiang Tian,Tsong Yueh Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DDMT方法，通过将蜕变测试集成到delta调试中，替代传统的测试预言机，使得delta调试能够应用于缺乏测试预言机的程序，从而显著扩展了其适用范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有delta调试技术依赖测试预言机来判断简化后的输入是否保留特定属性，对于预言机缺乏的程序（输出正确性无法直接判断）适用性受限，存在预言机问题。

Method: DDMT重新设计了一种独立于预言机的测试函数，采用蜕变测试技术建立基于蜕变属性的测试函数，替代delta调试中原有的测试函数，从而在不依赖测试预言机的情况下完成属性保留验证。

Result: 在66个测试对象上，涵盖有预言机和缺乏预言机两种场景，分别用不同delta调试方法评估。结果表明DDMT能够增强delta调试的适用性，同时通常能保持或提高约简效果和查询效率，且在适当配置下相比相关方法有性能提升。

Conclusion: DDMT有效解决了delta调试的预言机问题，通过蜕变测试实现了对预言机缺乏程序的delta调试，扩展了其应用范围，且不牺牲效率与效果。

Abstract: Delta debugging provides an automatic way to minimize a program input while preserving a certain property. However, its effectiveness fundamentally relies on the availability of test oracles to determine whether a reduced input still preserves the specific property. Consequently, the oracle problem substantially limits the applicability of existing delta debugging techniques, particularly for oracle-deficient programs where output correctness cannot be directly determined. To address this problem, this paper proposes a novel approach, DDMT, to enhance the applicability of delta debugging, especially facilitating its application to oracle-deficient programs. Our key insight is to redesign an oracle-independent test function and incorporate it into the reduction procedure of delta debugging such that the property-preservation validation can be accomplished without requiring a test oracle. To this end, DDMT employs the technique of metamorphic testing, which is a property-based and oracle-independent testing method. It establishes a metamorphic testing-based test function, using it as a replacement for the original test function adopted by delta debugging. The experiments evaluate DDMT on 66 subjects across both oracle-available and oracle-deficient scenarios, with different delta debugging approaches. The results positively confirm that DDMT can enhance the applicability of delta debugging while often preserving or improving reduction effectiveness and query efficiency. Furthermore, compared to the relevant delta debugging approaches, DDMT is also able to achieve performance improvements with proper configurations.

</details>


### [193] [Leveraging LLM-Based Agentic Systems to Generate Quantum Applications for Test Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00939)
*Ming Tao,Yuechen Li,Tao Yue,Man Zhang,Aitor Arrieta Marcos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: QPipe是一个基于LLM的多智能体架构，能够将自然语言需求自主转化为可执行的量子应用工作流，在20个真实基准测试中实现100%编译成功率和96.7%执行成功率，且生成的应用在多数情况下优于遗传算法基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自然语言任务级需求转化为可执行量子应用需要大量量子和编程专业知识，缺乏自动化工具来降低这一门槛。

Method: 提出QPipe架构，基于LLM构建多智能体系统，包含需求解析、问题形式化、代码生成、审查、执行和验证六个专门化智能体，协同完成从NL需求到量子应用工作流的自动转化。

Result: 在20个NL需求上测试，代码编译率100%，应用执行与结果组合率96.7%，平均生成时间260.1秒，平均消耗1.89M tokens；成功执行的量子应用在多数情况下优于离线遗传算法基线；消融实验表明代码生成技能、任务知识、审查反馈和多智能体分解是关键。

Conclusion: 智能体协同机制能够支持从真实世界基准测试中生成可执行的量子应用，用于解决测试优化问题。

Abstract: Quantum computing is increasingly explored for software engineering (SE) optimization, but translating natural-language (NL) task-level requirements into executable quantum applications still demands substantial quantum and programming expertise. We present QPipe, a large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent architecture that autonomously turns NL requirements into traceable quantum-application workflows through specialized agents for requirement parsing, formulation, code generation, review, execution, and verification. We evaluate QPipe on 20 NL requirements, each associated with a real-world benchmark and a test-optimization problem. QPipe successfully completes the key stages of quantum-application generation across requirements, achieving average rates of 100% for code compilation and 96.7% for application execution and final-result combination, with average generation costs of 260.1 seconds and 1.89M tokens per requirement. Among the generated quantum applications that execute successfully, the returned solutions outperform the offline genetic algorithm baseline in most cases. Ablation results further show that QPipe's advantage depends on retaining code-generation skills, task knowledge, review feedback, and multi-agent decomposition. These results indicate that agentic coordination can support generation of executable quantum applications for tackling test optimization problems from real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [194] [SWE-Doctor: Guiding Software Engineering Agents with Runtime Diagnosis from Multi-Faceted Bug Reproduction Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00990)
*Yaoqi Guo,Yang Liu,Jie M. Zhang,Yun Ma,Yiling Lou,Zhenpeng Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-Doctor，一种利用多维度BRT执行产生的运行时诊断信息来引导补丁生成的软件问题解决智能体。实验表明，该方法在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上分别达到75.7%和59.4%的平均解决率，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的软件工程智能体常使用Bug复现测试(BRT)进行补丁验证，但BRT能否辅助补丁生成阶段尚不明确。初步研究发现，直接使用BRT引导补丁生成效果不佳：fail-to-fail BRT会误导智能体，fail-to-pass BRT也收益有限或为负。分析发现根本原因在于fail-to-pass BRT仅覆盖问题的一种表现导致部分补丁，而fail-to-fail BRT作为补丁生成目标不可靠。

Method: SWE-Doctor包含三阶段流程：1) 根据问题报告中描述的不同行为需求生成多维度BRT；2) 执行并调试这些BRT，构建运行时诊断记录；3) 利用诊断结果结合BRT生成过程中推断的定位信息引导补丁生成，减少部分补丁问题。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified和SWE-bench Pro上，使用5种LLM后端进行评估。SWE-Doctor在所有10个LLM-基准组合中一致优于现有智能体，平均解决率在SWE-bench Verified上达75.7%，在SWE-bench Pro上达59.4%。在更具挑战性的SWE-bench Pro上，相比基线智能体提升8.0-8.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过运行时诊断而非直接使用BRT作为生成目标，能够有效利用BRT辅助补丁生成，避免部分补丁和误导问题。多维度BRT的执行诊断提供了更全面的问题理解，显著提升了LLM智能体在软件缺陷修复任务上的性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based software engineering agents are increasingly developed to resolve software issues by generating patches from issue reports and code repositories. Bug reproduction tests (BRTs) are an important building block for such agents and have been shown useful for patch validation. However, it remains unclear whether BRTs can also help the more central stage of patch generation. We first conduct a preliminary study and find that directly using advanced BRT generators to guide patch generation is not beneficial: fail-to-fail BRTs can mislead agents, while even fail-to-pass BRTs bring limited or negative gains. Our analysis reveals two reasons: fail-to-pass BRTs may cover only one manifestation of the reported issue, leading to partial patches, whereas fail-to-fail BRTs are unreliable as direct patch-generation targets. Motivated by these insights, we propose SWE-Doctor, a software issue resolution agent that guides patch generation with runtime diagnoses derived from multi-faceted BRT executions. SWE-Doctor first generates multi-faceted BRTs for different behavioral requirements stated in the issue, then executes and debugs these BRTs to construct runtime-grounded diagnosis records, and finally uses the diagnoses together with localization information inferred during BRT generation to guide patch generation and reduce partial patches. We evaluate SWE-Doctor on Python bug-fixing issues from the widely adopted SWE-bench Verified and SWE-bench Pro across five LLM backends. SWE-Doctor consistently outperforms existing agents across all 10 LLM-benchmark combinations, achieving average resolution rates of 75.7% on SWE-bench Verified and 59.4% on SWE-bench Pro. In particular, on the more challenging SWE-bench Pro, SWE-Doctor improves the average resolution rate by 8.0-8.9 percentage points over the baseline agents.

</details>


### [195] [Identifying Effective Program Comprehension Strategies through Gaze Transitions over Syntactic Elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01042)
*Kyogo Horikawa,Hidetake Uwano,Haruhiko Yoshioka*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究将眼动追踪数据转换为抽象语法树节点间的转换，分析正确与错误理解任务组之间的注视比例和视线转移模式差异，发现成功参与者表现出更系统化的语法元素间转换模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统眼动研究依赖基于屏幕坐标的测量方法，而新兴的基于语法的分析方法中，任务正确性与特定语法元素眼动特征之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 将眼动追踪数据转换为抽象语法树节点间的转换，分析注视比例和视线转移模式，比较正确组与错误组的差异。

Result: 正确组与错误组在视线转移模式上存在显著差异，成功参与者表现出更系统化的语法元素间转换。

Conclusion: 成功参与者采用了结构化阅读策略，表现为更系统化的语法元素间视线转移模式。

Abstract: Program comprehension is a central research topic in software engineering, focusing on how developers understand a program's structure, behavior, and intent. Eye-tracking studies have traditionally relied on display-based measurements, where gaze positions are represented as screen coordinates. However, syntax-based analyses have recently emerged. Prior work proposed methods to convert eye movements into transitions between nodes in an abstract syntax tree, but the relationship between task correctness and eye-movement features for specific syntactic elements remains unclear. This study converts eye-tracking data into transitions between syntactic nodes and analyzes fixation proportions and gaze transition patterns. We investigate the relationship between these patterns and task correctness, comparing correct and incorrect groups. Our results reveal distinct differences in gaze transition patterns between the two groups. In particular, successful participants exhibit more systematic transitions across syntactic elements, suggesting the use of structured reading strategies.

</details>


### [196] [AutoRestTest at the SBFT 2026 Tool Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01063)
*Tyler Stennett,Myeongsoo Kim,Saurabh Sinha,Alessandro Orso*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoRestTest 结合语义属性依赖图、多智能体强化学习和大型语言模型，在 SBFT 2026 REST League 的11个API上获得三项评估指标第一名，平均每API检测到67.09个独特服务器错误并成功处理17.27个操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑盒REST API测试面临输入空间大和操作间依赖复杂两大挑战，现有方法难以高效探索大型API输入空间。

Method: 提出AutoRestTest方法，结合三种技术：语义属性依赖图（SPDG）建模操作间依赖关系、多智能体强化学习协同探索、大语言模型辅助生成智能测试用例。

Result: 在SBFT 2026 REST League竞赛中，对11个API（317个操作，平均每API约29个操作）进行测试，在故障检测、整体效率和整体有效性三项评估中均排名第一。平均每API在1小时测试预算内发现67.09个独特服务器错误，成功处理17.27个操作。

Conclusion: AutoRestTest通过语义依赖图、多智能体强化学习和LLM的组合策略，有效解决了大规模黑盒REST API测试难题，在标准化基准测试中取得了最优性能。

Abstract: Large input spaces and complex inter-operation dependencies make black-box REST API testing challenging. AutoRestTest combines a Semantic Property Dependency Graph, multi-agent reinforcement learning, and large language models to intelligently explore large API input spaces. In the SBFT 2026 REST League, AutoRestTest ranked first in all three evaluation categories -- fault detection, overall efficiency, and overall effectiveness -- on 11 APIs (317 operations, approximately 29 per API), averaging 67.09 unique server errors and 17.27 successfully processed operations per API under a one-hour testing budget.

</details>


### [197] [Cheap Code, Costly Judgment: A Case Study on Governable Agentic Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01087)
*James C. Davis,Paschal C. Amusuo,Tanmay Singla,Berk Çakar,Kirsten A. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过12周的一手案例研究，本文提出"治理转换"过程模型，解释专家工程师如何将AI代理的高效实现转化为可治理的软件工程实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI将软件工程从稀缺实现努力转向充裕的低成本代码生产，这改变了核心工程问题：不是AI能否生成有用代码，而是工程师如何组织架构、工具、证据和反馈循环，使AI介导的开发保持可检查、可纠正和可维护。

Method: 第一人称案例研究：一名专家软件工程师使用前沿AI编码代理进行12周开发，构建文档可访问性修复系统。实证记录包括88条现场笔记、420 KLOC生产代码、1.16 MLOC测试/文档/代理工具。

Result: 提出"治理转换"中程理论的过程模型，解释代理实现速度如何暴露重复的结构性失败类型，以及工程判断如何将这些失败转换为持久的治理机制。

Conclusion: 与从已知义务衍生控制的现有治理模型不同，治理转换解释了控制如何从仅在代理工作中可见的失败中被发现。模型可用于生成可检验预测，并为软件工程研究与实践提供启示。

Abstract: Generative AI is shifting software engineering from a practice organized around scarce implementation effort toward one organized around abundant, low-cost code production. This shift changes the central engineering problem: not whether AI can generate useful code, but how engineers organize architectures, tools, evidence, and feedback loops so that AI-mediated development remains inspectable, correctable, and maintainable.
  We study this problem through a first-person case study: a 12-week development effort in which a single expert software engineer used frontier AI coding agents to build a document accessibility remediation system. The empirical record comprises 88 contemporaneous field notes, 420 KLOC of production code, and 1.16 MLOC of tests, lints, supporting documentation, and agent tooling. From this record, we develop a candidate middle-range theory of governance conversion, expressed as a process model explaining how high-velocity agentic implementation becomes governable. The model explains how agentic implementation velocity surfaces recurring structural failure classes, and how engineering judgment sustains velocity by converting those failures into durable governance mechanisms. In contrast to existing governance models that derive controls from known obligations, governance conversion explains how controls are discovered from failures that become visible only during agentic work. We use our model to make testable predictions and to describe implications for software engineering research and practice.

</details>


### [198] [A Model-based Testing Technique for Amazon Lex Task-based Chatbots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01094)
*Diego Clerissi,Alessandro Vasina,Leonardo Mariani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LexTester，一种针对Amazon Lex聊天机器人的自动化模型测试技术，通过构建对话图并生成可执行测试用例，在五个真实聊天机器人上的评估表明，该方法在测试复杂度、对话元素覆盖率和故障检测效率上均显著优于现有工具Botium。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的任务型聊天机器人质量评估技术存在测试场景简单和预言脆弱等局限性，迫切需要更有效的自动化测试方法。

Method: LexTester采用基于模型的测试方法：首先探索待测聊天机器人的对话空间，生成包含所有可能交互的对话图（Dialog Graph），然后根据不同的覆盖策略从中生成可执行的测试套件。

Result: 在五个Amazon Lex聊天机器人上，LexTester在所有测试对象上均优于Botium：生成测试数量更多、测试复杂度近乎翻倍、对话元素覆盖率达83-95%、故障检测效果提升最多达4倍，且时间成本相当。

Conclusion: LexTester是一种有效的Amazon Lex聊天机器人自动化测试技术，在多个评估指标上显著优于现有工具，为任务型聊天机器人的质量保障提供了更优方案。

Abstract: Task-based chatbots are nowadays widely adopted software systems, usually integrated into real-world applications and communication channels, designed to assist users in completing tasks through conversational interfaces. Like any other software, even chatbots are prone to bugs. Despite their increasing pervasiveness in everyday activities, existing techniques for assessing their quality still exhibit several limitations, such as the simplicity of generated test scenarios and oracle weaknesses.
  In this paper, we present LexTester, an automated model-based testing technique for Amazon Lex chatbots. The technique explores the conversational space of the chatbot under test to generate a Dialog Graph of all possible interactions, from which an executable test suite is generated according to different coverage strategies. LexTester was evaluated against the state-of-the-practice chatbot testing tool Botium on five Amazon Lex chatbots, consistently outperforming it in all subjects, generating more tests with nearly double complexity, achieving overall 83-95% coverage of conversational elements, and improving fault detection effectiveness by up to four times at comparable time costs.

</details>


### [199] [SAGE: Structured Agentic Graph Editing for Software Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01102)
*Tyler Sivertsen,Neal Singh,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SAGE是一个基于浏览器的工具，支持通过自然语言提示对Draw.io和Mermaid风格的工程图表进行编辑，它将图表映射为可编辑的图表示，将自然语言请求转化为结构化的编辑意图，并执行图操作步骤，同时支持验证修复和版本管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件图表通过自然语言界面编辑困难，因为自然语言表达必须同时保持视觉布局、可编辑结构和语义关系，现有工具缺乏对这类友好交互的支持。

Method: 将图表映射为可编辑图表示；将自然语言请求翻译为结构化编辑意图；分析意图为图导向的操作步骤；验证并修复Draw.io XML问题；存储成功结果为可恢复的版本化工件。系统将结构化状态管理与模型驱动解释分离，部分提示引导的XML编辑仍为模型辅助。同时支持直接画布编辑和基于掩码的图像编辑工作流。

Result: 通过单元测试和Kubernetes架构案例研究进行评估，衡量结构有效性、编辑成功率、无关元素保留情况以及失败原因。

Conclusion: SAGE设计了结构化状态管理与模型驱动解释相分离的架构，实现了通过自然语言提示对工程图表进行编辑，为图表编辑的人机交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Software diagrams are difficult to edit through human-friendly interfaces because edits expressed in natural language must still preserve visual layout, editable structure, and semantic relationships. As a step forward, we present SAGE, a browser-based tool for prompt-guided editing of Draw.io and Mermaid-style engineering diagrams. The tool maps diagrams into an editable graph representation, translates natural language requests into structured edit intents, analyzes those intents into graph-oriented operation steps, validates and repairs common Draw.io XML issues, and stores successful results as recoverable versioned artifacts. This design separates structured state management from model-driven interpretation, while acknowledging that some prompt-guided XML edits remain model-assisted. The tool also supports direct canvas editing and a secondary mask-based image-editing workflow. We evaluate the system using unit tests and a Kubernetes architecture case study, measuring structural validity, edit success, preservation of unrelated elements, and failure causes.

</details>


### [200] [Skills Are Not Islands: Measuring Dependency and Risk in Agent Skill Supply Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01136)
*Changguo Jia,Tianqi Zhao,Runzhi He,Minghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent技能供应链（ASSCs）概念，设计SkillDepAnalyzer工具从自然语言中提取技能依赖关系，在SKILL-DEP基准上优于基线方法，并对143万+技能进行大规模分析，揭示了四种结构模式和安全信号，最终提出依赖管理建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent技能包在规模增长后成为携带依赖的工件，但其身份、版本和来源信息不透明，导致依赖重复和不一致安装，而现有依赖管理尚未解决这一问题。

Method: 借鉴软件物料清单（SBOM）思想，设计SkillDepAnalyzer工具，通过捕获自然语言依赖证据，将技能建模为携带依赖的工件，构建混合技能-包-服务的依赖图（ASSCs）。

Result: 在SKILL-DEP基准上准确恢复技能元数据和依赖图，大幅优于LLM基线和传统SBOM工具；对143万+技能的分析揭示了四种结构模式，并发现仅检查技能本身会遗漏依赖中隐藏的安全信号，且识别出已知恶意技能。

Conclusion: 建议引入类型化依赖清单、一等公民的依赖簇管理、风险告警审计命令和类似lockfile的记录来改进技能依赖管理。

Abstract: Agent skills package reusable operational knowledge for Large Language Model (LLM) agents, yet as they grow in scope, they become dependency-bearing artifacts whose identities, versions, and provenance remain implicit. This opacity already causes duplicated dependencies and inconsistent installations, exposing a gap that dependency management has yet to close. We introduce Agent Skill Supply Chains (ASSCs) to characterize mixed skill-package-service dependency graphs and help close this gap. Borrowing from Software Bill of Materials (SBOMs), we design SkillDepAnalyzer to capture natural-language dependency evidence and model skills as dependency-bearing artifacts. On the SKILL-DEP benchmark, SkillDepAnalyzer recovers skill metadata and dependency graphs accurately and comprehensively, substantially outperforming an LLM-based baseline and package-centric SBOM tools. Applying SkillDepAnalyzer to over 1.43 million skills, we obtain ASSCs and explore their structural diversity and security signals. We find four structural patterns: skill metadata is activation-ready but governance-poor; dependency graphs span skill, package, and service dependencies with concentrated reuse; recursive skill reuse expands dependency graphs and creates hidden package inventory; and skill dependency clusters form around related workflows. We also find that inspecting a skill alone misses security-relevant signals hiding in its dependencies. By analyzing ASSCs, we identify and report known malicious skills persisting in ASSCs to their developers. Based on these findings, we recommend typed dependency manifests, first-class dependency-cluster management, risk-warning audit commands for skill infrastructure maintainers, and lockfile-like records for skill developers.

</details>


### [201] [Are Performance-Optimization Benchmarks Reliably Measuring Coding Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01211)
*Zhi Chen,Zhensu Sun,Yuling Shi,David Lo,Lingxiao Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了三个仓库级性能优化基准（GSO、SWE-Perf、SWE-fficiency），发现其排行榜分数存在三大问题：参考补丁在跨机器回放时大量失效（尤其是SWE-Perf仅有7.9%任务满足规则）；评分规则导致排名不一致（28对比较中9对存在分歧）；大多数任务（85.3%）已被至少一个公开提交解决，掩盖了剩余性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级性能优化基准的排行榜分数被广泛用作编码智能体进展的证据，但这些分数可能混淆了运行时不稳定、基准特定评分规则以及任务已被解决的程度，现有评估缺乏对这些混淆因素的系统性审计。

Method: 对GSO、SWE-Perf和SWE-fficiency三个基准进行审计：1）在四种Google Cloud机器上回放740个任务的官方参考补丁，检验跨机器有效性；2）比较8个公开提交在GSO和SWE-fficiency上的排名一致性；3）分析10个公开提交在450个可回放任务上的表现分布。

Result: 参考补丁跨机器有效比例：GSO 38.2%（39/102）、SWE-Perf 7.9%（11/140）、SWE-fficiency 82.5%（411/498）。SWE-Perf因参考补丁产生接近于零的运行时变化而尤其脆弱。公开提交排名中，28对两两比较有9对不一致；SWE-fficiency评分规则对最差的10个任务赋予58.5%-82.8%的过高权重。85.3%的可回放任务至少有一个公开提交匹配或超越参考补丁，99.8%的任务超越未优化基线。

Conclusion: 排行榜分数不能完全反映编码智能体的真实进展，需要识别信号更可靠的任务、量化每个任务的分数贡献，并揭露被聚合排名掩盖的剩余性能差距。

Abstract: Repository-level performance-optimization benchmarks such as GSO, SWE-Perf and SWE-fficiency evaluate coding agents by applying patches to real repositories and comparing runtime against unoptimized baselines and official reference patches. Their leaderboard scores are increasingly used as evidence of coding-agent progress, but those scores can conflate runtime instability, benchmark-specific scoring rules, and how many tasks are already solved by at least one public submission. We audit these issues across the three benchmarks. First, we replay the official reference patches for 740 code optimization tasks across four common types of Google Cloud machines. Most benchmark tasks can be replayed, but their reference patches satisfy the original benchmark validity rules in every cross-machine replay for only 39/102 GSO tasks, 11/140 SWE-Perf tasks, and 411/498 SWE-fficiency tasks; SWE-Perf is especially fragile because many reference patches produce close-to-zero runtime changes. Second, we show that public submission rankings depend strongly on the benchmark scoring rule. Among eight public submissions shared by GSO and SWE-fficiency, the official rankings disagree on 9 of 28 pairwise submission comparisons, and SWE-fficiency's leaderboard scoring rule assigns the worst ten tasks overly high score weights of 58.5%-82.8%. Third, looking across 10 public submissions for each task, we find that at least one submission matches or beats the reference patch on 85.3% (384/450) of replay-valid GSO and SWE-fficiency tasks, and beats the unoptimized base code on 99.8% (449/450). Our study complements leaderboard scores by identifying tasks with more reliable performance signals, quantifying per-task score contributions, and exposing the remaining performance gaps that are hidden by aggregate rankings.

</details>


### [202] [RepoRescue: An Empirical Study of LLM Agents on Whole-Repository Compatibility Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01213)
*Zhihao Lin,Mingyi Zhou,Zhensu Sun,Yizhuo Yang,Renyu Yang,David Lo,Li Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出兼容性救援任务，研究LLM代理能否使因环境变化而失效的旧代码仓库恢复运行。基于RepoRescue基准（193个Python+122个Java仓库），评估了多个代理系统，发现Kimi在运行时阻断下能救援41.5%的仓库，系统联合可达62.7%，但跨文件协调仍是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源库广泛复用但兼容性维护成本高昂，维护者离开后仓库会因运行时和依赖演变而失效。现有工作聚焦于bug修复，但尚未系统研究如何在环境漂移后使仓库恢复工作。

Method: 构建RepoRescue基准，包含193个Python和122个Java仓库，每个仓库均在原环境中通过测试、现代化后失败。代理仅获得仓库和失败环境，需诊断故障、定位代码并生成源码级补丁。评估包括全补丁通过率、仅源码修复率、运行时阻断测试编辑、实际场景验证，并引入推理标签。

Result: Claude Code系统有时会编辑失败测试；运行时阻断下Kimi救援41.5%的仓库；系统互补联合达62.7%，超过最佳单系统10.9个百分点。跨文件协调是主要难点：14个需全代码库协调的仓库中GPT-5.2通过全部，而Claude Code系统最多通过2个。34个未维护Python候选仓库中，22个在实际场景中有效，12个通过bug-hunt。

Conclusion: 兼容性救援不同于bug修复，现有代理系统在此任务上表现有限但互补。跨文件协调是核心瓶颈。测试通过仅是初步信号，需结合实际验证和bug-hunt全面评估。

Abstract: Open-source libraries and tools are widely reused, but compatibility maintenance is expensive. Once maintainers leave, useful repositories can stop working as runtimes and dependencies evolve. We study whether LLM agents can adapt old repositories to modern environments, a task we call compatibility rescue. Unlike bug repair, compatibility rescue starts from a repository that worked in its original environment but fails after ecosystem drift. RepoRescue gives agents only the repository and its failing modern environment; the agent must diagnose the failure, locate affected code, and produce a source-code rescue that restores the historical test suite. We build RepoRescue from 193 Python and 122 Java repositories, each verified to pass historically and fail after modernization. We evaluate five deployed agent systems on Python and three on Java. Beyond full-patch pass rate, we rerun patches after removing test-file edits to measure source-only repair, add a runtime-enforced regime that blocks test edits, and validate practical use for repositories whose suites pass after rescue. We find that Claude Code systems sometimes edit failing tests even when prompted not to; with runtime blocking, Kimi still rescues 41.5% of repositories. Systems are complementary: their union reaches 62.7%, exceeding the best single system by 10.9 points. Difficulty concentrates in cross-file coordination: on 14 repositories requiring coordinated whole-codebase changes, GPT-5.2 through Codex passes all 14, while every Claude Code system passes at most two. Finally, a passing suite is only an initial signal: among 34 unmaintained Python candidates whose suites pass after rescue, 22 work in realistic scenarios and 12 pass bug-hunt with patches that address the compatibility failure. RepoRescue benchmarks compatibility rescue with source-only auditing, runtime enforcement, practical validation, and reasoning labels.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Comparing the Emotional Impact of Thematic Versus Episodic Framing in Visualization Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00103)
*Poorna Talkad Sukumar,Maurizio Porfiri,Oded Nov*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过一项800人的预注册在线实验，研究了数据可视化中文本框架（情节性框架vs主题性框架）对观众情绪反应的影响。结果表明，情节性框架比主题性框架引发更强烈的负面情绪，而添加标注不影响情绪；框架虽未直接影响政策态度，但负面情绪的中介效应显著促进了对枪支管控的支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化中的文本框架已知会影响理解、回忆和偏见感知，但其对观众情绪反应的影响尚未得到充分研究。作者希望弥合这一研究空白，探究不同框架策略如何通过情绪路径影响观众的态度。

Method: 预注册的组间在线实验（N=800），参与者观看相同的美国大规模枪击数据可视化，仅改变文本框架：主题性标题、主题性标题+标注、情节性标题+标注。测量情绪效价和政策态度，并进行中介分析。

Result: 情节性框架引发显著更负面的情绪效价；添加标注未改变情绪影响；框架对政策态度无直接显著影响，但负面情绪在情节性框架与枪支管控支持度之间起显著中介作用。

Conclusion: 情绪是文本框架塑造数据可视化响应中一个关键但被忽视的维度，框架通过情绪间接影响政策态度。

Abstract: Although textual framing in data visualizations is known to influence comprehension, recall, and perceptions of bias, its effects on viewers' emotional responses remain underexplored. Drawing on two widely studied framing strategies in political communication, we examine how episodic framing (foregrounding a specific event) versus thematic framing (foregrounding broader trends) affects emotional and attitudinal responses to visualizations. We conducted a preregistered, between-subjects online experiment (N = 800) in which participants viewed identical visualizations of U.S. mass shooting data that varied only in textual framing: a thematic title, a thematic title with annotation, or an episodic title paired with the same annotation. Results show that episodic framing elicited significantly more negative emotional valence than both thematic conditions. In contrast, adding an annotation to a thematic title did not alter emotional impact. While framing did not significantly affect policy attitudes, mediation analysis revealed a significant indirect effect: increased negative emotion under episodic framing predicted greater support for gun control. These findings position emotion as a critical, yet underexamined, dimension of how textual framing shapes responses to data visualizations.

</details>


### [204] [May (A)I Beautify Your Visualization? Expert Judgments of Acceptable Aesthetic Alterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00239)
*Kalina Borkiewicz,Jixian Li,Joshua A. Levine,Katherine E. Isaacs*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过95位专家调查发现，3D自然现象可视化中的修改（包括人类和AI执行）的可接受性主要由修改的含义决定，而非修改层面（呈现层或数据层）。AI生成的修改一致被认为比相同的人类修改可接受性更低，但相对排序保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI工具使3D可视化设计的修改更加便捷，理解这些修改对数据感知的影响及其权衡变得日益重要。现有研究缺乏对可视化修改可接受性的系统性理解，尤其是人类与AI修改之间的差异。

Method: 对95名可视化研究者、从业者和领域科学家进行专家调查，评估15种不同类型的修改（涵盖呈现层调整和数据层修改），并比较人类执行与AI执行这两种来源的差异。

Result: 修改的可接受性差异主要由修改的含义驱动，而非操作层面（呈现层或数据层）。某些修改无论由人类还是AI执行，其相对可接受性排序保持一致，但AI生成的修改一致被评价为比相同的人类修改更不可接受。

Conclusion: 可视化修改中存在"更可允许"与"更敏感"的区分，建议设计师和AI辅助可视化工具应纳入反映这些差异的约束和护栏机制。

Abstract: In 3D visualizations of natural phenomena, improving aesthetics can provide measurable benefits, but often involves transformations that affect how the data is perceived. As a growing range of tools - including AI-based methods - make visual design and modification more accessible, it is increasingly important to understand trade offs and concerns when making these changes. We conducted an expert survey (N=95) with visualization researchers, practitioners, and domain scientists, investigating reactions to fifteen alterations spanning presentation-level adjustments (e.g., lighting, camera position) and data-level modifications (e.g., removing errors, filling gaps), applied by both humans and AI systems. Results show differences in perceived acceptability are driven by the transformation's meaning, regardless of whether it operates at the presentation or data level. Additionally, certain modifications were consistently judged as more permissible than others regardless of human or AI authorship. While this relative ordering remains largely stable, AI-generated transformations are consistently rated as less acceptable than identical human-produced changes. These results reveal a distinction between more permissible and more sensitive alterations, and suggest the need for both designers and AI-assisted visualization tools to incorporate constraints and guardrails that reflect these differences.

</details>


### [205] [Child Safety in Generative AI: An Expert-Guided and Incident-Grounded Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00395)
*Haein Kong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对儿童安全风险的生成式AI评估框架，该框架整合专家指导的风险因素和真实AI事故数据。将其应用于教育领域评估三个Llama Guard模型，发现这些模型难以识别教育相关的不安全用户提示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估框架主要针对一般用户群体，忽视了儿童和青少年特有的风险，因此需要建立专门针对儿童安全的风险评估框架。

Method: 提出一个评估框架，从专家指南和AI事故数据库中识别危害类别，并据此构建合成测试集用于模型评估。将该框架应用于教育领域，评估三个Llama Guard模型检测不安全用户提示的能力。

Result: 当前Llama Guard模型在识别教育相关的不安全用户提示方面表现不佳。

Conclusion: 未来工作可将评估扩展到更多风险类别，并在整个评估流程中纳入领域专家。

Abstract: As generative AI is increasingly used by children and adolescents, there is a growing need for risk evaluation frameworks that account for child-specific harms. However, most existing safety evaluation frameworks focus on general user populations, often overlooking risks unique to younger users. To address this gap, we propose an evaluation framework that integrates expert-guided risk factors with real-world AI incident data for child safety. The framework identifies hazard categories from expert guidelines and AI incident databases and uses this information to construct a synthetic test set for model evaluation. Particularly, we apply the framework to the education domain and evaluate three Llama Guard models on their ability to detect unsafe user prompts. Our results show that current Llama Guard models struggle to identify education-related unsafe user prompts. We conclude by discussing how future work can extend the evaluation to additional risk categories and incorporate domain experts throughout the evaluation pipeline.

</details>


### [206] [A Penny for Your Prompts: Experiments Detecting and Mitigating LLM Usage by Survey Respondents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00403)
*Zane Xu,Nathan Malkin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究量化了众包平台参与者使用LLM填写问卷的普遍性（从Prolific的不到10%到MTurk的超过80%），并探讨了检测与缓解措施，发现记录击键数据和分析指令问题可有效筛查AI辅助回答，但缓解措施未必提升数据质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台参与者越来越多地使用LLM辅助填写问卷，这可能破坏收集数据的有效性，亟需量化这一行为的普遍性并探索检测与预防方法。

Method: 通过一系列调查（N=250），操控平台选择、问卷长度、禁止使用AI的请求以及禁用复制粘贴功能等条件，分析LLM辅助回答的特征，并进行检测实验。

Result: LLM辅助回答的出现频率在不同平台上差异显著（Prolific<10%，MTurk>80%）；缓解措施减少了LLM使用但未必提升数据质量；未观察到浏览器使用代理，但自建检测实验可行。

Conclusion: 建议研究者主动通过记录和分析击键数据、设计针对AI的指令和问题来筛查LLM使用，以保障数据质量。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used by participants on crowdsourcing platforms when responding to surveys, potentially undermining the validity of collected data. Our study aims to quantify the prevalence of this behavior and investigate methods to detect and prevent it. In a series of surveys (N = 250), we examined conditions such as platform choice, survey length, requests not to use AI, and disabling copy-paste functionality. We were able to identify distinct characteristics of LLM-assisted responses and found that their frequency varied widely, from under 10% on Prolific to over 80% on Mechanical Turk. Mitigation measures reduced LLM usage but did not necessarily improve data quality. No participants employed browser-use agents at the time of our survey, but we report on our own detection experiments. We recommend that researchers actively screen survey responses for LLM usage by recording and analyzing keystroke data and crafting instructions and questions aimed at AI.

</details>


### [207] [A Simple Solution to Improving Human Supervision of Algorithms: Evidence from Smart Vending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00420)
*Minda Zhao,Brian Rongqing Han,Xin Chen,Tao Zhu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过随机实地实验（553名员工，59,000+台机器）提出了一种**受限覆盖策略**：限制每个决策周期内的人工覆盖次数，使工人能够优先选择高价值的覆盖干预。相比自由覆盖（库存降1.95%但销售额降1.19%），受限覆盖在**不损害销售额**的前提下将库存降低1.28%，且效果在经验丰富的工人、高激励SKU和成长期SKU上更显著。模拟个性化政策可进一步提升销售额概率9.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在运营决策（如库存补货）中日益普及，但完全赋予人类覆盖权可能因人类偏见和噪声降低性能，而完全禁止则可能忽略有价值的私有信息。因此，关键问题是：**如何设计覆盖权结构以改善人类对自主AI的监督？**

Method: 提出**受限覆盖政策**：限制每个决策时段内可进行的覆盖次数，迫使工人进行选择性过滤，优先干预高价值决策。在中国一家大型智能售货机零售商（管理59,000+台机器、4,000+个SKU）开展随机实地实验，553名工人被分配至三组：无覆盖、自由覆盖、每台机器最多两次向下覆盖。通过局部平均处理效应（LATE）分析工人选择的SKU质量。

Result: 自由覆盖使库存降低1.95%但销售额降低1.19%；受限覆盖使库存降低1.28%且**不影响销售额**，因为工人选择了更好的SKU进行覆盖（LATE证实）。效果在经验丰富的工人、高激励SKU和成长期SKU上最大。模拟个性化政策进一步使销售额概率提升9.1%。

Conclusion: 学术贡献：提供关于人类监督AI中自由裁量权设计的因果效应新见解，强调**选择性过滤**机制可提升决策质量。管理启示：提出一种可扩展、低成本的策略，适用于零售、物流和资源规划等运营场景，在不牺牲销售额的前提下减少过剩库存，同时利用人类私有信息，且无需算法重新设计、信息定制或额外培训。

Abstract: Organizations increasingly deploy autonomous artificial intelligence (AI) systems for operational decisions, such as inventory replenishment. Yet fully granting override rights can degrade performance due to human bias and noise, while prohibiting them may overlook valuable private information. This raises a key question: How should override rights be structured to improve human supervision of autonomous AI? Methodology/results: We propose a constrained override policy that limits overrides per decision episode to enable selective filtering that prioritizes high-value overrides. We tested it through a randomized field experiment with 553 workers at a major Chinese smart vending machine retailer that manages more than 59,000 machines and 4,000 SKUs. Workers were assigned to no overrides, free overrides, or a two-per-machine limit on downward overrides. Free overrides reduce inventory by 1.95% but also cut sales by 1.19%. Constrained overrides reduce inventory by 1.28% without harming sales, as workers select better SKUs to override, confirmed via local average treatment effects. Gains are largest for experienced workers, high-incentive SKUs, and growth-stage SKUs. A simulated personalized policy further increases sales probability by 9.1%. Managerial implications: Academics gain novel insights from the causal effects of discretion design in human-supervised AI, emphasizing selective filtering to enhance decision quality. Managers can benefit from a scalable, low-cost policy for operations such as retail, logistics, and resource planning, reducing excess inventory without sales loss while harnessing private human information, with no need for algorithmic redesign, information customization, or additional training.

</details>


### [208] [Gaze-Informed Proactive AI Assistance for Children's Picture Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00445)
*Zekun Wu,Man Su,Huiyong Li,Tomohiro Nagashima,Anna Maria Feit*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Ollie，一个基于注视信息（gaze）的主动式AI助手，通过检测儿童在图画探索中的视觉注意力，由LLM生成相关区域的叙事描述。实验表明，相比随机辅助，注视驱动的辅助能更长时间维持儿童注意力，更有效地引导其关注相关区域，获得了儿童、家长和教师的积极反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动式LLM辅助研究主要针对成年人和任务导向场景，缺乏对儿童用户的支持。儿童的兴趣和需求往往通过注视等非语言行为表达，而非显式请求，因此需要探索如何基于儿童的非语言行为实现主动辅助。

Method: 提出Ollie系统：（1）利用儿童注视估计注意力分布；（2）识别当前视觉焦点；（3）选择相关图画区域；（4）由LLM生成简短叙事描述。采用被试内实验设计，对比注视驱动辅助与随机辅助的效果。

Result: 注视驱动辅助相比随机辅助：（1）更长时间维持儿童在当前视觉焦点上的注意力；（2）更有效地引导儿童关注相关图画区域；（3）儿童、家长和教师普遍认为其更好地匹配了儿童的兴趣。

Conclusion: 验证了将注视作为隐式输入用于儿童主动式AI辅助的可行性，为未来以儿童为中心的AI系统设计提供了启示。

Abstract: Proactive assistance with large language models (LLMs) has received growing attention in the human computer interaction (HCI) community. However, most past work on proactive LLMs' assistance has focused on adult users and task-oriented settings, leaving open how such systems could support children, whose interests and needs are often expressed through gaze and other nonverbal behaviors rather than explicit requests. In this study, we focus on two key challenges of proactive assistance in children's picture exploration: when to provide assistance and what assistance to provide based on children's nonverbal behaviors. To address these challenges, we introduce Ollie, a gaze-informed proactive artificial intelligence (AI) assistant that offers short narrative descriptions based on where a child is looking. Ollie uses children's gaze to estimate their attention, identify their current visual focus, and select a related picture region for the LLM to verbally describe. In a within-subject experiment, we compared gaze-informed assistance with random assistance. Results show that gaze-informed assistance kept children's attention on their current focus for a longer period of time, and guided them more effectively to related picture regions. Children, parents, and a participating kindergarten teacher viewed Ollie positively and consider that it better matched children's interests when compared with the random assistance. This work shows the feasibility of using gaze as an implicit input for proactive AI assistance for children and provides design implications for future child-centered AI systems.

</details>


### [209] [Draped Surfaces: A Contour-Adaptive Interface Overlaid on the Physical Environment for Mixed Reality Workspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00518)
*SoonUk Kwon,Barrett Ens,Pourang Irani*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: CAMEO是一种轮廓自适应混合现实界面，将虚拟窗口贴合到物理表面上，以减少遮挡问题，使用户能更直接地与周围物品交互，同时不影响文本可读性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MR工作空间采用类似驾驶舱的布局，多个浮动窗口围绕用户，导致视觉遮挡，降低用户对物理环境的感知和对现实物体的访问能力。

Method: 提出CAMEO（Contour-Adaptive Mixed Environment Overlays），一种将虚拟窗口贴合到物理表面上的轮廓自适应MR界面，并通过两项控制实验进行评估：第一项测试手部移动路径的绕路程度，第二项测试文本可读性。

Result: 实验一表明，贴合方式相比平面空中界面减少了手部移动的绕路，支持更直接的交互；实验二表明，受控的窗口变形不会显著损害文本可读性。

Conclusion: CAMEO为MR工作空间提供了一种新的设计范式，平衡了沉浸感、可读性和环境理解能力。

Abstract: Conventional Mixed Reality (MR) workspaces are frequently organized in cockpit-like layouts, where multiple floating windows surround the user. While this configuration facilitates access to digital content, it often induces occlusion, reducing understanding of the physical environment and limiting access to real-world objects. To overcome this challenge, we present the Contour-Adaptive Mixed Environment Overlays (CAMEO), a contour-adaptive MR interface that drapes virtual windows onto physical surfaces. This design integrates digital content with nearby items, thereby improving users' visual access to background objects and supporting interaction with them. We evaluate CAMEO in two controlled studies. The first demonstrates that draping reduces hand-movement detours relative to flat mid-air surfaces, enabling more direct interaction with nearby items. The second shows that controlled window deformation does not significantly impair text legibility when compared to flat surfaces. Together, these findings contribute a novel design paradigm for MR workspaces that balances immersion, readability, and environmental understanding.

</details>


### [210] [AI, Trust, and Teaming: The Humans-as-Handlers Approach for Autonomous and Opaque AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00523)
*Nathan G. Wood*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出将高度自主且不透明的AI系统类比为狗等动物，人类应作为"处理者"而非"用户"，以此厘清责任归属，并最终走向人机协作关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在医疗、战争等高影响领域带来伦理与法律挑战，现有的人机关系框架难以明确责任归属，亟需重新定位人类与AI之间的关系。

Method: 通过动物类比（machine-animal analogy），将AI系统类比为与人关系密切的动物，将人类角色从"用户/部署者"重新定义为"处理者"，再剥离不恰当的类比元素，最终论证人机应走向协作关系。

Result: 建立了"人类作为处理者"的框架，明确了人类对自主系统结果负有清晰可追溯的责任，并指出了类比中不契合的部分及剥离路径。

Conclusion: 人机团队的发展方向应是将自主AI系统视为协作伙伴而非工具，共同追求复杂目标并完成复杂任务。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is becoming ubiquitous, and across domains, increasingly autonomous systems are carrying out tasks which raise significant ethical and legal challenges which demonstrate a need for strong human-machine teams rooted in trust. In this article, I argue that within highly impactful areas (such as medicine or warfighting) there are grounds for us initially treating autonomous and opaque systems as relevantly analogous to dogs (or other animals with which we have close relationships). Under this analogy, humans making use of these systems are not to be viewed as "users" or "deployers" of these systems, but instead take the role of "handlers". This recasting of roles shifts the way we view humans, AI-enabled and autonomous systems, and the relations between them, and moreover clarifies the clear and traceable lines of responsibility humans have for the outcomes brought about when using these systems. In developing this point, I clarify that the machine-animal analogy does admit disanalogous elements, but that its touch-points ground it as a starting point. I then explore how we can divest the humans-as-handlers approach of those aspects of our relationships with animals which are unfitting for how we engage with and make use of autonomous and AI-enabled systems. I conclude by arguing that the trajectory of human-machine teamings for autonomous and AI-enabled systems should be a state where we authentically view these not as artifacts which we simply make use of, but as collaborators with which we pursue complex goals and carry out complex tasks.

</details>


### [211] [You Shall Not Pass! Where and Why Developers Draw The Line on AI Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00533)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Marco Gerosa,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对448名微软开发者的混合方法研究显示，开发者对AI自主性的接受度因任务而异，在身份定义、面向人类和设计类工作中接受度最低，且受AI经验、风险承受能力、任务责任和身份等因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI越来越多地承担软件工作，人机边界正在变化，有必要研究开发者对AI自主性的接受程度，以设计维护有意义工作的工具和角色。

Method: 基于认知评价理论、工作设计和自动化研究，对448名微软专业开发者进行混合方法研究。

Result: 大多数开发者接受AI在监督下产出工作，但自主性接受度因任务和个体差异显著；身份定义、面向人类和设计类工作接受度最低；AI经验丰富和风险承受能力高的开发者接受度更高；任务责任降低授权AI行动的意愿，任务身份降低授权AI决策的意愿，而任务需求则增加授权意愿。

Conclusion: 开发者对AI自主性的偏好反映了他们对工作的认知体验，对设计有意义工作具有重要启示。

Abstract: As AI takes on more software work, the line between human and AI effort is shifting. Where developers draw that line around AI autonomy bears on how we design tools and roles that preserve meaningful work. Drawing on cognitive appraisal theory, work design, and automation research, we conducted a mixed-methods study of 448 professional developers at Microsoft to investigate their accepted levels of AI autonomy across software engineering work. Most developers accepted AI producing work under their oversight, although accepted autonomy varied substantively across tasks and individuals. Acceptance was lowest for identity-defining, human-facing, and design-oriented work, and higher among developers with more AI experience and risk tolerance. Task accountability was associated with lower odds of allowing AI to act on developers' behalf, whereas task identity was associated with lower odds of granting AI decision-making autonomy. Task demands had the opposite effect, increasing willingness to delegate decision-making to AI. Our findings suggest that preferences for AI autonomy reflect how developers cognitively experience their work, highlighting important considerations for designing meaningful work.

</details>


### [212] [AI-Centered Grand Challenges in Visual Analytics for Healthcare: Synthesizing the VAHC 2025 Community Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00542)
*Jürgen Bernard,David Gotz,Robert S Laramee,Silvia Miksch,Gabriela Morgenshtern,Renata G. Raidou,Alessio Arleo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于VAHC 2025研讨会，通过主题编码和小组讨论，识别出AI在医疗可视化分析中的五大挑战：信任与偏见、数据与基础设施、可解释性与沟通、人机交互、模型可靠性与验证，并为跨学科研究提供了方向建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗、AI与可视化交叉领域发展迅速但面临跨学科挑战，VAHC研讨会自2010年起连接可视化与医学信息学社区，本文旨在通过第16届研讨会凝聚社区经验，识别该领域未来应优先关注的重要挑战。

Method: 对15篇接收的VAHC论文进行主题编码，结合40多名参与者的结构化小组讨论（围绕三个主题：技术创新vs临床现实、以人为本与可扩展的VAHC、从基础到可操作洞察），以及研讨会后的反思性分析。

Result: AI是所有小组中最一致出现的核心关注点。研究提炼出五大挑战集群：信任与偏见、数据与基础设施、可解释性与沟通、人机交互、模型可靠性与验证。

Conclusion: 本文提出的挑战及研究方向可作为医疗、AI与可视化社区之间讨论与合作的起点，推动跨学科边界的未来研究。

Abstract: The intersection of AI, healthcare, and visualization is evolving rapidly, posing challenges that cut across disciplinary boundaries and resist easy resolution. The Visual Analytics in Healthcare workshop (VAHC), co-located every other year at the IEEE VIS conference and the AMIA (American Medical Informatics Association) annual conference, has served as a forum to connect the visualization and medical informatics community since 2010. In 2025, to celebrate the 16th edition, we used the workshop as an opportunity to consolidate the community's collective experience (and expertise) and identify Grand Challenges where the field should prioritize going forward. We combined thematic coding of the 15 accepted VAHC workshop papers with structured group discussions among more than 40 participants, organized around three major themes: "Technical innovation vs. clinical reality", "Human-centered and scalable VAHC", and "From foundations to actionable insights", followed by post-workshop reflexive analysis. Across all three groups, AI emerged as the most consistently recurring concern. In this paper, we report our AI-centered insights from the VAHC 2025 group activity, contextualize them against the broader literature along five Grand Challenges themes, and distill them into five challenge clusters, each concluded with recommendations for future research directions that cross disciplinary boundaries: (1) trust and bias, (2) data and infrastructure, (3) explainability and communication, (4) human-AI interaction, and (5) model reliability and validation. We share these challenges and their associated research directions as a starting point for discussion and collaboration across the healthcare, AI, and visualization communities. All supplemental materials are available at https://osf.io/p79uj.

</details>


### [213] [Understanding How Humans Inject Knowledge into Machine Learning Workflows through Visual Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00969)
*Yiwen Xing,Philip Beaucamp,Joyraj Chakraborty,Afrah Farea,Yuanzhe Jin,Saiful Khan,Gennady Andrienko,Natalia Andrienko,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该综述调查了过去十年IEEE VIS会议上200余篇VIS4ML论文，通过编码分析从ML特征、可视化、交互和动作四个视角，揭示了人类如何通过交互式可视化将知识注入ML工作流的多种途径，并建立了概念模型和理论分析框架来阐释VIS4ML现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在可视化领域（VIS4ML）中，人类如何通过交互式可视化将知识注入机器学习工作流，系统梳理该领域的研究现状与知识传递路径。

Method: 收集过去十年IEEE VIS会议中200余篇VIS4ML论文，开发了涵盖ML特征、可视化、交互和动作四个维度的编码方案进行文献分析，并构建了将VA视为模型构建的概念模型以及基于信息论的成本-收益分析框架。

Result: 识别出人类通过交互式可视化向ML工作流传递知识的多种不同路径，为VA在ML工作流中的价值提供了明确证据。

Conclusion: 交互式可视化在支持ML工作流中发挥重要作用，视觉分析可优化ML工作流，该工作提供了系统化的分类体系和理论解释。

Abstract: Visual analytics (VA) plays an increasingly important role in supporting machine learning (ML) workflows. In the field of visualization, such approaches and techniques are referred to as VIS4ML. While ML models are mostly learned automatically, the corresponding ML workflows receive a variety of human inputs, such as data labelling, feature engineering, model architecture designing, hyper-parameter tuning, and so on. In this work, we surveyed over 200 VIS4ML papers to gain an understanding of how humans inject their knowledge into ML workflows through interactive visualization. We collected a corpus of VIS4ML papers from the IEEE VIS conferences in the past decade. We developed a coding scheme to facilitate the literature research from four perspectives: characteristics of ML, visualization, interaction, and actions. The analysis of the coded dataset allows us to observe different pathways that transfer human knowledge to ML workflows via interactive visualization. Building on the analysis, we explain the phenomena of VIS4ML using the conceptual model that views VA as model building and the information-theoretic cost-benefit analysis that reasons VA as for optimizing ML workflows. This work provides unequivocal evidence showing the merits of using VA in ML workflows. The full list of surveyed papers, along with all analysis results and figures, is available at https://vis4ml4hd.github.io/ml-knowledge-inject-va/.

</details>


### [214] [Visualizing Engineering Fundamentals: Design of Mixed Reality and Physical Toolkits for Effective Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00979)
*Mohammad Abu Nasir Rakib,Sharmin Akter,Eshwara Prasad Sridhar,Somik Biswas,Md Rassel Raihan,Mahmudur Rahman*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过24名参与者的用户实验，比较了纯课堂教学、课堂结合混合现实应用和课堂结合物理工具包三种教学方式在工程力学中的效果，发现学习者的工作流程和参与度因教学模态而异，并提出设计指南应注重多模态交互和可视化清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学生在工程力学课程中使用混合现实应用和物理工具包的学习体验，为教育工具的设计提供基于人因工程的指导原则。

Method: 开展24名参与者的用户研究，比较三种教学条件：纯课堂教学、课堂加混合现实应用、课堂加物理工具包。采用主题分析法分析参与者反馈。

Result: 学习者的工作流程和对基础力学问题的参与度在不同教学模态间存在差异；学生重视结合可视化与动手操作的多模态交互体验，但复杂的或模糊的可视化会带来挑战。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持以人为中心设计混合现实和物理工具用于工程教育，强调多模态交互与可视化清晰度的重要性。

Abstract: This study examined students' experiences with mixed-reality applications and physical toolkits in Engineering Mechanics to inform design guidelines for educational tools. In a user study with 24 participants, we compared classroom instruction alone, classroom instruction with a mixed-reality application, and classroom instruction with physical toolkits. Thematic analysis of participant feedback revealed that learners' workflows and engagement with fundamental mechanics problems varied across instructional modalities. Participants valued multimodal and interactive experiences that combined visualization with hands-on interaction, while reporting challenges with complex or unclear visualizations. These insights support the human-centered design of mixed-reality and physical tools for engineering education.

</details>


### [215] [SenseWalk: Agent-Based Semantic Trajectory Simulation Powered by Large Language Models in Zoned Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00989)
*Ziyue Lin,Xinhang Xie,Kangyi Wang,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出了SenseWalk，一个利用LLM驱动的智能体来模拟语义轨迹的交互式系统，结合大型语言模型与社会力模型平衡物理合理性与语义连贯性，并提供了用户友好的界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义轨迹分析中高质量数据收集成本高昂且缺乏丰富语义信息，现有模拟工具需要大量技术专业知识，难以被实践者采用。

Method: 设计了结合LLM与社会力模型的模拟工作流以平衡物理合理性与语义连贯性，并开发了用户友好的交互系统支持自定义模拟配置和输出分析。

Result: 通过定量实验评估了模拟工作流的有效性，并通过12名用户的用户研究评估了系统的有用性和效率。

Conclusion: SenseWalk通过LLM驱动的智能体能有效模拟语义轨迹，降低技术门槛，使非专业人士也能进行语义轨迹分析。

Abstract: Semantic trajectory analysis has recently emerged as an approach for modeling human movement by capturing implicit patterns and behaviors through semantic information (e.g., visitors' profiles and goals) beyond raw spatial paths to better understand why people move in certain ways. However, analyzing semantic trajectories in real-world scenarios remains challenging, as collecting high-quality data is costly and often lacks rich semantic information. Meanwhile, existing simulation tools require substantial technical expertise, which makes them difficult for practitioners to adopt. To address these limitations, the paper proposes ${SenseWalk}$, an interactive system that supports simulating semantic trajectories by LLM-powered agents. We develop a simulation workflow that combines LLMs and the social force model to balance physical plausibility and semantic coherence. A user-friendly interface is designed to facilitate users in customizing the simulation configuration and analyzing simulation outputs. We also conduct a quantitative experiment to evaluate the effectiveness of our simulation workflow, and a user study (n=12) to assess the usefulness and efficiency of our system.

</details>


### [216] [Touching and Feeling the Data: A Reusable Software Pipeline for Tactile Statistical Graphs in Accessible Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01214)
*Lawrence Obiuwevwi,Krzysztof J. Rechowicz,Jessica M. Johnson,Erika Frydenlund,Vikas Ashok,Sachin Shetty,Sampath Jayarathna*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个约1500行JavaScript代码的三层可复用管道，用于将统计图表自动转换为盲文触觉图形（STL格式），解决传统CAD手工制作耗时长的问题，生成时间低于250毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D打印触觉图形能让盲人和低视力学生通过触觉感知数据分布和趋势，但每个图形需在CAD软件中手工数小时制作，严重限制了课堂规模的应用。作者将此瓶颈视为软件问题，旨在实现自动化生成。

Method: 设计三层可复用管道：第一层基于触觉感知研究从板材尺寸自动推导触觉设计参数；第二层提供共享图表框架及五个模块化构建器（散点图、柱状图、直方图、折线图、箱线图）；可选第三层使用多模态大语言模型从上传图像提取结构化图表规格，打印前需教师审核。最终输出打印就绪的二进制STL文件。

Result: 管道在约1500行JavaScript中实现，生成打印就绪的STL文件耗时低于250毫秒。

Conclusion: 该自动化管道显著降低了触觉统计图表的制作门槛，有望推动盲文触觉图形在课堂规模上的实际应用，同时需进一步探讨其设计、性能及局限性。

Abstract: Statistical visualization is usually treated as a visual medium, but data can also be touched. Three dimensional printed tactile graphs let blind and low vision students feel distributions, trace trends, and explore relationships through direct haptic interaction. Yet classroom scale use remains limited because producing each graph in CAD software requires specialized skill and hours of manual work. We address this bottleneck as a software problem through a three layer reusable pipeline in about 1500 lines of JavaScript. The first layer derives tactile design parameters automatically from plate dimensions using tactile perception research. The second provides shared chart scaffolding and five modular builders for scatter, bar, histogram, line, and box plots. The optional third layer uses a multi-modal large language model to extract structured chart specifications from uploaded images, with mandatory teacher review before print generation. The pipeline produces print ready binary Standard Tessellation Language files in under 250 milliseconds. We present the design, performance, and limitations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Query-Centric Optimization of AI Workflows via Approximate Query Processing and Proxy Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00254)
*Huayi Wang,Jun Xu,Gromit Yeuk-Yin Chan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM后训练和智能体推理等现代AI工作流表达为声明式查询，并应用经典数据库技术（在线聚合AQP和代理模型过滤PM）来大幅减少昂贵模型调用次数。在TPC-DS和真实LLM流水线上，AQP可在10-50%的调用量下达到<5%误差，PM可减少60%-19x的调用量且准确率损失<10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI工作流（如LLM后训练、智能体推理）中，昂贵的大模型或奖励函数调用成为性能瓶颈，而现有方法通常需要修改底层模型或流水线结构。作者希望在不改变模型和流水线的前提下，利用数据库经典技术来降低计算开销。

Method: 提出两种策略：1) AQP（在线聚合）：将工作流视为在线聚合问题，渐进采样并维护带置信区间的聚合估计，当区间稳定且在用户指定误差范围内时提前终止。2) PM（代理模型过滤）：在少量标注样本上训练轻量级CPU决策树，对高置信度结果进行预过滤，仅将不确定记录交由昂贵模型处理。

Result: 在TPC-DS上，AQP在均衡分布下仅用10-15%的Oracle调用量即可保持<10%聚合误差（减少85-90%），PM减少60-70%调用量。在LLM流水线中，AQP在20-50%调用量下对结构化数学和代码任务准确率损失<5%；PM在结构化任务上奖励模型评分时间减少19倍且准确率损失<10%。

Conclusion: 经典数据库近似查询处理技术可以高效迁移至现代AI工作流中，显著降低昂贵模型调用开销，且不改变底层模型或流水线结构，为AI系统的效率优化提供了跨领域的新思路。

Abstract: Many modern AI workflows, ranging from LLM post-training pipelines to agentic reasoning tasks, can be expressed as declarative queries whose expensive predicate is evaluated by a large model or reward function.
