We propose a query-centric formulation of these workflows and show that classical database techniques, namely approximate query processing (AQP) and proxy-model (PM) based filtering, can substantially reduce the number of expensive model invocations without requiring changes to the underlying models or pipelines. Our first strategy treats the workflow as an online aggregation problem: it progressively samples records, maintains a running aggregate estimate with a confidence interval, and terminates early once the interval stabilizes, accepting the estimate when it falls within a user-specified error bound. Our second strategy trains a lightweight, CPU-resident decision tree on a small set of oracle-labeled examples and uses it to pre-filter records whose outcome can be predicted with high confidence, routing only uncertain records to the expensive model. We evaluate both strategies on TPC-DS aggregate queries and on real LLM post-training pipelines including math reasoning, general instruction following, and code generation. On TPC-DS, Strategy AQP keeps aggregate error under 10% while reaching its adaptive stopping point at 10-15% of oracle calls under balanced distributions, an 85-90% reduction, and Strategy PM reduces oracle calls by 60-70%. On LLM pipelines, Strategy AQP reaches its adaptive stopping point at 20-50% of oracle calls with less than 5% accuracy loss on the structured math and code tasks; open-ended instruction following, scored by a reward model, shows a larger but bounded reduction. Strategy PM reduces reward-model scoring time by up to 19x on structured tasks with less than 10% accuracy loss.

</details>


### [218] [When Classic Cache Policies Fail: Learning-Augmented Replacement for Semantic Retrieval Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00394)
*Yushi Sun,Bowen Cao,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文形式化研究了LLM代理中的在线语义缓存替换问题，发现经典缓存策略（LRU、LFU）在语义工作负载下甚至不如简单的FIFO，并提出了学习增强框架SOLAR，在理论上证明其具有常数竞争比（≤3）和次线性遗憾界，实验显示相比FIFO有5-75%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越依赖检索缓冲区来存储和复用过去的经验，但管理这些缓冲区的缓存策略大多是特设的，缺乏系统性的理论分析和有效的解决方案。

Method: 首先形式化描述问题为带切换成本的在线语义缓存替换问题；然后在MemoryBench-Full数据集上对8种替换策略进行实验评测；提出SOLAR框架，通过遗憾积累确定修改时机（约17%修改率），通过贝叶斯在线学习从隐式检索反馈中选择内容。

Result: 经典启发式（LRU、LFU）在语义工作上持续逊于朴素FIFO基线；SOLAR在紧缓存大小下相对FIFO有5-75%的改进；合成实验发现池大小与检索质量呈倒U型关系。

Conclusion: SOLAR在理论上具有常数竞争比（≤3，不依赖于缓存大小和时间范围）和O(√(KT log T))的驱逐遗憾，匹配下界；容量约束应被理解为检索噪声现象而非存储限制。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on retrieval buffers to store and reuse past experience, yet the cache management policies governing these buffers remain largely ad-hoc. We formalize this as an online semantic cache replacement problem with switching costs, where items are matched by embedding similarity and hit quality is continuous rather than binary. Through experiments on two datasets from MemoryBench-Full (LoCoMo, DialSim) with 8 replacement policies, we reveal a surprising finding: classic heuristics (LRU, LFU) \emph{consistently underperform} the naive FIFO baseline on semantic workloads, due to the absence of temporal locality and frequency concentration. We propose SOLAR, a learning-augmented framework that derives modification timing from regret accumulation (achieving $\sim$17\% modification rate) and content selection from Bayesian online learning over implicit retrieval feedback. We prove SOLAR achieves a constant competitive ratio $\leq 3$, independent of cache size and horizon (vs.\ $Ω(K)$ for FIFO), and eviction regret $O(\sqrt{KT\log T})$, matching the $Ω(\sqrt{KT})$ lower bound up to logarithmic factors. Experiments demonstrate 5--75\% relative improvement over FIFO at tight cache sizes, with a clearly characterized phase transition at the working set boundary. Synthetic experiments with 5000-item pools further reveal an inverted-U relationship between pool size and retrieval quality, justifying capacity constraints as a retrieval noise phenomenon rather than a storage limitation.

</details>


### [219] [TVA: A Version-aware Temporal Graph Storage System for Real-time Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00406)
*Wenhao Li,Zhanhao Zhao,Jinhao Dong,Jiamin Hou,Wei Lu,Yunhai Wang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: TVA是一个新型时序图存储系统，通过多版本存储架构、时序表和增强型hopscotch哈希等优化，实现高效时序查询，相比现有系统延迟降低9.9倍，存储开销降低2.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图存储系统要么缺乏对时序数据的原生支持，要么在查询时序图时延迟较高，无法高效处理时序图分析任务。

Method: 1) 提出多版本存储架构，将版本元数据与实际属性值分离存储；2) 设计时序表和增强型hopscotch哈希来紧凑组织相邻顶点和边的版本元数据；3) 提出版本跳过策略，重用先前扫描的时序信息以避免冗余元数据查找。

Result: 相比最先进的时序图存储系统，TVA在时序查询延迟上降低高达9.9倍，存储开销降低2.2倍。

Conclusion: TVA通过架构创新和数据结构优化，有效解决了时序图存储和查询的效率问题，为时序图分析提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Analyzing temporal graphs can reveal valuable insights that are typically hidden in static graphs. Unfortunately, existing graph storage systems either lack native temporal support or suffer from high latency when querying temporal graphs. This paper presents TVA, a new temporal graph storage system designed for efficient temporal query processing. First, TVA introduces a specialized multi-version storage architecture that separates version metadata from actual data, i.e., the property values associated with different versions of vertices and edges. This architecture enables efficient version retrieval for a vertex or edge by quickly locating valid version metadata and directly dereferencing it to access the corresponding property values. Second, we design tailored data structures, namely the temporal table and enhanced hopscotch-based hashing, to compactly organize the version metadata of adjacent vertices and edges, thus reducing random I/O for metadata lookups during the neighborhood scan initiated from a vertex. Finally, to further accelerate neighborhood scans over multiple vertices, we propose a version-kipping strategy that reuses temporal information obtained from prior scans, thereby avoiding redundant metadata lookups across scans. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that TVA achieves up to 9.9x lower temporal query latency and 2.2x lower storage overhead compared to state-of-the-art temporal graph storage systems.

</details>


### [220] [Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with Graph Range Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00727)
*Qian Tao,Yuntao Jiang,Yongxin Tong,Yu Sun*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出DLH和DLH-M，一种基于距离感知标签索引与哈希压缩的方法，用于解决图范围过滤下的近似最近邻搜索问题，在大幅提升吞吐量的同时保持高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有过滤近似最近邻搜索方法仅支持数值范围约束或分类存在性检查，无法处理更复杂的图范围过滤场景，限制了实际应用的适用性。

Method: 提出DLH（距离感知标签索引+哈希压缩），通过创建距离感知标签集实现高效集合交集操作，并使用布隆过滤器压缩大标签集；进一步优化得到DLH-M，对查询节点进行中间哈希索引的记忆化缓存。

Result: 在多种数据集上，DLH和DLH-M吞吐量提升最高达70.3%，召回率保持在98.5%以上，且额外存储开销有限。

Conclusion: 所提方案在实际场景中具有可行性和有效性，能够在复杂图范围过滤条件下实现高效的近似最近邻搜索。

Abstract: Vector databases have become a fundamental component for high-dimensional vector retrieval in artificial intelligence applications. Recent research has focused on filtered approximate nearest neighbor search (filtered ANN), which involves retrieving the nearest vectors that satisfy a given attribute-based filter. However, existing filters are generally limited to numerical range constraints or categorical existence checks, which restricts their applicability in more complex, real-world scenarios. In this paper, we investigate filtered ANN using graph range filters, where the retrieved vectors must be within a specified distance from the query node in a predefined filter graph. To address this problem, we propose DLH, a Distance-aware Labeling index with Hashing compression. DLH creates distance-aware labeling sets to enable efficient graph range filters via the simplified set intersection operations. Large labeling sets are further compressed into Bloom filters to improve query efficiency in DLH. Furthermore, recognizing that the query node is always involved in in-range queries of the graph range filters, we enhance DLH by memoizing the intermediate hashing index for the query node, yielding an optimized version called DLH-M. Experimental evaluations on diverse datasets demonstrate that DLH and DLH-M improve throughput by up to 70.3%, and could maintain recall rates over 98.5% with limited extra storage, validating the practical availability of the proposed solution.

</details>


### [221] [When to Repair a Graph ANN Index: Navigability-Signal-Triggered Local Repair Protects Tail Recall Under Bursty Churn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00728)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出基于可导航性退化信号的触发式边修复策略，替代固定周期修复，在同等预算下显著提升ANN图索引在增删操作下的尾召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中ANN图索引（如HNSW、DiskANN/Vamana）在频繁插入/删除操作后因删除节点孤立了贪心搜索路径，导致召回率下降。现有系统采用固定周期的图修复（每X次操作合并一次），但这种方式是盲目的，无法高效利用有限修复预算。作者希望探索是否基于可导航性退化信号触发局部边修复，能比固定节奏修复更好地分配预算。

Method: 在SIFT-128和Fashion-MNIST-784两个真实ANN数据集上，设置突发性增删流（churn stream），比较信号触发修复与固定节奏修复策略。在匹配的摊销修复预算（相同合并次数）下，使用探测召回率（probe-recall）作为可导航性退化信号。采用四个随机种子，统计最差情况（尾）召回率和平均召回率，并计算95%置信区间。

Result: 信号触发修复在预算稀缺时帕累托主导固定节奏修复：最小召回率@10提升+0.014（SIFT）至+0.050（Fashion-MNIST），95%置信区间排除零。平均召回率提升很小（<0.005）。优势随漂移严重程度梯度变化——图越稀疏、越脆弱，提升越大；当索引鲁棒或预算充裕时，差距消失。探测召回率信号与真实召回率高度相关（Spearman rho≈0.95）。

Conclusion: 基于可导航性退化信号的触发式修复在有限预算下比固定节奏修复更有效，尤其能改善最差情况下的尾召回率。但本文不声称平均召回率提升或提出新索引结构。未来工作包括建立召回率-修复成本边界以及处理数据分布漂移的耦合问题。

Abstract: Graph approximate-nearest-neighbor (ANN) indexes (HNSW, DiskANN/Vamana) lose recall under insert/delete churn, because deletions orphan the greedy-search paths that route through removed nodes. Production systems restore navigability by repairing the graph on a fixed schedule (consolidate every X operations). We ask whether triggering local edge repair on a measured navigability-degradation signal, rather than a blind clock, spends a fixed repair budget better. On two real ANN datasets (SIFT-128 and Fashion-MNIST-784) under a controlled bursty churn stream, and comparing repair policies at matched amortized repair budget (equal consolidation count), signal-triggered repair Pareto-dominates fixed-cadence repair. The gain is concentrated on worst-case (tail) recall at scarce budget: at roughly one consolidation it improves the minimum recall@10 by +0.014 (SIFT) to +0.050 (Fashion-MNIST) across four stream seeds, with 95% confidence intervals excluding zero, while the mean-recall gain is small (<0.005). The advantage follows a clean drift-severity gradient -- larger for sparser, more fragile graphs -- and fades to parity when the index is robust or budget is ample. A cheap probe-recall signal is a valid, leading indicator of true recall (Spearman rho ~= 0.95). We contribute the mechanism, a budget-matched evaluation protocol that separates repair scheduling from repair spend, and an open, reproducible churn-repair harness. We deliberately do not claim a mean-recall improvement or a new index; a recall-versus-repair-cost bound and data-distribution-drift coupling are left as future work.

</details>


### [222] [SessionBound: Turning Enterprise Task Approval into Budgeted Database Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00751)
*Minmin Wu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SessionBound是一种将企业AI代理的批准任务转化为受限数据库会话的系统，通过控制平面签发任务令牌，数据库运行时绑定令牌并强制执行安全视图、行范围、字段限制、操作限制、查询预算、披露预算和审计日志，不依赖LLM判断查询安全性。PostgreSQL原型在24场景验证集上通过测试，微基准测试显示p50延迟约1.4-1.5ms（原始PostgreSQL约0.052-0.074ms），相对开销高但绝对延迟低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI代理在执行内部审计、合规审查等任务时，管理者批准的业务任务会被代理转化为底层SQL查询，现有系统无法将批准的任务直接转化为有边界的数据库执行上下文，存在授权失控风险。

Method: 提出SessionBound框架：控制平面定义任务模板、接受申请、记录审批、分配预算、签发令牌；数据库运行时SessionBoundDB将令牌绑定到会话，强制执行安全视图、行范围、禁用字段、操作限制、查询预算、披露预算和收据审计，不依赖LLM判断查询安全性。

Result: PostgreSQL原型通过24场景验证集；微基准测试p50延迟约1.4-1.5ms vs 原始PostgreSQL约0.052-0.074ms（小规模合成查询），相对开销高但绝对延迟低。

Conclusion: SessionBound有效填补了企业AI代理数据库授权控制的空白，通过将批准任务转化为有预算、可审计的受限数据库会话，实现了安全的代理SQL执行，虽引入一定性能开销但在可接受范围内。

Abstract: Enterprise AI agents are useful for internal analysis, audit, compliance review, and operational investigation, but they create a difficult authorization problem. A manager or data owner may approve a business task, while the agent later generates open-ended SQL below the application layer. Existing systems help identify agents, delegate authority, govern data products, or enforce database policy, but they do not directly turn an approved enterprise task into a bounded database execution context. SessionBound fills this gap. It turns approved enterprise tasks into short-lived, budgeted, and auditable database sessions for AI agents. A control plane defines task templates, accepts task applications, records approvals, assigns budgets, and issues signed task tokens. A database runtime, SessionBoundDB, binds a token to a session and enforces safe views, row scope, denied fields, operation limits, query budgets, disclosure budgets, and receipts. The database does not rely on an LLM to decide whether a query is safe. The agent may generate SQL freely, but each attempt must stay inside the approved boundary. A PostgreSQL prototype passed a 24-scenario validation suite. Microbenchmarks show p50 SessionBound execution around 1.4--1.5 ms versus raw PostgreSQL p50 around 0.052--0.074 ms on small synthetic queries: high relative overhead, but low absolute latency.

</details>


### [223] [RACORN-1: Adaptive Recall-Preserving Speedup for Low-Selectivity Filtered Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00768)
*Yoonseok Kim,Gyusik Choe*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: RACORN-1是一种对ACORN-1的就地扩展，通过自适应搜索回退（ASF）和自适应精确回退（AEF）机制，解决了ACORN-1在低选择度（<5%）下的连接不稳定性和召回率崩溃问题，在极端低选择度（≤0.1%）下实现召回率1.00，速度提升20-75倍，并在负相关性评估中保持0.80-0.98的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACORN-1在低选择度（<5%）下存在连接不稳定性，在<1%选择度时发生召回率崩溃，需要一种更鲁棒的过滤向量搜索方法。

Method: (i) 自适应搜索回退（ASF）：将未通过过滤的节点重新用作临时桥梁，绕过断裂路径，使用步长采样选择桥梁和两跳候选节点以保证空间多样性。(ii) 自适应精确回退（AEF）：针对极端低选择度场景，结合线性扫描与图搜索，形成RACORN-1+变体。

Result: 在1M和40M规模数据集上：选择度1%-0.3%时延迟降低约9-26倍；召回率从ACORN-1的0.45-0.72（1%）和0.03-0.10（0.3%）恢复至0.70-0.96和0.77-0.98；极端低选择度（≤0.1%）下召回率1.00，速度提升20-75倍；负相关性场景下召回率0.80-0.98，延迟优势5-9倍。

Conclusion: RACORN-1和RACORN-1+构成了对ACORN-1兼容的机制，对极端低选择度和对抗性查询-过滤相关性均具有鲁棒性，有效改善了过滤优先方法的结构性召回率-延迟权衡曲线。

Abstract: Filtered Vector Search (FVS), which combines vector embedding similarity with structured metadata predicates, has emerged as a core requirement in RAG and production retrieval systems. ACORN-1, the representative In-filtering algorithm that reuses an existing HNSW index, substantially reduces latency at low selectivity but suffers connectivity instability below 5% selectivity and recall collapse below 1%. We propose RACORN-1, an in-place extension of ACORN-1 that resolves this collapse via (i) Adaptive Search Fallback (ASF) -- repurposing filter-failing nodes as transient bridges to detour around severed paths; bridge and two-hop candidate selection uses stride sampling for spatial diversity. While filter-first ACORN-family methods have a structural recall trade-off relative to distance-first HNSW, RACORN-1 improves the trade-off curve via ASF, minimizing recall loss while substantially reducing latency. Across three 1M-scale and one 40M-scale dataset, RACORN-1 delivers approximately 9-26x latency reduction over HNSW in the sweet spot (1%-0.3%), and recovers ACORN-1's recall collapse from 0.45-0.72 (1%) and 0.03-0.10 (0.3%) to 0.70-0.96 and 0.77-0.98 respectively. For the extreme-low-selectivity regime where linear scan can outperform graph search, we combine RACORN-1 with (ii) Adaptive Exact Fallback (AEF) in a variant RACORN-1+, achieving recall 1.00 with 20-75x speedup at 1M <=0.1% and 13x speedup at 40M 0.01%. Under a Negative Correlation evaluation (K-means clusters), where ACORN-1 collapses (recall 0.08-0.41), RACORN-1 maintains recall 0.80-0.98 with a 5-9x latency advantage over HNSW. Together, RACORN-1 and RACORN-1+ form an ACORN-1-compatible mechanism robust to both extreme-low-selectivity and adversarial query-filter correlation.

</details>


### [224] [Exploring the Semantic Gap in Agentic Data Systems: A Formative Study of Operationalization Failures in Analytical Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00828)
*Jalal Mahmud,Eser Kandogan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: LLM生成的代理分析工作流中存在语义鸿沟，导致操作化失败。通过对236个分析意图的跨领域研究，识别出153个反复出现的失败案例，归纳为五类失败模式：比较基准、过程推理、量化推理、角色混淆和政策基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在工作流生成和执行方面取得进展，但操作化分析概念所需的语义信息往往超出了数据库模式和数据值所能明确表达的范畴，现有系统存在语义鸿沟。

Method: 跨领域形成性研究，涵盖金融、人力资源和公共安全三个领域，分析236个分析意图中代理生成的分析工作流，识别操作化失败案例并进行分类归纳。

Result: 在236个分析意图中识别出153个反复出现的操作化失败案例，归纳为五类失败模式：比较基准（comparative grounding）、过程推理（process reasoning）、量化推理（quantitative reasoning）、角色混淆（role confusion）和政策基准（policy grounding）。

Conclusion: 用户层面的分析概念与工作流生成系统可用信息之间存在语义鸿沟，未来代理数据系统可能需要更丰富的语义表示来弥合分析意图与可执行计算之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate queries, invoke tools, and construct analytical workflows. Although recent advances have substantially improved workflow generation and execution, the semantic information required to operationalize analytical concepts often lies beyond what is explicitly represented in database schemas and data values. We present a cross-domain formative study of operationalization failures in agent-generated analytical workflows. Across 236 analytical intents spanning finance, human resources, and public safety domains, we identify 153 recurring failures despite successful workflow generation and execution. Our analysis reveals five recurring classes of failures: comparative grounding, process reasoning, quantitative reasoning, role confusion, and policy grounding. These findings suggest a semantic gap between user-level analytical concepts and the information available to workflow-generation systems. More broadly, they raise questions about the admissibility of analytical operations and suggest that future agentic data systems may require richer semantic representations to bridge the gap between analytical intent and executable computation.

</details>


### [225] [Generative Retrieval for Table Union Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00833)
*Shulun Zhang,Linting Wang,Yuwei Xu,Yingli Zhou,Chenhao Ma*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GenTUS将表联合搜索(Table Union Search)重新形式化为基于离散语义表标识符的约束生成问题，通过直接生成可联合表的标识符而非传统的编码-搜索-精炼流水线，在7个公开基准上取得了平均排名1.05（最强基线为2.57）的最佳效果，同时显著降低了在线延迟、检索存储和增量更新成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的表联合搜索方法采用编码-搜索-精炼流水线，检索质量依赖候选池召回率，且随着数据湖规模扩大，延迟和存储成本不断增加。

Method: 提出生成式检索框架GenTUS：为候选表分配紧凑的联合性感知标识符，训练生成器直接从查询表产生可联合表的标识符，查询时通过约束解码保证生成的标识符对应数据湖中的有效表并作为排序结果返回。

Result: 在7个公开TUS基准上，GenTUS平均排名1.05（最强基线2.57），同时大幅降低在线延迟、检索存储和增量更新成本。

Conclusion: 生成式检索范式可有效替代传统编码-搜索-精炼流水线，在保证检索质量的同时显著提升效率与可扩展性。

Abstract: Modern data lakes contain heterogeneous tables whose task-relevant information is often scattered across different schemas, sources, and naming conventions. Table union search (TUS) retrieves tables that can be reliably unioned with a query table, supporting data discovery, enrichment, and downstream analytics. Although learning-based TUS methods improve table- or column-level representations, they still follow an encode-search-refine pipeline: candidate retrieval is followed by query-candidate matching or reranking, making quality dependent on candidate-pool recall and incurring growing latency and storage costs as the data lake scales. We propose GenTUS, a generative retrieval framework that reformulates TUS as constrained generation over discrete semantic table identifiers. Instead of searching and reranking an explicit candidate pool, GenTUS assigns candidate tables compact unionability-aware identifiers and trains a generator to produce the identifiers of unionable tables directly from the query. At query time, constrained decoding ensures that generated identifiers correspond to valid data-lake tables and returns them as ranked retrieval results. Experiments on seven public TUS benchmarks show that GenTUS achieves the best overall retrieval quality, with an average rank of 1.05 compared to 2.57 for the strongest baseline, while substantially reducing online latency, retrieval-artifact storage, and incremental update cost.

</details>


### [226] [From Single to Multiple Attributes: Experimental Insights on Sampling-Based Distinct Combination Estimation in GROUP-BY Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00868)
*Yujie Zhang,Xiaochun Yang,Bin Wang,Yuan Sui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文针对多属性GROUP-BY查询中唯一组合数估计这一未被充分研究的挑战，通过设计专门的工作负载生成器，在四个真实数据集和TPC-H基准上对现有方法进行实证评估，分析了估计误差来源及其对查询计划选择的影响，并为未来估计器设计提供了关键建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基数估计技术主要关注SPJ查询而忽略GROUP-BY操作，且不同值估计研究多聚焦于单属性设置；尽管基于采样的方法（包括近期基于学习模型的方法）理论上可支持多属性估计，但其实际效果尚不明确，缺乏全面的实证评估。

Method: 设计专门的工作负载生成器生成带过滤和不带过滤的多属性GROUP-BY查询，在四个真实数据集和TPC-H多表基准上对现有方法进行系统评估，分析GROUP-BY基数估计误差的来源及其对PostgreSQL查询计划选择的影响。

Result: 揭示了采样方法在多属性估计中的有效性边界，指出了现有方法在利用单属性信息方面的不足和优化空间，并量化了估计误差对PostgreSQL计划选择的具体影响。

Conclusion: 为未来多属性GROUP-BY基数估计器的设计提供了关键建议和指导方向。

Abstract: Estimating the number of distinct combinations in multi-attribute GROUP-BY queries remains a significant yet underexplored challenge. Current cardinality estimation techniques primarily focus on SPJ queries (i.e., selections, projections, and joins) and neglect GROUP-BY operations; meanwhile, distinct value estimation research has mainly targeted the single-attribute setting. Although sampling-based methods, including recent approaches with learned models, can theoretically support multi-attribute estimation, their practical effectiveness remains unclear. A comprehensive empirical evaluation is thus lacking to address whether joint distribution information from samples alone is sufficient for accurate multi-attribute estimation, whether existing methods fully exploit single-attribute information and can be further optimized, and whether filtered GROUP-BY queries can be accurately estimated. To this end, we propose a specialized workload generator for multi-attribute GROUP-BY queries and generate both filtered and non-filtered queries over four real-world datasets. By evaluating existing methods across synthetic workloads and the multi-table TPC-H benchmark, we analyze the sources of GROUP-BY cardinality estimation errors and their impact on PostgreSQL's plan selection, offering key recommendations for future estimator design.

</details>


### [227] [The Decode-Work Law: Margin-Governed, Provably-Exact Spatial Joins over Compressed Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01182)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于渐进式解码的Douglas-Peucker LOD梯度的空间连接认证机制，通过对多边形进行双面Hausdorff余量测试，在保证精确连接结果的前提下大幅减少解码顶点数。实验表明，相比naive解压再精炼方法，中位数解码顶点减少5.9倍；相比Brinkhoff等人的多步基线方法减少约4.9倍。同时提出了"解码工作量定律"：解码工作由每个候选对的符号净空余量（距谓词翻转边界的距离）决定，与对象大小无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统filter-and-refine空间连接方法对通过过滤器认证的候选对从未考虑几何解压成本。当几何以压缩、渐进可解码的多分辨率编码格式存储时，连接的真实成本是解码的字节数。现有方法缺乏对解码成本的建模，导致无法在高压缩环境下精确控制性能开销。

Method: 采用Douglas-Peucker LOD阶梯进行渐进式解码，通过双面Hausdorff余量测试进行认证；候选对根据符号净空余量（与谓词翻转边界的距离）动态决定所需解码层级，直到LOD分辨率超过该余量为止；实现预算诚实的解码记账机制；使用真实美国人口普查TIGER水域多边形数据评估。

Result: 在31个工作负载上实现零正确性违规（与全精度预言机集合相等）；解码顶点数比naive解压再精炼减少3.4-16.8倍（中位数5.9倍），比Brinkhoff等人基线减少约4.9倍；解码工作量定律在受控几何上R²=0.87且与大小无关，在真实数据上R²≈0.55。

Conclusion: 认证渐进连接机制能显著减少解码成本，解码工作量定律揭示了净空余量是决定解码工作的关键因素。但文中也明确指出该方法的局限性：近边界顶点预测器模型被拒绝、选择性预测未能实现、最坏情况下存在Omega(v)的平凡读取界限。

Abstract: Filter-and-refine spatial joins have always avoided touching exact geometry for certified candidate pairs, but the field never modeled the decompression cost of the pairs that survive the filter. When geometry is stored in a compressed, progressively-decodable multiresolution codec, the join's true cost is bytes decoded. We study provably-exact polygon intersection joins over a Douglas-Peucker level-of-detail (LOD) ladder, certified by a two-sided Hausdorff-margin test, and make two contributions. First, a reproducible mechanism and harness: on real U.S. Census TIGER water polygons, our progressive certificate join returns the exact join result while decoding 3.4-16.8x (median 5.9x) fewer vertices than naive decompress-then-refine, and about 4.9x fewer than the single-approximation multi-step baseline of Brinkhoff et al. (1994), with zero correctness violations (set-equality against a full-precision oracle) across 31 workloads. Second, a characterization we call the decode-work law: decode work is governed by each pair's signed-clearance margin -- how close it is to the predicate-flip boundary -- independent of object size, because the certificate descends the ladder only until its resolution beats the margin. The law is clean on controlled geometry (held-out R2=0.87, size-independent) and directional on real data (R2 ~= 0.55). We are explicit about what does not hold: a near-boundary-vertex predictor is the wrong model (we pre-registered one and rejected it), a selectivity regime forecaster did not materialize, and the worst case is the trivial Omega(v) read bound on adversarially interleaved boundaries. We contribute the mechanism, budget-honest decode accounting, and an open harness; we do not claim a new index.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [228] [Scalable Security and Migration-Aware SFC Provisioning in LEO Satellite Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.00471)
*Mohammed Mahyoub,Wael Jaafar,Sami Muhaidat,Halim Yanikomeroglu*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文针对LEO卫星星座中多租户切片共享安全VNF带来的跨切片侧信道风险，提出了一种安全感知与迁移感知的SFC部署优化框架。该框架基于ISO/NIST原则构建共置风险模型，将问题建模为多切片MILP，并设计了ADMM启发的逐切片最佳响应分解方法（S-ADMM和P-ADMM），以解决联合求解的可扩展性难题。仿真表明，该方法在消除共置风险、减少SFC迁移次数、保证延迟合规性的同时，能在单周期预算内求解原本不可行的安全感知MILP问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座作为6G骨干网，独立租户切片共享星载基础设施，但星上计算与网络资源稀缺，无法为每个切片分配专用VNF，导致不同切片共享同一卫星上的VNF实例，扩大了攻击面并引入跨切片侧信道风险。同时，卫星轨道运动导致可见性和拓扑随时间变化，迫使VNF迁移成为结构性需求。现有研究未将资源效率、服务连续性和安全隔离耦合为统一问题，亟需提出一种安全与迁移感知的SFC部署方案。

Method: 1) 基于ISO/NIST原则构建共置风险模型，给出解析界；2) 将问题建模为多切片混合整数线性规划（MILP），区分可避免迁移与轨道运动导致的强制迁移；3) 提出ADMM启发的罚函数逐切片最佳响应分解方法：S-ADMM（串行）和P-ADMM（并行带冲突修复），将跨切片耦合转化为线性逐切片罚项，分解为独立子问题。

Result: 在Walker-Delta星座仿真中，所提框架消除了共置风险，减少了SFC迁移次数，并维持了完整的延迟合规性。在切片数量较大、安全感知MILP不可解的场景下，所提方法仍能在单周期预算内找到可行解。

Conclusion: 本文通过安全与迁移感知的联合优化框架，有效解决了LEO卫星网络中多切片共享VNF的安全隔离问题，提出的ADMM分解方法在可扩展性和求解可行性上显著优于原始MILP方案。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are emerging as a backbone for global 6G connectivity, where independent tenant slices share orbital infrastructure, each requiring an ordered chain of security virtual network functions (VNFs). Because onboard computation and networking are scarce, slices cannot be given dedicated VNFs. They must share instances on the same satellites, enlarging the attack surface and exposing tenants to cross-slice side-channel risk. This exposure shifts continually as visibility, orbital motion, and the inter-satellite topology change in time (epochs), making VNF migration a structural necessity that couples resource efficiency, service continuity, and security isolation into a single problem. We formulate this security- and migration-aware security function chain (SFC) placement as a multi-slice mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) whose core is a co-location risk model, grounded in ISO/NIST principles and supported by analytic bounds, in which we separate avoidable migrations from those forced by orbital motion. Because the joint program scales quadratically with the cross-slice co-location terms, we develop an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM)-inspired penalized per-slice best response decomposition that recasts the coupling as a linear per-slice penalty, yielding independent subproblems through sequential (S-ADMM) and parallel, collision-repaired (P-ADMM) schedules. Simulations over a Walker-Delta satellite constellation show that the proposed framework eliminates co-location risk, reduces SFC migrations, and sustains full delay compliance, while remaining feasible within the per-epoch budget for slice counts where the monolithic security-aware MILP is intractable.

</details>
